  window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
  window.__agyGtmLoaded = window.__agyGtmLoaded || false;
  window.__agyLastPageViewPath = window.__agyLastPageViewPath || '';

  function __agyDetectViewport() {
    const width = Math.round(
      window.innerWidth ||
        (document.documentElement && document.documentElement.clientWidth) ||
        1440
    );
    const height = Math.round(
      window.innerHeight ||
        (document.documentElement && document.documentElement.clientHeight) ||
        900
    );
    const orientation = width >= height ? 'landscape' : 'portrait';
    const minDim = Math.min(width, height);
    const maxDim = Math.max(width, height);
    const aspectRatio = minDim > 0 ? maxDim / minDim : 1;
    const ua =
      typeof navigator !== 'undefined' ? navigator.userAgent || '' : '';

    const hasSegments =
      Boolean(
        window.visualViewport &&
          window.visualViewport.segments &&
          window.visualViewport.segments.length > 1
      ) ||
      Boolean(
        typeof navigator !== 'undefined' &&
          navigator.devicePosture &&
          navigator.devicePosture.type === 'folded'
      ) ||
      Boolean(
        window.matchMedia &&
          window.matchMedia(
            '(horizontal-viewport-segments: 2), (vertical-viewport-segments: 2)'
          ).matches
      );
    const isFoldableUA =
      /Pixel Fold|SM-F9|SM-F7|Galaxy Z Fold|Surface Duo/i.test(ua);
    const isFoldableUnfolded =
      minDim >= 600 && maxDim <= 1150 && aspectRatio <= 1.3;
    const isFoldableCover =
      (width >= 320 &&
        width <= 368 &&
        height >= 700 &&
        height / width >= 2.0) ||
      (height >= 320 &&
        height <= 368 &&
        width >= 700 &&
        width / height >= 2.0);

    const isIpad =
      /ipad/i.test(ua) ||
      (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' &&
        navigator.maxTouchPoints > 0 &&
        /macintosh/i.test(ua));
    let os = 'unknown';
    if (/iphone|ipod/i.test(ua)) os = 'iphone';
    else if (isIpad) os = 'ipad';
    else if (/googlebook|cros/i.test(ua)) os = 'googlebook';
    else if (/android/i.test(ua)) os = 'android';
    else if (/windows/i.test(ua)) os = 'windows';
    else if (/macintosh|mac os x/i.test(ua)) os = 'mac';
    else if (/linux/i.test(ua)) os = 'linux';

    let viewportCategory = 'desktop';
    if (
      hasSegments ||
      isFoldableUA ||
      isFoldableUnfolded ||
      isFoldableCover
    ) {
      viewportCategory = 'foldable';
    } else if (width < 600) {
      viewportCategory = 'mobile';
    } else if (width <= 1024 || isIpad) {
      viewportCategory = 'tablet';
    }

    return {
      viewportCategory: viewportCategory,
      formFactor: viewportCategory,
      viewportWidth: width,
      viewportHeight: height,
      orientation: orientation,
      operatingSystem: os,
    };
  }

  function __agyRecordPageView() {
    const currentPath = window.location.pathname || '/';
    if (window.__agyLastPageViewPath === currentPath) return;

    if (
      window.agyAnalytics &&
      typeof window.agyAnalytics.trackPageView === 'function'
    ) {
      window.agyAnalytics.trackPageView();
      return;
    }

    window.__agyLastPageViewPath = currentPath;
    const vp = __agyDetectViewport();
    const eventParams = {
      pagePath: currentPath,
      viewportCategory: vp.viewportCategory,
      formFactor: vp.formFactor,
      viewportWidth: vp.viewportWidth,
      viewportHeight: vp.viewportHeight,
      orientation: vp.orientation,
      operatingSystem: vp.operatingSystem,
    };
    if (document.title) {
      eventParams.pageTitle = document.title;
    }
    if (document.referrer) {
      eventParams.referrer = document.referrer;
    }
    try {
      const sp = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search || '');
      const utmSource = sp.get('utm_source');
      const utmMedium = sp.get('utm_medium');
      const utmCampaign = sp.get('utm_campaign');
      const utmTerm = sp.get('utm_term');
      const utmContent = sp.get('utm_content');
      if (utmSource) {
        eventParams.utm_source = utmSource;
        eventParams.utmSource = utmSource;
      }
      if (utmMedium) {
        eventParams.utm_medium = utmMedium;
        eventParams.utmMedium = utmMedium;
      }
      if (utmCampaign) {
        eventParams.utm_campaign = utmCampaign;
        eventParams.utmCampaign = utmCampaign;
      }
      if (utmTerm) {
        eventParams.utm_term = utmTerm;
        eventParams.utmTerm = utmTerm;
      }
      if (utmContent) {
        eventParams.utm_content = utmContent;
        eventParams.utmContent = utmContent;
      }
    } catch (_err) {
      // Ignore URLSearchParams errors
    }

    const entry = {
      event: 'page_view',
      pagePath: currentPath,
      viewportCategory: vp.viewportCategory,
      formFactor: vp.formFactor,
      viewportWidth: vp.viewportWidth,
      viewportHeight: vp.viewportHeight,
      orientation: vp.orientation,
      operatingSystem: vp.operatingSystem,
      eventParams: eventParams,
    };
    if (eventParams.pageTitle) entry.pageTitle = eventParams.pageTitle;
    if (eventParams.referrer) entry.referrer = eventParams.referrer;
    if (eventParams.utm_source) {
      entry.utm_source = eventParams.utm_source;
      entry.utmSource = eventParams.utmSource;
    }
    if (eventParams.utm_medium) {
      entry.utm_medium = eventParams.utm_medium;
      entry.utmMedium = eventParams.utmMedium;
    }
    if (eventParams.utm_campaign) {
      entry.utm_campaign = eventParams.utm_campaign;
      entry.utmCampaign = eventParams.utmCampaign;
    }
    if (eventParams.utm_term) {
      entry.utm_term = eventParams.utm_term;
      entry.utmTerm = eventParams.utmTerm;
    }
    if (eventParams.utm_content) {
      entry.utm_content = eventParams.utm_content;
      entry.utmContent = eventParams.utmContent;
    }

    window.dataLayer.push(entry);
    window.dataLayer.push({ eventParams: undefined });
  }

  __agyRecordPageView();

  window.glueCookieNotificationBarLoaded = function () {
    if (window.__agyGtmLoaded) return;
    window.__agyGtmLoaded = true;

</script><script type="module" src="/_astro/AnalyticsBootstrap.astro_astro_type_script_index_0_lang.DQy9iQ8q.js"></script><script defer src="https://www.gstatic.com/glue/cookienotificationbar/cookienotificationbar.min.js" data-glue-cookie-notification-bar-category="2A" data-glue-cookie-notification-bar-language="" data-glue-cookie-notification-bar-site-id=""></script>\<script\>
	window.StarlightThemeProvider = (() => {
		const storedTheme =
			typeof localStorage !== 'undefined' && localStorage.getItem('starlight-theme');
		const theme =
			storedTheme ||
			(window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: light)').matches ? 'light' : 'dark');
		document.documentElement.dataset.theme = theme === 'light' ? 'light' : 'dark';
		return {
			updatePickers(theme = storedTheme || 'auto') {
				document.querySelectorAll('starlight-theme-select').forEach((picker) => {
					const select = picker.querySelector('select');
					if (select) select.value = theme;
					/** @type {HTMLTemplateElement | null} */
					const tmpl = document.querySelector(`#theme-icons`);
					const newIcon = tmpl && tmpl.content.querySelector('.' + theme);
					if (newIcon) {
						const oldIcon = picker.querySelector('svg.label-icon');
						if (oldIcon) {
							oldIcon.replaceChildren(...newIcon.cloneNode(true).childNodes);
						}
					}
				});
			},
		};
	})();

	(() => {
		const openBtn = document.querySelector('button[data-open-modal]');
		const shortcut = openBtn?.querySelector('kbd');
		if (!openBtn || !(shortcut instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
		const platformKey = shortcut.querySelector('kbd');
		if (platformKey && /(Mac|iPhone|iPod|iPad)/i.test(navigator.platform)) {
			platformKey.textContent = '⌘';
			openBtn.setAttribute('aria-keyshortcuts', 'Meta+K');
		}
		shortcut.style.display = '';
	})();

				const state = JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem('sl-sidebar-state') || '0');

<p>Track new features, improvements, and bug fixes across Google Antigravity
surfaces, including Antigravity 2.0, Antigravity CLI, the Antigravity SDK, and
Antigravity IDE.</p>
<div class="rn-wrapper not-content astro-oabwef3t" data-changelog-root><div class="rn-tab-bar astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-tabs astro-oabwef3t" role="tablist" aria-label="Surfaces"><button type="button" role="tab" class="rn-tab-btn active astro-oabwef3t" data-tab-target="hub" data-surface-key="2.0" aria-selected="true" aria-controls="panel-hub" id="tab-hub">Antigravity 2.0</button><button type="button" role="tab" class="rn-tab-btn astro-oabwef3t" data-tab-target="cli" data-surface-key="cli" aria-selected="false" aria-controls="panel-cli" id="tab-cli">Antigravity CLI</button><button type="button" role="tab" class="rn-tab-btn astro-oabwef3t" data-tab-target="extensions" data-surface-key="ide" aria-selected="false" aria-controls="panel-extensions" id="tab-extensions">IDE Extensions</button><button type="button" role="tab" class="rn-tab-btn astro-oabwef3t" data-tab-target="sdk" data-surface-key="sdk" aria-selected="false" aria-controls="panel-sdk" id="tab-sdk">Antigravity SDK</button><button type="button" role="tab" class="rn-tab-btn astro-oabwef3t" data-tab-target="ide" data-surface-key="antigravity-ide" aria-selected="false" aria-controls="panel-ide" id="tab-ide">Antigravity IDE</button></div></div><div class="rn-subbar astro-oabwef3t"><p class="rn-rollout-text astro-oabwef3t"><svg class="rn-info-icon astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="14" height="14" fill="currentColor" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 2C6.48 2 2 6.48 2 12s4.48 10 10 10 10-4.48 10-10S17.52 2
             12 2zm1 15h-2v-6h2v6zm0-8h-2V7h2v2z" class="astro-oabwef3t"></path></svg><span class="astro-oabwef3t">New versions roll out gradually and may take a few days to reach all users.</span></p><button type="button" class="rn-toggle-btn astro-oabwef3t" data-toggle-all aria-expanded="false" title="Toggle all sections"><svg class="toggle-icon astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="13" height="13" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="7 13 12 18 17 13" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline><polyline points="7 6 12 11 17 6" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg><span class="toggle-text astro-oabwef3t">Expand all</span></button></div><div class="rn-panels-container astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-panel active astro-oabwef3t" id="panel-hub" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="tab-hub" data-panel-id="hub" style="display: block;"><h2 class="rn-panel-heading astro-oabwef3t">Antigravity 2.0</h2><div class="rn-timeline astro-oabwef3t"><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-hub-2.22.0"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/releases?tab=hub&version=2.22.0" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 2.22.0">v2.22.0</a></h3><span class="rn-latest-tag astro-oabwef3t">Latest</span></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">October 8, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Sidebar drag-and-drop, UI Extensions, and localized conversation timestamps</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Now you can drag-and-drop conversations to organize threads across the sidebar and conversation timestamps now use localized month names. This release also supports UI extensions that Antigravity shows in the side pane next to a conversation.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.22.0" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(3)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Construct and display custom user interfaces inside the side panel as part of installed Antigravity plugins.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">You can now drag and drop conversations between sections in the sidebar to organize your projects.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Conversation timestamps in message headers and turn tooltips now display with month names rather than numeric dates.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-hub-2.21.1"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/releases?tab=hub&version=2.21.1" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 2.21.1">v2.21.1</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">October 7, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Advanced Search, Automations, and grouped agent actions</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Use ⌘+K (or CTRL+K on Windows) to search conversations based on their contents. The Scheduled Tasks tab is now called Automations, where you can run on-demand or scheduled automations or set up with the agent's help. Antigravity now also groups the agent's file edits, terminal commands, and file reads into one collapsible row.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.21.1" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(9)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Scheduled Tasks are now called Automations. You can run a scheduled automation right away with Run Now, or select Create with Prompt to have the agent walk you through setting up a new one.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">File edits, terminal commands, and file reads between agent replies now group into a single collapsible row with a short summary of what the agent did.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added the Gruvbox Material theme preset for light and dark modes, with matching syntax highlighting colors.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">The time under each agent reply is now always shown as a relative time, such as 5m ago. Hover over it to see the full date and time.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Grouped agent actions no longer show as still running after a command moves to the background, and they now collapse as soon as the agent starts its reply.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Auto-generated conversation titles now take images attached to your first message into account, and messages sent with only attachments can now get a title.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">On Windows and Linux, selecting the Antigravity icon in the top-left corner of the window now starts a new conversation.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Archived conversations in the sidebar now keep their status and last-updated time, and the sidebar heading shows (Archived) while you're viewing archived conversations.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">On macOS, if privacy settings block Antigravity from reading a file or folder you drop into a conversation, the message box now explains how to allow access in System Settings > Privacy & Security > Files and Folders. The agent also no longer reports a blocked file as missing.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.21.1" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(14)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where the agent reading an incomplete or truncated MP4 or MOV video could make every later message in the conversation fail.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where your settings stopped working after the settings file was saved in Windows Notepad or with PowerShell, which add an invisible character to the start of the file.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where, after you changed the model in one conversation, switching to another conversation could show the wrong model.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where opening a PDF, Word, Excel, or PowerPoint artifact showed the file's raw contents as unreadable text. These files now show a message with a download link instead.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where the file tree sidebar didn't appear in the File Viewer, including when viewing files from conversations that aren't part of a project.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where, on Windows, removing or updating a Build With Google plugin such as Dart and Flutter could fail because the plugin was still running.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where attachments you sent from the home screen came back in the message box the next time you started a new conversation.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where returning focus to a Markdown preview, artifact, or review diff in the side pane scrolled it back to the top.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where reverting a conversation could make the message toolbar briefly disappear, or leave an outdated message on screen after you sent new messages.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where holding down a keyboard shortcut such as Cmd+B or Ctrl+B repeatedly toggled the sidebar or side pane open and closed.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where sending review comments without any message text while the agent was working showed "Empty message" in the queued messages card instead of the number of comments.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where pressing Cmd+L or Ctrl+L sometimes didn't move the cursor to the message box after you selected text in the conversation.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where the agent's temporary working files opened as artifacts instead of in the File Viewer.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where Rust files and code blocks marked rs appeared without syntax highlighting.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-hub-2.19.1"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/releases?tab=hub&version=2.19.1" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 2.19.1">v2.19.1</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 30, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Message subagents directly, export Markdown as PDF, and conversation-only undo</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>You can now send messages straight to a subagent from the message box, export rendered Markdown as a PDF, and revert only the conversation when you undo. You can also reopen the window from the tray icon on Windows and Linux. This release includes 5 improvements and 5 fixes.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.19.1" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(5)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">You can now send a message directly to a subagent from the message box, without going through the main agent.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Rendered Markdown in artifacts and in files opened in the side pane can now be exported as a PDF from the overflow menu, including tables, code blocks, and diagrams.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">The Confirm Undo dialog now offers an option to revert only the conversation.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">On Windows and Linux, a left-click on the tray icon now reopens or focuses the Antigravity window.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Pressing Cmd+A or Ctrl+A now selects only the content pane or terminal you are focused on instead of the whole page.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.19.1" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(5)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where a terminal command step you had expanded collapsed again when the command finished.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where custom agents ignored your global and project rules.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where selecting the image pill on a file step opened the image in the side pane and also expanded the step to show a second copy.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where double-clicking or triple-clicking text in the file viewer and then dragging did not extend the selection by word or line.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where the title bar Forward button could stay disabled, and where the Back and Forward tooltips on Windows and Linux showed shortcuts that did nothing.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-hub-2.18.1"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/releases?tab=hub&version=2.18.1" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 2.18.1">v2.18.1</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 28, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Manage & Install plugins in AGY</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>This release introduces a Customizations tab and marketplace for discovering and installing plugins and includes overall improvements to the sidebar and chat experience.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.18.1" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(14)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">You can now discover, install and manage plugins within the Customizations tab, with curated shelves for development tools and workspace integrations.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">You can now switch the sidebar to show only archived conversations from the Display Options menu and filter by archived conversations on the Conversation History page, with archive folder icons marking archived projects.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Terminal commands in permission requests are now syntax highlighted, making it easier to read what the agent wants to run before approving it.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Terminal command steps in the conversation now stay collapsed by default while running and after completing, expanding automatically only when waiting for your approval.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Slash commands provided by plugins now show their plugin name in the / menu, can be filtered by typing the plugin name, and display a hover card with the plugin name and full description.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Images generated by the agent now stay visible at the bottom of the turn alongside the response text instead of collapsing into the completed steps section.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">The token usage breakdown now shows rules and other customizations as separate bars against their own budgets, and flags any rules or customizations that were left out or included by path only after going over budget.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">You can now delete any project directly from the overflow menu in its sidebar header.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Press Option+Command+P on macOS or Alt+Shift+P on Windows and Linux to pin or unpin the active conversation.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">The startup loading screen now shows a pulsing Antigravity logo in place of the loading dots and text.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Arrow-key navigation in the command palette now wraps around between the first and last rows.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">When the agent asks you a question and an answer choice is truncated, hovering over it now shows the full text.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Starting a conversation in a new workspace renders the workspace in the sidebar immediately.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Grouping conversations by workspace in the sidebar now groups by folder path, labels each group with its folder name, and hides empty headers.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.18.1" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(15)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where Google Sign-In could fail with a browser security error when signing in from the app or the built-in browser.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where scrolling up in long conversations could stall after collapsed tool steps, unloading earlier turns could jump the scroll position, and page bounds could collapse to zero.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where copying text from dark mode and pasting it into rich-text editors such as Google Docs or Gmail carried over dark background colors and light text.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where closing the app during enterprise sign-in before choosing a license or project left an incomplete sign-in saved the next time you opened it.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where cancelling a new conversation while it was still being created showed a Conversation unavailable notification a few seconds later.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where pressing Ctrl+Left or Ctrl+Right on Windows and Linux jumped across the entire prompt when the message box started with an @ mention or slash command chip.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where pressing Up or Down inside a multiline prompt navigated prompt history instead of moving the cursor between lines unless already at the start or end.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where pasting a message that contained a slash command sent only the command name to the agent instead of its full instructions.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where keyboard shortcuts such as Ctrl+/ and Ctrl+M were not routed to the focused pane in split-pane layouts.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where choosing a sort order in Display Options did not apply if conversations had previously been reordered by dragging.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where open files were duplicated in @ mention search results and folder paths with special characters were displayed with percent encoding.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where files in .git, .env, and .vscode directories could be modified without a permission prompt under Default and Request Review permission presets.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where Git worktrees created by subagents were saved in conversation directories and misclassified as artifacts.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where background file viewer updates and menus could steal focus while the application window was in the background.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where copying text containing entity pills dropped the pill labels from the copied text.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-hub-2.17.0"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/releases?tab=hub&version=2.17.0" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 2.17.0">v2.17.0</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 22, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Plan before you build</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Type <code>/plan</code> and the agent drafts a plan you can read and edit before it writes any code. The Agent setting that governs this is now called Plan Review Policy, and you can set it to review every plan, review only when the agent judges it worthwhile, or skip review entirely.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.17.0" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(11)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Reveal in Finder for projects.</strong> Projects in the sidebar have a new Reveal in Finder entry (Show in File Explorer on Windows) in their overflow menu, opening the project folder in your system file manager. Multi-folder projects open one window per folder.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Toggle theme from the command palette.</strong> A new "Toggle Between Light/Dark Themes" command flips the theme without a trip to Settings. On System, the first toggle pins the opposite of whatever your OS resolved to.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Custom agents can declare their own hooks.</strong> A Markdown agent can list hook files in its front matter with a <code>hooks:</code> entry. Paths may be relative to the agent, relative to the project, or absolute.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Save Video.</strong> Right-click a video anywhere it appears — in the chat, the file viewer, a browser recording, or the full-screen preview — and choose Save Video.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Rules no longer crowd out everything else.</strong> Rules get their own 20,000-token budget, so a large rules file stops pushing your skills, subagents and MCP tools out of the agent's context. A rule over budget is listed by file path and description for the agent to open when relevant, instead of being cut short.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Per-project configuration moved.</strong> Repository customization settings now load from <code>\<project\>/.gemini/config.json</code>. The older <code>.agents/settings.json</code> is no longer read — move any <code>personal_customization_dir</code> entry into the new file.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Paste properly into the chat box.</strong> Right-clicking the chat input and choosing Paste handles images and rich text, keeping links as markdown, instead of only plain text. Pasting over a selected attachment no longer drops the pasted text.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Slash commands and plugins read like products.</strong> The pill inserted when you pick a slash command shows the skill's proper name and logo instead of its raw command name, and the built-in Google plugins show readable names — Chrome DevTools, Google Maps Platform, Gemini API, Android CLI, Data Agent Kit, Modern Web Guidance, Google Antigravity SDK.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Fewer link cards you did not ask for.</strong> The agent surfaces a link card in the conversation only when there is something to open or act on, such as a review it created, a document it edited, or a local server it started. Links it merely consulted are bookmarked to the sidebar quietly.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Sidebar polish.</strong> Hovering a conversation highlights the whole row rather than just the text, and a sidebar that closed itself because you narrowed the window reopens when you widen it again. A sidebar you closed yourself stays closed.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><code>.cppm</code> files are syntax-highlighted as C++ and get the code file icon.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.17.0" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(10)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Rewinding a conversation no longer restores out-of-date files.</strong> The snapshot search used to sweep backward from the point you rewound to, so it could hand back a state from earlier in the conversation. It now takes the first snapshot at or after that point and never an earlier one, and replays the steps when no such snapshot exists.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Large files and diffs no longer freeze the app.</strong> Syntax highlighting and line diffing run off the main thread, and files over 100 KB fall back to plain text.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Custom rules, workflows and agents load on large projects.</strong> They no longer report a load failure when scanning your project folders takes more than three seconds; the timeout is now fifteen seconds, matching what skills already had.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong><code>/btw</code> keeps your agent.</strong> A side question runs with the same agent and settings as the conversation you asked it in, instead of falling back to the default agent.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Saving media works for more formats.</strong> Saving an image or video from the media menu now works for SVG files and for videos whose type carries codec details; those saves used to fail silently. Files saved from inline data get the right extension.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Maths renders in more cases.</strong> Display maths written with the <code>$$</code> fence attached to the formula on the same line now renders, and no longer swallows the markdown that follows it. Row spacing like <code>\\[12pt]</code> inside a cases block no longer turns into a stray dollar sign, inline maths followed immediately by a digit stays maths, and comma-separated numeric spans no longer swallow the prose after them.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Cut and Copy in the message box are available only when the selected text is inside the message box, and act on the message box itself.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Right-clicking an image caption or an enlarged image no longer pops open the thumbnail's own menu. Images, video and audio whose address carries a query string display inline instead of falling back to a plain link.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">The service announcement banner no longer sits on top of the window controls or pushes the breadcrumbs down, and the main title bar stays visible whenever a single conversation pane is open.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Renaming a conversation no longer has the pin, archive and overflow buttons covering the end of a long title.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-hub-2.16.0"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/releases?tab=hub&version=2.16.0" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 2.16.0">v2.16.0</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 22, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Windows Subsystem for Linux, Office attachments, and live subagent cards</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Connect to a Windows Subsystem for Linux distribution from Application Settings, and drop Word, Excel, and PowerPoint files straight into a prompt. Subagents now appear as live cards you can follow and stop while they work. This release also adds right-click copy and save for images.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.16.0" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(12)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added a Windows Subsystem for Linux (WSL) section to Application Settings on Windows, so you can connect to an installed WSL distribution or switch back to your local Windows environment. When connected, the title bar shows the active distribution name.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">You can now attach Microsoft Office documents (Word, Excel, and PowerPoint) to a prompt by dragging and dropping them into the message box, and Gemini models read them natively instead of falling back to opening the stored file.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Subagent invocations now appear as dedicated cards in the conversation stream with live running, waiting, and completed status, a hover control to stop a running subagent, and one-click navigation to its conversation in the side pane.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Prompt actions in interactive widgets and HTML artifacts now fill in and focus the message box instead of sending automatically, so you can review or edit the prompt before submitting it.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Right-click an image in an attachment, a preview modal, or an earlier message to Copy Image or Save Image.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">SVG images can now be copied to the clipboard and pasted into other applications.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">The loading indicator now shows what the agent is doing while it prepares a response, including tool initialization progress and a live countdown with attempt count when a model request is retried.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">The timestamp in the agent's message footer now shows when the response finished rather than when it started, matching the duration shown on long-running turns.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Application keyboard shortcuts now take priority over web previews, so shortcuts keep working while a preview has focus.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">The Confirm Undo dialog now keeps the file list at a fixed size and scrolls it in place, so Confirm and Cancel stay visible when reverting many files.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Inline pills and icons inside markdown headings now scale with the heading's size and weight instead of rendering small.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Discovering skills and other customizations is now dramatically faster on large projects and network-mounted folders, cutting a startup scan that previously took over a minute to under a second.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.16.0" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(15)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where browser sign-in could fail permanently if the app disconnected, reloaded, or the computer went to sleep while you were completing sign-in in your browser.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where expired or rejected enterprise sign-in credentials stayed cached, leaving the app in a state you could not sign back in from.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where web search returned no summary when the search summarizer used a reasoning model.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where reopening an older conversation could re-apply that conversation's earlier file edits to your project and overwrite newer work.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where a custom icon set on a skill did not appear in the slash command menu, context pills, or step renderers.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where wide markdown tables could bleed outside a pinned message or show through behind it while scrolling.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where enlarging an image attached to a restored draft showed a broken image.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where the Narrow conversation width setting silently fell back to the default width.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where saving images and other media from a conversation could fail.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where a "No Models Available" error could flash briefly right after sign-in while models were still loading.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where screen readers did not announce the name, selected value, or options of dropdown menus in Settings.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where icons and logos across menus, toolbars, and the sidebar could render blank or lose their color gradients.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where the conversation history truncation notice appeared at the bottom of the chat instead of at the point where earlier messages were cleared.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where typing @file: did not show project file suggestions until you typed another character.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where choosing Paste from the message box's right-click menu did not paste.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-hub-2.15.1"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/releases?tab=hub&version=2.15.1" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 2.15.1">v2.15.1</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 19, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Windows sandbox support</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>This release introduces file and network sandboxing on Windows.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.15.1" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(1)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">File and network sandboxing on Windows are now supported.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-hub-2.15.0"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/releases?tab=hub&version=2.15.0" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 2.15.0">v2.15.0</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 18, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Custom agent controls and keyboard navigation</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Custom agents can now switch off the default prompts and tools, and the agent you pick sticks with you after a reload. This release also adds Page Up, Page Down, Home, End, and Escape for moving around a conversation, speeds up switching conversations in the sidebar, and fixes a batch of settings and conversation state issues.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.15.0" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(7)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Custom agents can now switch off the default prompt sections and default tools and add back only the tools they need.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Page Up, Page Down, Home, and End now scroll through a conversation, and Escape steps out of the prompt box.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">The custom agent you pick in the message box is remembered after a reload, and resets to the main agent if that agent is deleted or turned off.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Dragging a file over the chat now highlights the message box itself, so it is clearer where the file will land.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">The project picker above the message box now reads "No Project" when you are working outside of a project, instead of "New Conversation".</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved performance when switching between conversations in the sidebar.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Bold text in chat now renders at a slightly lighter weight so it sits better with the text around it.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.15.0" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(16)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where your saved settings and project list could be overwritten when the app failed to read its state file.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where permission settings accumulated duplicate entries, which made conversation files grow very large and slowed the app down.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where starting a new conversation could show a false crash recovery notice.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where a conversation started without a folder pulled in files and settings from unrelated projects you had open.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">A command that is killed when the app restarts is now reported as canceled instead of appearing to finish successfully.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">The agent now reports a clear error when it tries to read a binary file it cannot display, instead of returning unreadable content.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">A malformed tool call from the model now appears as a short "Invalid tool call" note rather than a failure.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where an error that arrived without any tool activity in the turn was dropped instead of shown in the conversation.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where a system message could appear twice, or out of order, in the chat transcript.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where a turn ending in an error or a scheduled task showed an empty expandable section.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Chat messages now fade out smoothly at the bottom of the conversation instead of being cut off sharply.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">The close-window shortcut now closes the dialog on top, such as settings, instead of the conversation behind it, and Escape inside an open menu now closes just the menu rather than the whole dialog.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where the projects list in settings could not be collapsed again after you expanded it.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where the side pane's tabs ran underneath the controls in the top-left corner of the window when the pane was maximized and the sidebar was collapsed.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where selected rows in the file tree and file path menus rendered with a heavy blue fill.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where sending feedback with large diagnostic logs attached failed, and removed an empty gap in the feedback form.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-hub-2.14.0"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/releases?tab=hub&version=2.14.0" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 2.14.0">v2.14.0</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 15, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">New Permissions System & Terminal and Git version control for Enterprise</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Enterprise and Business accounts can now use the integrated terminal and Git version control in the sidebar. This release also cuts memory use in long conversations and fixes a range of stability and accessibility issues.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.14.0" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(10)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Enterprise and Business accounts can now use the integrated terminal and Git version control in the sidebar.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Conversations with very long histories now load faster and use less memory.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Removed a redundant scan of your skills, rules, and other customization files that ran on every message, so messages are acknowledged faster.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Settings, artifacts, and terminals opened in their own tab now load faster and no longer flash an empty container first.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">The agent's temporary scratch files are no longer watched, synced, or shown in the Artifacts panel and Review pane, so they no longer add noise to either.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Reorganized the conversation menu so Archive and Delete sit together at the bottom behind their own divider, and added Share and Move to Group to the right-click menu.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">The command palette now opens over a dimmed backdrop with enter and exit animations, matching the other dialogs in the app.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Renamed the global permissions section on the General settings screen to "Global Permissions" and the inherit option in project settings to "Inherit Global", so the names match the screen they point at.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Pressing the new conversation shortcut on the home screen now toggles between your last-selected project and a standalone conversation instead of staying in standalone mode.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Updated the Vesper dark theme with lilac and pink accents for keywords and operators, and dimmer comments.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.14.0" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(18)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where opening Settings, an artifact, or a terminal in its own tab could show a blank screen.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a crash that could occur when the app failed to read your rule files.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a memory leak where finished terminal commands held on to their full output for the rest of the session.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where the app could freeze for several seconds after reconnecting following a period of inactivity, which was most noticeable with a large number of conversations.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where subagents that had already finished could keep showing as running, which also left follow-up messages queued until restart.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where a single transient service error could end a session. Transient errors are now retried with backoff for about twelve minutes.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed two issues in the @-mention menu: categories now narrow as you type instead of waiting for search results to return, and pressing Tab no longer overwrites text you already typed.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where a markdown link pointing at a heading in the same document opened the file viewer at the project root instead of scrolling to that heading.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where an escaped dollar sign inside a block math expression prevented the formula from rendering.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where URL artifacts pointing at a Google Drive folder offered "Open Preview" as their primary action, even though Drive folders cannot be displayed in the preview pane.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where the quota reset time in the model selector was cut off mid-sentence. It now shows a compact duration, with the full text in the tooltip.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the minimized side question button in the composer toolbar so it shows how many side questions are minimized instead of reading like a mode selector.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed duplicate titles and cramped spacing on the license selection screen.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where signing in with a personal account after using a business account failed with a project access error.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where git hooks or global git configuration on your machine could stop the app from saving its internal checkpoints.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed screen reader support for queued messages: queueing a message is now announced along with the queue length and the available actions, and the Send now, Edit, and Delete buttons are labelled.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed screen reader announcements for side questions, which could be repeated or dropped when browsing question history or when an answer arrived in the background.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where the agent produced Mermaid diagram types the app cannot render, such as Gantt charts and timelines.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-hub-2.13.0"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/releases?tab=hub&version=2.13.0" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 2.13.0">v2.13.0</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 9, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Introducing new Documents section and several UX enhancements for organization and control</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>External files such as Google Drive links, PDFs, and Office documents now have their own Documents section in the sidebar. This release also adds a virtualized viewer for large code and data artifacts, an option to hide whitespace-only changes in diffs, and clearer warnings when a configuration file fails to parse.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.13.0" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(16)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">External files such as Google Drive links, PDFs, and Office documents now appear in their own Documents section in the sidebar above Artifacts.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added a "Hide Whitespace Changes" option to the Review Changes overflow menu and file diff viewers to filter out whitespace-only edits.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Code and data artifacts like SQL and JSONL files now open in a virtualized viewer with syntax highlighting and line numbers, keeping the app responsive when viewing large files and supporting inline comments.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Commands executed in the sandbox now display a shield badge in the command header that links to sandbox documentation.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Settings displays clear warnings with file paths and a copy button when configuration or project files fail to parse.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Closing a side question now minimizes it to a button in the composer toolbar instead of clearing it. Clicking the button restores the panel so you can review previous side questions without retyping /btw, and a new delete button lets you remove questions you no longer need.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added a Cancel button and keyboard shortcuts (Ctrl+C on macOS, Ctrl+D on Linux and Windows) to interactive question prompts, letting you stop the agent and return to the chat input without submitting blank answers.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Quote or add selected text to chat using Cmd+L or Cmd+I on macOS, or Ctrl+L and Ctrl+I on Windows and Linux. The text selection popup also adjusts button widths to fit shortcut labels.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Agent scratch files now appear in a dedicated, collapsible Scratch Files section in the side panel rather than mixing into the primary Artifacts list. Turn cards also hide scratch files unless explicitly marked as user-facing.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Unified sidebar section headers and spacing so row heights, collapse controls, and section gaps are consistent between pinned items and projects.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Pin, unpin, and rename conversations directly from the command palette when a conversation is open.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added an option to archive the active conversation directly from the command palette, and updated archiving to navigate back to the home screen.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Attached review comments now clear immediately from the input box and diff panes when you send a message, and restore if the message fails to send.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Toast notifications stay on screen for 8 seconds instead of 5 seconds by default, giving you more time to read multi-line messages and error details.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Adjusted spacing in the model quota panel and increased its maximum height to eliminate unnecessary scrollbars when viewing quota across multiple models.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Lowered the volume of the task completion sound to match the action-required notification chime.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.13.0" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(16)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue that could cause the conversation view to crash when rendering messages with repetitive unclosed HTML tags.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where the model selector could crash if available models failed to load or returned empty groups.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where screen readers dropped rapid status updates or missed repeated notifications like clipboard confirmations. Very long announcements are now shortened at a sentence boundary with a spoken notice.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where denying a tool permission request (for example an MCP tool call) removed the step from the conversation. Denied steps now stay visible with a Rejected label.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where the agent still prompted for permission to read or write artifact files from other projects when file access outside the current project was already allowed.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Removed the usage panel from the model selector for Enterprise and Business accounts, which do not have per-model quotas.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed cramped vertical spacing on cards above the chat input, such as queued messages and input notifications.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Turned off browser and operating system autocorrect, spellcheck, and capitalization in folder path and quick picker inputs so path segments are not changed while typing.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where hover cards stayed open after switching windows, moving the cursor outside the window, or dragging items.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where chat autoscroll failed to resume when scrolling near the bottom of a response.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where quoting chat text containing file links or mentions inserted unwanted line breaks into the quoted text.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where the scroll-to-bottom button remained visible when viewing trailing whitespace past the latest message, and resolved tooltip flickering during streaming responses.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Custom sidebar widths in the auxiliary pane are now remembered instead of resetting when switching conversations.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where clicking the copy button on a failed chat turn did nothing instead of copying the error details to the clipboard.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where .ico files displayed as raw binary text in the diff viewer instead of rendering as visual image previews.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed layout alignment and spacing in the auxiliary pane so the top-right controls line up with the file header icons below.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-hub-2.12.2"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.12.2" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-hub-2.12.0"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/releases?tab=hub&version=2.12.0" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 2.12.0">v2.12.0</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 2, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Quoting, /boost, and improved Settings</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>You can now highlight part of a response and quote it as context for a follow-up prompt, and paid users get the new <code>/boost</code> command for a deeper multi-agent reasoning pass. This release also brings UX improvements across settings, the chat panel, and sidebar navigation.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.12.0" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(7)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">You can now highlight portions of Antigravity responses to quote as context for follow-up prompts.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Introduced the <code>/boost</code> slash command to enhance thinking effort by using a multi-agent reasoning pipeline.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">General Settings now shows which of your projects override a setting, with a link to jump straight to that project's settings, and opening Settings opens the active project's settings when overrides are present.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Terminal split layouts are now preserved across window reloads.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">The side question panel now has back and forward controls for stepping through the questions you have asked in a session.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">You can now send a message with the send button while dictation is still running, instead of having to stop recording first.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Dragging to select a region of an image for a comment now updates the selection immediately upon release.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.12.0" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(9)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added drag-and-drop and improved support for attaching audio files to conversations, and fixed an issue where WebM files were treated as audio.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue that could cause authentication errors part way through a turn on slower network connections.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Permission prompts again describe the specific action being requested instead of showing a generic title.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where subagents running side by side disappeared from the conversation when one of them reported progress.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where an extension or automation could briefly show an unavailable error after being restarted.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">A failing custom hook no longer ends the session; it is now reported as an error and the conversation continues.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the connected tool server configuration dialog overflowing its window.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the built-in workflow migration helper reporting wrong paths and moving the wrong files on Windows.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed keyboard focus rings on sidebar items being clipped or rendering with square corners.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-hub-2.11.0"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/releases?tab=hub&version=2.11.0" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 2.11.0">v2.11.0</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">August 26, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Generative UI and UI improvements</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Generative UI renders HTML artifacts inline in chat, with support for KaTeX math and Chart.js and Plotly charts. This release also adds side-by-side terminal splits, the Darcula theme, and <code>@path/to/file</code> references inside AGENTS.md and custom rule files.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.11.0" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(10)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added support for referencing and inlining external files directly using <code>@path/to/file</code> syntax within <code>AGENTS.md</code> and custom rule files.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added support for rendering YAML frontmatter as a formatted metadata card in markdown files and artifacts.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added support for KaTeX math formatting, Chart.js, and Plotly charts in generative UI widgets and artifact previews.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added the ability to split terminals side-by-side in the terminal panel using a dedicated split button or keyboard shortcut (<code>Ctrl+Shift+5</code> on Linux/Windows, <code>Cmd+\</code> on Mac), complete with draggable divider resizing and tree indicators in the terminal list.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added the Darcula theme preset and syntax highlighting color palette.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added support for viewing specific page ranges (StartPage and EndPage) in multi-page documents (like PDFs) and specifying image resolution (MediaResolution) in the <code>view_file</code> tool.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added support for hover preview card on skills chip in chat.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added support for defining a <code>rules:</code> key in custom markdown agent frontmatter to bind specific rule files directly to an agent.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added support for discovering custom skills, agents, and rules defined in <code>skills.json</code>, <code>agents.json</code>, and <code>rules.json</code> configuration files located within project subdirectories.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Several overall UX improvements for performance, accessibility, and project configurations.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.11.0" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(30)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"
