astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where slash commands unavailable to a selected custom agent were incorrectly shown in the command menu.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where image attachments in the message composer were lost when switching between conversations.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where Ogg audio and video files were assigned generic MIME types that could cause model errors.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where custom color themes with transparency or alpha channels caused UI text to render in red.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an icon mismatch where the title bar overflow menu displayed a generic terminal icon instead of matching the tab icon in the side panel.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a memory leak where uncommitted conversation renders could leave agent state updates running in the background.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a memory leak where rapidly switching between conversations could leave background streaming connections open indefinitely.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where clicking a comment link would not scroll to the comment if it was located below the visible fold in virtualized file and diff viewers.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where project folder names with special characters or spaces were improperly decoded twice.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where newly added files in the review pane displayed an inaccurate added line count due to trailing newlines.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where users authenticated using Workforce Identity Federation (WIF / Enterprise sign-in) were logged out prematurely after one hour due to token refresh endpoint routing.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a z-index stacking conflict where inline file change panels overlapped the @-mention dropdown menu.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where the side panel overflow menu incorrectly marked the last-clicked action with a checkmark.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where form controls and scrollbars inside generative UI widgets did not adapt to the dark or light theme.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where Enterprise users authenticating using Application Default Credentials could erroneously see an "Out of credits" error message.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed cold-start token endpoint routing to prevent HTTP 429 resource exhaustion errors on launch, and cached account authentication status to eliminate redundant backend checks on user interactions.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where long terminal commands in permission prompts caused the popup layout to overflow.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where rapid consecutive agent status updates caused repeated notification chime sounds.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where expanded tool steps and collapsible sections could collapse or remount when earlier steps updated during long conversations.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where disabled menu items displayed an active pointer cursor instead of a disabled cursor.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where previously configured command permission allow and deny rules were ignored after migrating to the updated permissions system.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Disabled the Git Push action in repositories without a configured remote, and added clear tooltips explaining why VCS actions are disabled.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where pasting text into the integrated terminal using Ctrl+V failed unless clipboard read permissions were explicitly granted.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where artifacts created in nested subdirectories could fail to appear in the artifacts panel.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where signed-in users clicking "Manage" in Settings briefly saw an unauthenticated account overlay.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where selecting text in an assistant response failed to show the "Quote" button if the mouse drag released below the message bubble.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed authentication race conditions that could cause error loops when signing in again or leave stale account state after signing out.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where a single corrupted or empty project file could cause the entire projects sidebar to appear empty.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue in the file viewer where selecting a single code token or word failed to display the comment button.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where temporary network disruptions or DNS lookup timeouts during startup caused permanent account failure screens instead of automatically retrying.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-hub-2.10.0"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/releases?tab=hub&version=2.10.0" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 2.10.0">v2.10.0</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">August 24, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Embedded Terminals, Git Version Control, and Audio Attachments</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>An embedded terminal and Git-native version control now live directly in the sidebar. This release also adds audio file attachments, interactive image commenting, richer previews for MCP tool execution, and core performance work.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.10.0" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(13)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added an embedded terminal directly in the sidebar (Ctrl/Cmd + `), allowing you to run build commands, test suites, and scripts without switching applications.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Introduced Git version control in the Review pane to inspect working tree diffs, stage and unstage files, and commit changes directly within the app.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved Git responsiveness and reduced background CPU usage on large repositories and projects with submodules.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added support for attaching audio files (MP3, WAV, M4A, AAC, OGG, FLAC, OPUS) up to 20MB using file picker or drag-and-drop.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">You can now drag to select regions and leave comments directly on any image in the file viewer, with smooth scrolling for tall images.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Model Context Protocol (MCP) and custom tool execution steps now display structured headers, expandable argument blocks, and formatted result previews.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Redesigned pending review comments in the chat input with unified attachment badges, file-grouped hover previews, and quick modal access for queued messages.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved sidebar smoothness and message streaming responsiveness during active generation.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">URL artifact cards now open supported links (such as local dev servers and Google Docs, Sheets, Slides, and Drive) directly in an in-app preview pane.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved folder picker navigation with live directory suggestions, Windows path support, and inline new folder creation.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">File search in the @-mention menu is now typo-tolerant, making it easier to reference files with approximate names.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Markdown file previews and artifacts now cleanly format frontmatter metadata as a structured key-value summary.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Double-clicking an option in single-select questions and confirmation prompts now immediately selects and submits the answer.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.10.0" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(8)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where the slash command menu and input suggestions displayed commands unsupported by the active custom agent.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where goal progress and summary sections were missing from the chat interface during /goal execution.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where unsaved an issue where quoted text and context items in question headers appeared as raw markup instead of interactive pills.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue during business login where id="rel-hub-2.9.1"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/releases?tab=hub&version=2.9.1" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 2.9.1">v2.9.1</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">August 20, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Remote Control, faster project switching, and more</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>This update introduces Remote Control to drive and monitor agent sessions on your local device from any browser, alongside core performance gains, improved sidebar navigation, and cleaner error recovery.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.9.1" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(11)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Introduced Antigravity Remote Control, allowing you to securely connect to and drive agent sessions on your local device from any browser, with proactive push notifications when agents complete tasks or require input.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved performance across the app: faster startup and project switching, faster loading of conversations with long histories, less lag during active sessions, and smoother opening and collapsing of the sidebar and side panels.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added the ability to collapse top-level sidebar sections. The ungrouped list now shows up to 30 conversations, and conversation actions appear instantly on hover.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Notification toasts now appear in the bottom-right corner and collapse into a compact stack that expands on hover, so they no longer cover the screen.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Hovering over a file's comment count pill now previews all comments on that file alongside their line numbers, and clicking a comment opens the referenced file, diff, or artifact with that comment focused.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Refreshed the Customizations settings screen with collapsible sections and item counts for skills, rules, plugins, and custom agents, consistent back navigation across sub-pages, and clearer card layouts and status indicators for MCP server configurations.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Clicking Retry on an agent error card now resumes the task directly, without adding an artificial "Continue" message to your conversation.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Custom agents can now reuse the skills, rules, and subagents you already have, using a single <code>inheritCustomizations</code> setting in the agent's frontmatter.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added support for uploading and attaching WebP images in chat.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added syntax highlighting for PowerShell (<code>.ps1</code>, <code>.psm1</code>, <code>.psd1</code>, and <code>pwsh</code>/<code>ps1</code> code fences) and Zsh (<code>.zsh</code>, <code>zsh</code>) in chat and the file viewer.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Refreshed the Google Docs, Sheets, Slides, and Drive icons to the 2026 Workspace logos, and URL artifact cards for those products now show the product icon instead of a generic link glyph.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.9.1" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(15)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where Find (Ctrl+F / Cmd+F) in the Review pane missed matches that were outside the visible area or hidden inside collapsed files and folded line blocks. Search now scans all open files and automatically expands and scrolls to each match.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an error that could stop you from rewinding a long conversation.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where a conversation could get stuck showing as still running and never return to idle, most often after pressing Stop.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Large binary attachments returned by connected MCP tools — images, PDFs, and audio — are now saved to a file and referenced in the conversation instead of being pasted inline, where they could previously overflow the conversation and break the turn.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where one invalid or unsupported MCP server configuration would prevent all other configured MCP servers from loading.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue on Windows where the Proceed button failed to appear on artifact review cards and plan editor headers.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where titles for long-running conversations could unexpectedly change to an older message.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where pinning, archiving, or restoring a conversation that was no longer available on disk appeared to succeed. The action is now reverted and an explanatory notification is shown.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where newly created conversations in the sidebar temporarily displayed raw link text instead of clean @-mention names.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added visible error notifications when file attachments fail because of file size limits, attachment counts, or unsupported formats.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where confirmation dialogs (such as reverting actions or deleting conversations) focused the close button by default, so pressing Enter dismissed the dialog instead of confirming it.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where updated images in chat could keep showing stale, cached versions; they now refresh on reload.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where comments added to markdown files or artifacts in preview mode attached to the first line instead of the selected text.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where images uploaded or pasted into the chat composer alongside a comment on an artifact were left out of the message sent to the model.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue in the Review pane where dragging file header pills into the chat composer did not correctly reference files beyond the first.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-hub-2.8.1"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 id="rel-hub-2.8.0"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/releases?tab=hub&version=2.8.0" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 2.8.0">v2.8.0</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">August 12, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Persistent Sidebar Folders, Crash Fixes, and UI Enhancements</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Antigravity improves workspace organization by persisting your sidebar folder states. This release also ensures reliable file and artifact previews.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.8.0" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(1)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">The sidebar now remembers which folders you have collapsed or expanded, including when you use the 'Collapse All' action, keeping your Projects list organized across sessions.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.8.0" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(6)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue that prevented submitting or dismissing the feedback form on the welcome screen.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed frequent application crashes when opening conversations that contain large command outputs. The file viewer now limits the size of files it will display, and large changes in the history are limited to ensure stability.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a styling issue where inline code in chat messages and the file viewer did not display in the correct colors.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a layout bug that caused multi-line commands to display without line breaks in the activity history.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where the application header and top menu bar failed to display on Linux.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where empty section headers (such as "Terminal") appeared in Settings when all contained settings were disabled.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-hub-2.7.1"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/releases?tab=hub&version=2.7.1" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 2.7.1">v2.7.1</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">August 11, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Reliability, Performance Gains, and User Experience Polish</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Antigravity 2.7.1 introduces visual side-by-side previews for image diffs alongside comprehensive screen-reader and keyboard accessibility enhancements. This update also delivers key performance boosts, bug fixes, and UX refinements across chat interactions and project navigation.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.7.1" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(11)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved performance of the conversation sidebar, especially when managing a large number of projects and conversations.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the performance of the conversation sidebar, reducing lag when switching between conversations.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added the ability to delete projects from the Project Settings page. Deleting a project permanently removes it along with all associated active and archived conversations.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added side-by-side previews for image files (including SVGs) when comparing file changes.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added a 'Last Prompt' sorting option to the display options menu, allowing you to sort your conversation history by the time of your last message.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the slash-command menu with smarter search ranking and highlighting, making it easier to find and select commands as you type.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Adjusted the appearance of the thumbs-up and thumbs-down feedback buttons on messages so they no longer look disabled.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">The conversation hover card now displays the project name next to a folder icon, showing where the conversation is saved.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">You can now use the keyboard to navigate to action buttons and menus that appear when hovering over list items (such as pinning or deleting conversations).</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved sidebar organization with new conversation grouping options. You can now group conversations by project or status while keeping standalone conversations separate, or view all conversations in a single combined list.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the design and organization of the display options menu in the projects sidebar and history pages. Additionally, menu checkboxes have been updated to display clearer selection states.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.7.1" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(20)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" astro-oabwef3t">Ensured that local web servers started by the agent are reachable from your host machine.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a visual issue where submenu items (such as the 'Copy' options) were vertically misaligned when opened.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where the chat input box lost focus after switching conversations using keyboard astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where line numbers were incorrectly displayed on file pills for images, audio, and video files in the chat.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue on mobile devices where the keyboard remained open after sending a message.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where selected checkbox options in menus changed text color, ensuring consistent styling across all menu items.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where the Review tab would get stuck loading when trying to display binary files or files that failed to load, replacing the spinner with an informative message.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where SVG images without specified dimensions would appear blank or collapsed when opened in the media viewer.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where renaming a conversation from the header menu did not work on mobile web browsers.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where SVG files failed to display and showed errors; they now render correctly as images in the file viewer.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where folders added to the prompt displayed file icons instead of folder icons.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a bug where searching for text inside a file diff would fail to return matches a bug in the conversation display options menu where the subtitle setting could appear unselected. Conversation subtitles now display folder and branch icons next to project and branch names for improved visual clarity.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the project selector in the input box to ensure you can see and select all your projects when starting a new conversation.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where search matches were not highlighted when searching within a file.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where the highlighted option in the @-mention menu could scroll out of view when navigating with the arrow keys.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where conversation titles in the sidebar would temporarily revert to "Untitled Conversation" while updates were loading.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where project artifacts would fail to load if the conversation was not active in the sidebar.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-hub-2.6.0"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Faster long conversations, more reliable hooks and subagents</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Conversations with long histories now open faster, improved custom hooks and subagents behavior, and administrator policies for connected tool servers are applied correctly — plus a broad set of fixes across chat, comments and the file viewer.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.6.0" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(9)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the ask questions panel: clearer option selection, and continue now only activates once you've answered the current question.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Hover details on the small icon chips in chat now appear almost immediately instead of after a noticeable pause.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Images and videos in chat now open in a preview window when clicked, with the option to open them in a tab instead.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Hook configurations that could never run are now rejected at load time with a clear error instead of being silently ignored.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Projects in the command palette now use the same folder icon as the sidebar, instead of a mismatched briefcase.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Opening long conversations is now dramatically faster.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">You can now copy an image to your clipboard from an artifact or the file viewer, using the menu or right-click.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">The conversation list now loads far faster for users with a large conversation history.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Settings descriptions now use consistent wording throughout, and two typos in the shortcut and host address settings were fixed.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.6.0" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(21)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Slash commands now work when a newline or tab separates the command from its arguments.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Conversations started by background automations no longer disappear from the sidebar list; they now appear under the scheduled-tasks filter.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Steps waiting on your input now show the icon for the tool involved, including in the permission prompt, instead of a generic bell.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the comment popup following your cursor while dragging, and whole-file comments landing on the first line with no highlight.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Agents no longer hang forever when a stop hook keeps blocking the end of a turn; the turn now finishes after repeated blocks.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Conversations that had already finished no longer stay stuck showing as running after you quit and reopen the app.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed hovering a message revealing the Stop button on every running subagent card; now only the card you hover shows it.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">The Review Changes button's keyboard focus outline now wraps the entire button instead of only part of it.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Hooks that call a model now stop at their configured timeout with a clear error, instead of stalling the agent indefinitely.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Pasting plain multi-line text into the chat box no longer adds an extra blank line between each line.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed pinning and unpinning a conversation often failing on the first click and snapping back, forcing you to click repeatedly.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Commands still running no longer display a nonsensical negative elapsed time when their start time is missing.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed administrator policies for connected tool servers being skipped at startup, which could let restricted servers start without their required settings.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed agents losing access to their built-in helpers, so research, browser and teamwork assistants work again.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the wrong keyboard shortcut being displayed for toggling the side panel.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the built-in browser inspection tool being wrongly blocked when your organization restricts which tool servers can run.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Stopping a subagent now also stops everything it spawned, including nested subagents and their background tasks, instead of leaving them running.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed long answers and previews periodically jumping back to the top and losing your scroll position while you read.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Custom hooks you define now run at the end of a turn instead of being skipped before they could fire.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a crash that happened when you selected a region of an image and submitted a comment on it.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a stray outline around the pending-step badge in the conversation list under some themes.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.5.0" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added support for signing in with your Gemini Enterprise user account, including support for organization admin controls.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Allocated and auto-assigned license seats against GE-Standard & GE-Plus organization subscription tiers.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Regionalized (US, EU, Global) model class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Sidecar links now render as pills that open the sidecar in the auxiliary pane.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added "Reveal in Finder/Explorer/File Manager" to the file viewer.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added "Collapse All Folders" and "Expand All Folders" to the command palette.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added search and keyboard navigation to the project selector.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added syntax highlighting for Objective-C, Swift, and Dart.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">External markdown links now reveal the destination URL on hover.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">The environment selector now remembers your last-used worktree.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Increased voice recording limits.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved @-mention file search speed and result stability.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Reduced typing lag while the agent streams responses.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Pinned conversations now show their workspace or project name.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved mobile UI with native pickers and no text selection drift and incorrect line numbers.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed Markdown tables and code blocks wrapping incorrectly.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed (Cmd+L / Ctrl+L) to quickly quote selected text into the chat input.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added support for attaching .json and .md files to the input box with distinct visual badges.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added preview (temporary) tabs to the auxiliary pane, allowing files to open in a temporary slot that is reused until promoted to a permanent tab (for example, by double-clicking).</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added a timeout for MCP server connections and tool calls to prevent the agent from hanging indefinitely.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added an 'Only Unread' filter option to the Conversation History dropdown.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added support for uploading and attaching CSV (.csv) files in chat as approval rules to reduce redundant permission prompts for everyday tools.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the speed of conversation switching by optimizing trajectory summary list selectors.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved authentication error messages to show the specific failure reason.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Draft comments are now automatically saved and preserved across sessions.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved interface responsiveness by preventing unnecessary re-renders when toggling sidebars or auxiliary panes.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved accessibility in the Settings modal with better heading hierarchy and keyboard focus management.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Updated the default settings screen name from 'Agent' to 'General' and updated copy for inherited settings ('Use Global' to 'Inherit General').</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Restored the default 5-second auto-dismiss behavior for in-app notifications.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Optimized scroll performance in conversation lists and panels, especially during long chats.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="hub" data-version="2.4.3" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added view options (grouping, sorting, filtering) and fixed search filtering on the Conversation History page.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue id="panel-cli" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="tab-cli" data-panel-id="cli" style="display: none;"><h2 class="rn-panel-heading astro-oabwef3t">Antigravity CLI</h2><div class="rn-timeline astro-oabwef3t"><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.3.1"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.3.1">v1.3.1</a></h3><span class="rn-latest-tag astro-oabwef3t">Latest</span></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">October 7, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Enhanced /diff navigation and position indicators, Windows plugin lifecycle management, and subagent and task status fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Enhances <code>/diff</code> file view navigation with preserved cursor positions, cross-file <code>n</code>/<code>N</code> change traversal, and a <code>change A/B · file X/Y</code> header indicator, while fixing <code>/rewind</code> on compacted messages, Windows <code>/plugin</code> MCP server lifecycle handling, relative paths in Markdown custom agents, subagent error states, and background task tracking.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.3.1" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(2)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved navigation in the <code>/diff</code> file view: <code>←</code>/<code>→</code> now open the next or previous file at its first change with the hunk header and leading context in view, each file remembers its cursor and scroll position when you switch files or go back to the file list, <code>n</code>/<code>N</code> continue into the next or previous file's changes instead of stopping at the last change in the current file, and the key-hint footer drops the generic scroll and page hints and wraps onto extra lines instead of being cut off on narrow terminals.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the <code>/diff</code> file view header to show where you are: it now ends with a right-aligned <code>change A/B · file X/Y</code> position, counting the block of added or removed lines under the cursor and the file's place in the file list (<code>file X/Y</code> is left out when only one file changed, and the position is hidden on narrow terminals).</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.3.1" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(10)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>/rewind</code> in very long conversations listing older messages as <code>(empty message)</code> and failing with an error when you picked one: messages that were cleared to save space now show their original text, dimmed and labeled <code>cleared to save space, can't rewind here</code>, and can no longer be selected.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed installing, reinstalling, and uninstalling plugins with <code>/plugin</code> on Windows failing while one of the plugin's MCP servers was running: the CLI now stops the plugin's MCP servers before replacing or deleting its folder and restarts them afterwards, and if another Antigravity process such as another <code>agy</code> session, the Antigravity Hub, or the <code>remote-control</code> daemon still holds the plugin open, it tells you to close it and retry. Plugin manifests, <code>mcp_config.json</code>, and <code>hooks.json</code> files saved with a UTF-8 byte-order mark now load, and installing from a marketplace no longer overwrites an existing plugin folder that came from a different source.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed markdown custom agents ignoring <code>skills:</code>, <code>plugins:</code>, <code>rules:</code>, <code>agents:</code>, and <code>hooks:</code> entries written as paths relative to the agent's own file when the agent ran as a subagent or as the main agent; such entries now resolve against the agent's directory, so an agent that lists <code>agents: [child.md]</code> can invoke <code>child</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed subagents that stopped on an error, such as running out of quota or model capacity, being shown as <code>Done</code> in <code>/agents</code> and the list of running agents; they now show <code>Error:</code> with the reason, and the error clears once the subagent makes further progress.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>/tasks</code>, the active task list, and the status line's task count showing only background shell commands; timers and recurring jobs the agent schedules, and other tools running in the background, now appear there too and are shown as <code>completed</code> or <code>failed</code> without a made-up exit code.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed messages sent with <code>Queued Messages</code> set to <code>Send Immediately</code> in <code>/config</code> being silently queued until the end of the turn whenever the agent had already written or updated an artifact, such as a task list or plan, during that turn; they now reach the agent right away.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed terminal notifications not firing when the agent stops to ask you a question; with <code>Notifications</code> turned on in <code>/config</code>, the CLI now alerts you for pending questions just as it does for tool permission prompts.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed restarting an MCP server from <code>/mcp</code> sometimes leaving it stopped, with an error such as <code>Failed to stop existing instances for reload: ... signal: killed</code>, when the old server process took too long to exit; the server now always starts again.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the sign-in error for accounts that need to verify their identity or appeal a Terms of Service block sometimes showing only <code>Verify your account to continue.</code> with no link; the verification or appeal link now always appears, and the CLI no longer suggests logging out and back in when that would not help.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>GEMINI_API_KEY</code> sessions failing a turn as if you had cancelled it when the connection to the Gemini API was dropped internally; the CLI now retries the request instead.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.3.0"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.3.0">v1.3.0</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">October 6, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Medium default verbosity, Vim-style j/k line navigation in /diff, and smooth trackpad scrolling</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Updates the default <code>Verbosity</code> setting to <code>medium</code> to group related tool calls and thoughts into concise summaries, aligns <code>j</code> and <code>k</code> in the <code>/diff</code> file view with standard line-by-line cursor movement, smooths trackpad and mouse-wheel scrolling over SSH and <code>tmux</code>, and fixes special-character and Windows drive path handling.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.3.0" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(2)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Changed the default <code>Verbosity</code> setting from <code>high</code> to <code>medium</code>, so the conversation view now groups related tool calls and thoughts into concise summaries while keeping commands and responses visible. This applies to everyone who never picked a verbosity, including anyone who selected <code>high</code> while it was still the default; set <code>Verbosity</code> back to <code>high</code> in <code>/config</code> to see every tool call, command, and thought in full again.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Changed <code>j</code> and <code>k</code> in the <code>/diff</code> file view to move the line cursor like the up and down arrow keys, matching the file list and Vim, instead of jumping to the next or previous file; use the left and right arrow keys to switch files.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.3.0" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(2)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed trackpad and mouse-wheel scrolling in the conversation view jumping back and forth, most noticeably over SSH or inside tmux, by ignoring the sideways part of diagonal swipes and limiting how far a single fast scroll step can move; <code>Page Up</code> and <code>Page Down</code> still move a full page.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed files and folders whose paths contain spaces, <code>#</code>, <code>%</code>, or other special characters, or that sit on a Windows drive, being mishandled across the CLI: <code>@</code> file mentions, <code>/codesearch</code> results, and file links in agent responses now open the right file, and workspace trust and project-scoped custom agents now recognize projects created in the Antigravity desktop app in such folders.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.2.17"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.2.17">v1.2.17</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">October 5, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 dismissible announcement cards above the prompt for model launches and service notices, improves the Windows command sandbox to run without administrator rights with out-of-the-box support for Python, Git, and <code>npm</code>, and fixes Markdown table alignment with emoji and <code>.tiff</code> image MIME handling.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.17" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(2)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added announcement cards above the prompt for model launches, deprecations, and other service notices. Cards appear one at a time, newest first; press <code>Esc</code> on an empty prompt to dismiss the current card permanently and show the next one, and sending a message hides the card for the rest of the session.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the Windows command sandbox so sandboxed commands no longer need administrator rights, and common tools such as Python, Git, and <code>npm</code> work inside the sandbox out of the box.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.17" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed markdown table class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>.tiff</code> images being reported with the wrong file type and silently converted to PNG when the agent viewed them.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.2.16"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.16" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added <code>←</code>/<code>→</code> shortcuts to <code>/config</code>: on a highlighted setting they switch to the previous or next value and save it right away, without opening the dropdown, and the footer shows a <code>←/→ Change</code> hint.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved responsiveness in long conversations in no flicker (altscreen) mode: resizing the terminal no longer freezes or lags the CLI, the full-screen view redraws the messages on screen first instead of showing text wrapped for the old width until the whole conversation has re-rendered, and interrupting the agent with <code>Esc</code> or resuming a conversation no longer re-renders every step already on screen.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved mouse text selection in the <code>altscreen</code> view and in the artifact viewer: dragging past the top or bottom now auto-scrolls, the selection stays on its text while the agent streams or you scroll with the wheel, and copying includes lines that scrolled off screen, without line numbers or comment previews from the artifact viewer.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the always-allow suggestion when approving <code>go</code> commands: approving a command such as <code>go vet</code> or <code>go build</code> now offers to allow that subcommand with any arguments, while <code>go run</code>, <code>go test</code>, <code>go generate</code>, <code>go install</code>, and <code>go tool</code> still require the exact command.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the <code>Send Immediately</code> option for Queued Messages in <code>/config</code>: a message you send while earlier messages are still queued now goes to the agent together with them, in order, instead of joining the queue, and a message that fails to send mid-turn goes back to the queue instead of being lost.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Changed how the agent generates images: it now hands image requests to a built-in <code>image-generator</code> subagent, which writes the prompt, checks each result with up to three attempts, and saves the images to the conversation's artifacts, so image generation appears as a subagent run in the conversation.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.16" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(7)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the agent stalling when a background command it was waiting on crashed or was killed by the system, for example when it ran out of memory; the agent now sees the command finish with its exit code, such as <code>137</code> or <code>134</code>, and keeps going.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed headless <code>-p</code> runs sometimes exiting before the agent could respond to a background command that finished after its first turn.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>k</code> in Vim Normal mode recalling the last history entry instead of your queued messages when pressed on the top line, which could send a queued message twice; <code>k</code> now pulls queued messages back into the editor like the <code>Up</code> arrow does.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>skills.json</code>, <code>rules.json</code>, and other customization manifests in a parent <code>.agents/</code> directory being ignored when a session started in a subdirectory; manifests now load from every <code>.agents/</code> directory between the working directory and the project root.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the <code>/</code> menu listing built-in skills that the current agent does not enable, which inserted instructions for tools the agent could not use when selected.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed settings failing to load when <code>~/.gemini/config/config.json</code> starts with a UTF-8 byte order mark, as files saved by Notepad or PowerShell <code>Set-Content</code> on Windows do.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed slash-command output and alerts where a line exactly as wide as the terminal had its last word pushed to the start of the next line without indentation.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.2.15"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.2.15">v1.2.15</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">October 2, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Native Android Termux binaries, lower-memory streaming redraws, and SSH and quota fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Adds prebuilt native Android binaries for Termux, reduces memory allocations during typing and streaming redraws, displays <code>/usage</code> quota reset times in the local time zone, scopes <code>repo</code> command allow-listing to subcommands, and fixes terminal resize freezes, exhausted AI credit retries, and SSH terminal image probing.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.15" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(5)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added prebuilt native Android binaries of the CLI that can run directly in Termux, without requiring a proot-distro environment.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved responsiveness while typing, scrolling, and streaming agent output by sharply reducing the memory the CLI allocates on every update and redraw.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved <code>-p "/usage"</code> to show quota reset times in your local time zone when printing to a terminal; piped and <code>--json</code> output still use UTC timestamps, so existing scripts are unaffected.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the always-allow suggestion when approving <code>repo</code> commands: it is now limited to the subcommand, such as <code>repo status</code>, instead of allowing every <code>repo</code> command, matching <code>git</code>, <code>hg</code>, and <code>jj</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved how the agent handles a permission request that is not approved, such as one raised by a subagent that cannot ask you: it now respects the denial and no longer tries to work around it with other commands, scripts, or tools.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.15" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(9)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the conversation view freezing for the rest of the session, with no new steps appearing while the agent kept working, after redraws such as several quick terminal resizes in a long conversation; the CLI now reconnects automatically, warns only if reconnecting fails, and sending a new message recovers a lost connection.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed requests made after your plan quota is used up and your AI credits balance cannot cover them: instead of retrying on <code>Working...</code> for about two and a half minutes and then showing the generic "Agent execution terminated due to error.", the CLI now immediately says "Your AI credits balance is too low to continue."</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed text like <code>Ga=q,f=32,s=1,v=1,i=31;AAAAAA==</code> being left behind in macOS Terminal.app after exiting the CLI over SSH; the CLI now asks the terminal to identify itself before probing for image support, and over SSH only Kitty and Ghostty are treated as image-capable, so WezTerm and Konsole no longer show garbled image placeholders.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the Vim mode cursor stopping inside emoji such as 👩‍💻 or ⚠️ and characters with combining marks such as Devanagari: <code>h</code>, <code>l</code>, and other motions now move over whole characters, and <code>x</code> and <code>r</code> delete or replace the whole character instead of a hidden part of it.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the search box in the <code>/resume</code> conversation picker wrapping long queries so that their beginning scrolled out of view; the box now uses the full terminal width.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the agent being blocked from reading your global rules and customization files in <code>~/.gemini/config</code>, including the <code>rules/</code> folder, <code>AGENTS.md</code>, <code>GEMINI.md</code>, <code>skills.json</code>, <code>rules.json</code>, <code>plugins.json</code>, and <code>agents.json</code>; it can now read them and asks before editing them, while other files in that folder stay off-limits.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed global rules being added to the agent's context more than once when <code>~/.gemini/GEMINI.md</code>, <code>~/.gemini/AGENTS.md</code>, or their <code>~/.gemini/config/</code> counterparts are symlinks to the same file.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed signing in to MCP servers whose OAuth client registration responds with HTTP <code>200</code> instead of <code>201</code>, which previously failed with <code>registration failed with status 200</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed large WebP images opened by the agent not being scaled down to fit image size limits the way PNG and JPEG images are.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.2.14"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.2.14">v1.2.14</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 30, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Queued messages configuration, systemd-free remote-control, faster conversation loading, and cursor and file handling fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Adds a configurable Queued Messages option in <code>/config</code>, supports running <code>remote-control</code> on Linux systems without systemd, accelerates resuming long conversation transcripts, factors attachments into automatic thread titles, hardens <code>--json-schema</code> validation, and fixes unicode cursor drift, special file hangs, and corrupted transcript step recovery.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.14" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(6)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added the <code>Queued Messages</code> option to <code>/config</code>: keep the default <code>Queue</code> to hold follow-up messages until the current turn ends, or choose <code>Send Immediately</code> to interrupt the agent with them; it can also be set with <code>"queuedMessages": "send-immediately"</code> in <code>settings.json</code>, which was previously ignored.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved <code>remote-control start</code> on Linux machines without a systemd user service manager, such as most containers: instead of failing, it now starts the daemon as a background process and warns that it will not restart after a crash or start at boot; <code>remote-control status</code> shows its PID and <code>remote-control stop</code> shuts it down.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved loading long conversations: resuming a conversation with thousands of steps is noticeably faster because the CLI no longer scans every step up front.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved automatically generated conversation titles to take images and files attached to your first message into account, so screenshot- or file-driven requests get specific titles and messages with only attachments get a title too.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the sign-in error shown to accounts blocked for a Terms of Service violation, which now includes a link to submit an appeal.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Changed <code>--json-schema</code> to reject plain text, bare type names such as <code>string</code>, and missing schema files instead of silently treating them as a string schema; these inputs, and any schema whose root is not <code>"type": "object"</code>, now fail at startup with an error and exit code <code>1</code>.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.14" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(3)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the prompt cursor drifting away from the end of the text after emoji such as ⚠️ or 👩‍💻, or scripts with combining marks such as Devanagari and Thai, especially inside <code>tmux</code>, and fixed prompt wrapping splitting such characters across two lines.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the agent hanging when it tried to view a pipe, socket, or device file, and a conversation getting stuck with <code>INVALID_ARGUMENT</code> errors after the agent viewed a truncated MP4, MOV, or M4A recording; both are now rejected up front with a clear message.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed resuming a conversation whose history had a missing step, for example after a crash or an interrupted write, hiding the most recent steps and letting new messages overwrite them.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.2.13"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.2.13">v1.2.13</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 29, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Dynamic retry delays for rate limits, rendering performance optimizations, and narrow terminal text wrapping</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Adopts server-requested retry delays for API rate limits while halting early on extended caps, optimizes conversation redraw rendering efficiency to reduce CPU and memory churn, clarifies artifact viewer mode toggle hints, and fixes text clipping on narrow terminals.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.13" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(3)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved rate-limit handling when the model API returns a retry delay: the CLI now waits the delay the server asks for instead of a fixed 5 seconds, and stops right away instead of retrying when the delay is longer than 30 seconds or the quota is a daily or billing cap.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved rendering efficiency, cutting CPU use and memory churn while the conversation view redraws, such as during fast scrolling or while the agent is working.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the artifact viewer's <code>m</code> hint to name the content and the mode it switches to, such as <code>diagram ASCII</code>, <code>diagram source</code>, or <code>math image</code>, instead of <code>toggle ASCII</code> or <code>toggle raw</code>.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.13" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(1)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the workspace trust dialog, the <code>/help</code> panel, and the sign-in and MCP authentication screens clipping text on narrow terminals of around 40 columns; long lines and navigation hints now wrap, and the <code>/help</code> tab bar compacts to fit.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.2.12"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.2.12">v1.2.12</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 27, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Smooth full-screen scrolling, fast quota exhaustion handling, and replay and terminal fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Coalesces bursts of scroll events to reduce latency in full-screen views, terminates exhausted Gemini API key sessions immediately without wasteful retries, speeds up long conversation replays in lower verbosities, prevents corrupt terminal titles inside screen/tmux/Zellij multiplexers, and fixes Vim mode multibyte character navigation and undo history.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.12" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(2)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved scrolling in the full-screen view: bursts of mouse-wheel or trackpad events are now coalesced before redrawing, which cuts CPU use and reduces scroll lag in long conversations and the artifact viewer.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved <code>GEMINI_API_KEY</code> sessions to stop immediately when the Gemini API reports an exhausted daily quota, a project or billing-account spend cap, or depleted prepaid credits, instead of spending several minutes on retries that cannot succeed; short-lived per-minute rate limits are still retried.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.12" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(3)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed resuming a long conversation in <code>medium</code> or <code>low</code> verbosity taking many seconds and lagging while history replayed: earlier tool groups now appear already finished instead of each re-animating, and keys pressed during the replay no longer refresh a half-loaded transcript.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the terminal or tab title changing to a string like <code>Ga=q,f=32,...</code> every time the CLI starts inside GNU <code>screen</code>, <code>tmux</code> (including iTerm2 <code>tmux -CC</code> tabs), or Zellij; the CLI no longer sends its image-support probe through a multiplexer, and <code>CLI_GRAPHICS=kitty</code> still forces image mode.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed Vim mode ignoring non-ASCII characters such as <code>é</code> or <code>中</code> after <code>r</code>, <code>f</code>, <code>F</code>, <code>t</code> and <code>T</code>, <code>fv</code> and <code>fV</code> in Visual mode leaving Visual mode instead of extending the selection, and <code>D</code>, <code>C</code>, or Visual <code>~</code>/<code>u</code>/<code>U</code> recording an empty undo step and clearing redo history when they changed nothing.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.2.11"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.2.11">v1.2.11</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 25, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Model reasoning effort level controls, unconfigured plugin handling, and terminal rendering and selection fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Introduces reasoning effort level controls selectable with <code>--effort</code> or <code>/effort</code>, adjusts unconfigured plugin loading behavior, restores ASCII fallbacks for Mermaid diagrams and LaTeX in WezTerm and VS Code terminals, improves artifact viewer copy interactions, and fixes project custom agent discovery across workspaces and headless runs.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.11" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(2)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved reasoning effort level for models with different support, selectable with <code>--effort</code> or from the effort gauge in <code>/effort</code> and <code>/model</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Changed plugins placed directly in <code>~/.gemini/config/plugins</code> so that a plugin whose MCP server needs configuration variables now starts disabled until you enable it, matching plugins installed through <code>/plugin</code>.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.11" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(3)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed Mermaid diagrams and LaTeX rendering as rows of garbled placeholder characters in WezTerm and the VS Code integrated terminal; these terminals are no longer assumed to support Kitty graphics and now show the ASCII fallback.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed copying text from the artifact viewer when <code>Copy on Select</code> is turned off in <code>/config</code>: the viewer no longer captures the mouse, so your terminal's own selection and copy shortcut work, and <code>Ctrl+C</code> goes back to interrupting or exiting.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed project custom agents in <code>.agents/agents/</code> not being found or selectable in workspaces created in the Desktop App, in already-trusted workspaces, under execution with <code>--agent</code>, in headless (<code>-p</code> / <code>--prompt</code>) runs, and in the <code>/agents</code> panel.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.2.10"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a
