href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.2.10">v1.2.10</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 24, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Medium verbosity mode, artifact viewer background rendering, sandbox improvements, and error handling fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Introduces medium verbosity mode to group related tool calls into concise summaries, pre-renders Mermaid diagrams and LaTeX in the artifact viewer for Kitty terminals, enhances terminal sandbox behavior and directory configuration scanning, and fixes headless run exit codes and subagent worktree artifact handling.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.10" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(6)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added <code>medium</code> verbosity mode to <code>/config</code>, between <code>high</code> and <code>low</code>, which groups related tool calls and thoughts into concise summaries (such as <code>Explored N files</code>) while keeping commands and responses visible; the Verbosity setting and each option now describe what they show.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved Mermaid diagram and LaTeX rendering in the artifact viewer on Kitty-compatible terminals: artifacts are pre-rendered in the background so diagrams appear as soon as the viewer opens, images are uploaded once at a smaller size, and zooming with <code>Ctrl+=</code> / <code>Ctrl+-</code> no longer flickers between the old and new sizes.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the artifact viewer footer by combining the separate top and bottom hints into one <code>g/G top/bottom</code> entry, labeling <code>m</code> with the view it switches to (diagram, LaTeX, ASCII, or raw), and fixing hints that contain arrow symbols wrapping onto a new line too early.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved terminal sandbox behavior so the agent asks to bypass the sandbox less often: it now knows sandboxed commands can read and write its own artifact and scratch directories, and it tries the sandbox first even after an earlier command needed a bypass.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Changed the step title of commands that exit with a non-zero code from <code>Errored</code> to <code>Failed</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Changed how directory entries in <code>skills.json</code>, <code>rules.json</code>, <code>agents.json</code>, and <code>plugins.json</code> are scanned: an entry now loads only the items directly inside the directory, the same as a <code>.agents/skills/</code> folder, instead of recursively loading everything beneath it; to load a nested item, name it in <code>include_only</code>, for example <code>{"path": "shared_skills", "include_only": ["category/my-skill"]}</code>.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.10" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(5)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed pressing <code>Esc</code> while the suggestions dropdown is open interrupting the agent's turn; it now closes the dropdown, and a second <code>Esc</code> interrupts.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed Mermaid diagrams and LaTeX leaving a blank gap inside <code>tmux</code> or GNU <code>screen</code> when the outer terminal supports Kitty graphics; the CLI now falls back to ASCII diagrams unless images actually reach the terminal, and <code>CLI_GRAPHICS=kitty</code> still forces image mode for setups with image passthrough configured.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed headless (<code>-p</code> / <code>--prompt</code>) runs that streamed part of a response and then ended on a model or agent error exiting with code 0; they now exit with code <code>3</code> and print the <code>AGY_ERROR</code> line, and JSON error output includes the partial response, while multi-turn <code>stream-json</code> sessions still warn and continue.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed machines set up with the old Remote Control installer script crash-looping after an upgrade; when the CLI is launched by that deprecated background service it now unregisters the service and points to <code>remote-control start</code> instead of restarting repeatedly.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed files that subagents write inside their own Git worktree being treated as artifacts, which demanded artifact metadata and left <code>.metadata.json</code> files in the worktree; subagent worktrees now live under a <code>worktrees/</code> directory in the app data directory.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.2.9"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.2.9">v1.2.9</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 23, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Direct subagent messaging syntax, Vim numeric count multipliers, headless lifecycle handling, and concurrency fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Introduces direct <code>@\<subagent\> \<message\></code> prompt syntax with interactive autocomplete, adds Vim count multipliers across Normal and Visual modes, enhances headless execution lifecycle and task timeout handling, and fixes file-lock concurrency issues in conversation history databases along with streaming response stability fixes.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.9" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(6)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added <code>@\<subagent\> \<message\></code> prompt syntax to send a message directly to a subagent conversation, with autocomplete listing running and completed subagents.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added Vim numeric count multipliers so counts apply to operators, motions, and actions in Normal and Visual modes, including <code>3dw</code>, <code>2d3w</code>, <code>3dd</code>, <code>3x</code>, <code>3rX</code>, <code>3p</code>, <code>3u</code>, <code>[count]G</code>/<code>gg</code>/<code>$</code>, and counted text objects such as <code>2di(</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved <code>GEMINI_API_KEY</code> sessions to reduce behavior discrepancies with the non-API-key sign-in path.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the artifact viewer to show a Left/Right pan hint in the footer when a wide diagram overflows the window.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved <code>/rewind</code> to show a relative timestamp for each step and to focus the most recent step when the panel opens.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved command allow-listing suggestions to recognize <code>jj config</code> and <code>jj op</code> subcommands when offering to always allow a command.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.9" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(9)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed headless (<code>-p</code> / <code>--prompt</code>) runs leaving daemon background processes running after exit, which could hang scripts reading the CLI's output until end-of-file; daemon processes now terminate when the run ends.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed headless (<code>-p</code> / <code>--prompt</code>) runs cancelling still-running background tasks about 5 seconds after the agent went idle; runs now wait for background tasks until the <code>--print-timeout</code> deadline, up to a 30-minute cap.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a conversation-history database corruption risk where checking the database file for write access could silently drop file locks held by concurrent CLI processes on the same file.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed context compaction failing when the tool configuration used for compaction checkpoints was rejected in certain scenarios.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a backend crash when a streamed model response chunk arrived without its response envelope, which terminated the session with connection errors.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed markdown table column alignment when a table cell contains file links that wrap across lines.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed markdown file links to code symbols dropping their display text and rendering the raw path instead.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed incomplete enterprise sign-ins (for example closing the browser window before finishing license or project selection) leaving behind a stuck partial credential; such credentials are now cleared automatically so sign-in can be retried cleanly.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the browser companion page title to read <code>Antigravity CLI</code> instead of <code>Antigravity Cli</code>.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.2.8"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.2.8">v1.2.8</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 22, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Proportional context compaction budgets, full-window sizing, custom model media support, and leak fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Improves context compaction by spreading user-request budgets proportionally across all prompts and sizing truncation ceilings from the model's full context window, enables default PDF and audio support for custom models, routes <code>Ctrl+G</code> editor launches to a real terminal stdout, caps voice dictation recordings to avoid deadline failures, and resolves resource and memory leaks across conversations and background pollers.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.8" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(2)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved context compaction to spread its user-request budget across all captured prompts, so a long initial instruction is no longer truncated to a small fixed slice when the other prompts in the conversation are short.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved context compaction to size summary and truncation budgets from the model's full context window instead of the compaction trigger threshold, so compacted summaries and background-task lists are no longer prematurely cut short.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.8" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(9)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed PDF and audio tool outputs and attachments failing with <code>unsupported mime type</code> errors on custom models configured in <code>settings.json</code>; custom models now accept PDFs by default and honor the <code>modelFeatures</code> media flags for images, video, PDF, and audio.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed pressing <code>Ctrl+G</code> to edit the prompt in a full-screen terminal editor such as <code>vim</code> hanging with <code>Vim: Warning: Output is not to a terminal</code>; external editors now run against a real terminal stdout.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed voice dictation sessions longer than 4 minutes failing with a deadline error and erasing the drafted transcription; recordings now automatically stop and finalize at 3 minutes 30 seconds.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the startup banner displaying a Google Cloud Project ID for consumer (non-enterprise) sign-ins.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a stack-overflow crash when loading or compacting conversations containing background-task, subagent-management, messaging, or scheduling steps.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed forked conversations inheriting step output data from steps after the fork point, which could collide with new steps produced in the fork.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a background poller and timer leaking for every conversation session, slowly accumulating memory over long-running sessions.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed canceled conversation and workspace creation requests continuing to provision resources in the background; aborted requests now stop immediately.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed quitting the CLI taking around 5 extra seconds before the process exited; shutdown now cancels open streaming connections immediately instead of waiting for a forced timeout.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.2.7"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.2.7">v1.2.7</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 19, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Inline Kitty graphics for math and Mermaid diagrams, interactive ask_question navigation, dedicated rule token budgets, and 30s retry backoff</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Introduces inline Kitty graphics rendering for LaTeX math and Mermaid diagrams in the artifact detail viewer with ASCII fallback and horizontal panning, interactive question navigation with write-in previews in <code>ask_question</code>, dedicated 20,000-token rule budgets to prevent customization eviction, and caps model retry backoff at 30 seconds, alongside plugin lifecycle hardening, Bubble Tea v2.0.9 upgrades, and headless background task logging.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.7" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(7)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added inline Kitty graphics rendering for LaTeX math equations (<code>$$...$$</code> and fenced <code>math</code>, <code>latex</code>, and <code>tex</code> blocks) and Mermaid flowcharts and sequence diagrams in the artifact detail viewer, with Unicode ASCII fallback, Left/Right arrow horizontal panning for wide diagrams, and <code>m</code> to cycle between Image, ASCII, and Raw views.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the <code>ask_question</code> interactive prompt with Left/Right arrow navigation between questions, inline previews of confirmed write-in answers, check marks on answered options, and single-step submission on the final question.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the CLI startup banner for enterprise accounts to display the active Google Cloud Project ID beneath the signed-in account and plan tier.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved model API retry responsiveness by capping per-attempt retry backoff at 30 seconds instead of waiting up to 4 minutes between attempts.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved customization token budgeting by giving user and workspace rules a dedicated 20,000-token budget (cutting oversized rules on newline boundaries and listing over-budget rules by path and description) so large rule sets no longer evict skills, workflows, subagents, or MCP tools.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the <code>/usage</code> panel by removing the duplicate remaining-quota percentage line beneath each progress bar.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the default agent and subagent toolset by retiring the legacy <code>find_by_name</code>, <code>grep_search</code>, and <code>list_dir</code> tools from the default baseline while keeping them available to custom agents that explicitly list them in <code>tools</code>.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.7" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(6)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed plugin skill slash commands being double-prefixed (<code>/\<plugin\>:\<plugin\>:\<skill\></code>) when a skill's frontmatter name already includes the plugin prefix, or being shadowed when multiple plugins define skills with the same short name.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed plugin upgrades leaving old MCP server and sidecar processes running from deleted install directories, and pruned superseded plugin versions and staging directories from the marketplace cache on startup.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed disabled plugins disappearing from the <code>/plugin</code> list after enabled-only customization queries, and hardened plugin ID path validation on uninstall.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed terminal rendering and Kitty keyboard protocol stack handling on exit and screen clear by upgrading Bubble Tea to v2.0.9.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed headless (<code>-p</code> / <code>--prompt</code>) runs occasionally skipping the background-task waiting notice, logged background SDK tool progress to task log files, and reduced memory usage by cloning truncated command output previews.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed starting the CLI unpinning conversations or marking them unread in the desktop app.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.2.6"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.2.6">v1.2.6</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 18, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Session-scoped Remote Control, unlimited headless run timeouts, structured error reporting, and artifact text selection</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Introduces session-scoped Remote Control using <code>--remote-control</code> and <code>/remote-control</code>, lifts the default 5-minute timeout on headless runs, adds structured <code>AGY_ERROR</code> output on stderr, improves word-wise navigation in the prompt editor, and fixes Remote Control workspace permission inheritance, oversized file edit storage limits, full-screen artifact text selection, queued <code>/model</code> switching, and <code>/rewind</code> snapshot resolution.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.6" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(4)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added Remote Control (start a connection using <code>--remote-control</code> startup flag or <code>/remote-control</code> slash command) to create a session-scoped remote connection for following and controlling your active terminal session from another device. Typing <code>/remote-control off</code> or closing the session automatically tears down the tunnel and unregisters the device from the active Remote Control session list.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Changed the default timeout for headless (<code>-p</code> / <code>--prompt</code>) runs from 5 minutes to unlimited so long-running agent turns run until the response completes unless <code>--print-timeout</code> is passed explicitly, and enabled daemon background commands in headless <code>GEMINI_API_KEY</code> sessions so background servers stay running after the turn finishes.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved headless (<code>-p</code> / <code>--prompt</code>) error reporting when a turn terminates on an agent or model API failure: the CLI now prints a structured <code>AGY_ERROR: {...}</code> JSON line on stderr with canonical status, HTTP or gRPC error code, retryability, and error ID (including HTTP status mapping for <code>GEMINI_API_KEY</code> SDK errors) and exits with code <code>3</code> instead of <code>1</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved word-wise cursor movement (<code>Alt+F</code>, <code>Alt+B</code>, and <code>Ctrl</code>/<code>Alt</code>+Arrow keys) and word deletion (<code>Ctrl+W</code>, <code>Alt+Backspace</code>, and <code>Alt+D</code>) in the prompt editor to stop at punctuation boundaries instead of only whitespace, making it easy to step through or delete individual segments of file paths, URLs, and flags.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.6" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(6)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed turns triggered from a connected Remote Control session or running across secondary workspaces executing without the CLI session's active permission mode, cycle mode, and non-workspace file access grants.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed artifacts in the Remote Control companion rendering as plain <code>\<name\>.md</code> file links instead of rich artifact cards when the CLI's application data directory differs from the default bundle name.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed text selection in the full-screen artifact viewer capturing the line-number gutter and trailing padding; dragging across the viewer now highlights and copies only the document text, with <code>Ctrl+C</code> to re-copy an active selection, <code>Esc</code> to clear it, and <code>Shift</code>+drag for native terminal selection.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed submitting <code>/model \<name\> \<prompt\></code> while a turn is already running switching the active model immediately in the middle of the in-flight turn; the one-shot model switch now waits until the queued prompt begins executing so the running turn and any earlier queued prompts finish on the session's original model.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed file edits with diffs larger than 1 MiB exceeding the conversation storage limit and force-clearing the session; oversized edits now preserve their line-change statistics while omitting the raw diff body from stored conversation history and skipping the elided diff during <code>/rewind</code> reverts.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>/rewind</code> conversation reverts and forks sweeping backward to an older workspace snapshot when recent snapshot commits were skipped, which could silently overwrite kept work; snapshot lookup now sweeps forward from the target step and falls back to step-by-step revert replay when no later snapshot exists.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.2.5"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.2.5">v1.2.5</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 17, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Subagent roster prompt injection, background task names, credential invalidation handling, and thinking frame freeze fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Introduces automatic subagent roster and instruction injection for custom Markdown agents, preserves explicit background task names across notifications and lists, cleanly clears invalidated auth credentials, and fixes interrupted command exit codes, remote cancellation indicators, artifact viewer window resizing, and frozen thinking frames.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.5" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(2)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added automatic subagent guidance for custom agents defined in Markdown: agents that list <code>invoke_subagent</code> in their tools now receive the roster of available subagents and usage instructions in their system prompt, so they can actually delegate to the subagents they declared.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved background task naming so a task keeps the name it was given when sent to the background; completion notifications and task lists now show that name instead of a generic auto-derived one.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.5" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(5)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed stale credentials lingering after the sign-in server definitively rejects them (for example a revoked or invalidated enterprise account); the CLI now signs you out and clears the stale tokens so you can sign in again cleanly, while temporary network failures, server errors, and rate limits never cost you your session.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed commands killed by an outside signal (for example when the whole session shuts down mid-run) being recorded as successful runs with exit code 0; such commands now finish as canceled while keeping the output collected before the interruption.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the <code>Interrupted</code> hint only appearing when Esc was pressed locally in the terminal; it now appears whenever the current turn is actually cancelled, including when it is stopped from a connected remote session.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the artifact viewer leaving markdown text stuck at its original width after a terminal resize; content now re-wraps to the new window size.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed in-progress thinking frames occasionally freezing permanently in the conversation history (for example showing <code>Thinking... (31s)</code> forever), including after a terminal resize or session reload.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.2.4"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.2.4">v1.2.4</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 16, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Live skill reloading, interactive model search, mid-session thought signature fixes, and self-correcting tool validation</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Adds <code>/skills reload</code> for live skill discovery, introduces fuzzy search and autocomplete for <code>/model</code>, improves OAuth scope error guidance, and resolves mid-conversation model switching errors with Gemini thinking models, <code>search_web</code> summarization failures, tool schema validation recovery, and daemon conversation resumption.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.4" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(3)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added a <code>/skills reload</code> subcommand to asynchronously reload discovered skills and slash commands without restarting the session or blocking user input.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added an inline argument autocomplete dropdown when typing <code>/model</code> and a live fuzzy search bar inside the interactive <code>/model</code> picker to filter models by name or ID.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved authentication error messages when OAuth tokens lack required scopes by including explicit instructions to run <code>/logout</code> and <code>/login</code>.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.4" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(10)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed mid-conversation model switching to Gemini thinking models failing with <code>thought_signature</code> validation errors when prior turns were generated by non-Gemini models.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed web search (<code>search_web</code>) failing with <code>"no summary returned from GenerateContent"</code> when the summarization model emits leading thought parts.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed agent turns terminating prematurely with <code>NO_TOOL_CALL</code> when a tool call fails schema validation, allowing the agent loop to return the validation error and self-correct.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed MCP tool schema augmentation mutating tool definitions in place, preventing strict argument validation failures.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>hooks.json</code> configurations being silently dropped when customization token budget truncation is active.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed custom subagents losing configured <code>exclude</code> skill filters when runtime skill filtering is applied.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed subagent status and termination tracking under pubsub batch coalescing so killed subagents are not reset to active by late step updates.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed conversations with active turns or running daemon background tasks failing to auto-resume after a restart.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed terminal <code>ERROR</code> steps (such as failed tool executions or timeouts) being omitted from <code>transcript.jsonl</code> conversation logs.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed raw OSC 8 escape sequences rendering in <code>Apple_Terminal</code> over SSH connections by disabling unsupported hyperlink sequences.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.2.3"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.2.3">v1.2.3</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 15, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Copy support for side questions, native backend tool-choice constraints, and subagent MCP inheritance</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Introduces <code>/copy btw</code> to copy side-question responses directly to the clipboard, translates tool-choice constraints to native backend function calling, and fixes plugin hook discovery, subagent MCP server inheritance, relative config path resolution, and premature proactive feedback prompts.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.3" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(2)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added the <code>/copy btw</code> slash subcommand to copy the full text of the active <code>/btw</code> side-question response to the clipboard even when the response card is collapsed or scrolled, along with argument ghost hints when typing <code>/copy</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved tool-calling fidelity by translating per-request tool-choice constraints (<code>any</code>, <code>required</code>, and named functions) into native backend function-calling configurations.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.3" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(3)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the <code>/hooks</code> command and hook inspection utilities omitting hooks bundled inside enabled plugins when listing active <code>hooks.json</code> configurations.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed custom subagents created with <code>enable_mcp_tools: true</code> receiving an empty MCP server list instead of inheriting the parent agent's configured MCP servers, and fixed declarative subagent configurations failing to resolve relative config file paths (<code>relative_path_to_config</code>) against the parent agent's directory.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed proactive feedback prompts triggering prematurely during the first few turns of a session or on transient, recovered tool errors.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.2.2"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.2.2">v1.2.2</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 12, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Plugin MCP namespacing, conversation memory leak fixes, oversized file guardrails, and unsandboxed rule migration warnings</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Enhances startup migration diagnostics for unsandboxed permission rules, speeds up <code>/resume</code> with stale caches, automatically namespaces plugin MCP servers to avoid collision, resolves step-cache goroutine leaks, and fixes critical issues with Gemini API thinking block retention, oversized binary file loading, artifact reviews, and database cleanup.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.2" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(2)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the startup warning for deprecated <code>unsandboxed</code> permission rules across CLI, shared, and project configuration files to list each affected file path, up to five offending rules, and step-by-step instructions for migrating them to <code>command</code> rules.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved <code>/resume</code> startup responsiveness when opening a large conversation history with a cold or stale summary cache.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.2" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(7)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed MCP servers bundled inside plugins colliding with each other or with user-configured servers in <code>mcp_config.json</code> when they shared the same server name by automatically namespacing plugin MCP servers as <code>\<plugin\>_\<server\></code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a memory leak where opening or scanning conversations left background step-cache eviction goroutines running for the rest of the session, significantly reducing memory usage after opening <code>/resume</code> or switching conversations.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>view_file</code> attempting to parse non-UTF-8 binary files as text or loading files larger than 100 MB into the model context; unsupported binary formats and oversized files are now rejected with a clear error before overflowing the context window.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed Gemini API (<code>GEMINI_API_KEY</code>) sessions dropping model thinking blocks from prior turns on subsequent user messages and failing to propagate thought signatures on text and thought parts.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed artifact review failing to trigger when a directory name above <code>.gemini/</code> matched a skipped path component such as <code>scratch</code>, prevented conversation forks and snapshot reverts from copying internal <code>.system_generated/subagents</code> and <code>.system_generated/worktrees</code> directories, and cleaned up subagent metadata records when deleting a conversation.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed deleted conversations being recreated as empty, schema-less SQLite database files when background queries reconnected after deletion.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed conversations launched without a workspace folder inheriting workspace paths and customizations from other open sessions.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.2.1"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.2.1">v1.2.1</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 11, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Custom agent component exclusions, resilient model API retry handling, and conversation continuation fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Introduces <code>excludeDefaultComponents</code> for custom agent Markdown definitions, adds automated exponential backoff retries for transient model API errors, improves MCP open object schema validation, and fixes issues with <code>--continue</code> workspace resolution, inline terminal screen flashes, Remote Control auth display, and sandbox status indicators.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.1" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(4)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added support for <code>excludeDefaultComponents: true</code> in custom agent Markdown frontmatter, allowing custom agents to opt out of default prompt sections and built-in tools while preserving post-invocation hooks.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved model API error resilience and diagnostics: transient <code>genai.APIError</code> failures (<code>502</code>, <code>503</code>, <code>504</code>, per-minute <code>429</code> rate limits, and mid-stream interruptions) automatically retry in-process with exponential backoff while preserving completed tool call outputs, and unrecovered <code>503</code> and <code>429</code> responses surface clear user-facing error messages.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved MCP and provider tool schema validation to preserve open object schemas (such as <code>{"type": "object"}</code> or explicit <code>additionalProperties: true</code>) instead of rejecting undeclared arguments on schemas that allow them.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved per-turn responsiveness and reduced memory usage when opening large conversations.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.1" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(5)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>--continue</code> starting a brand-new conversation when launched from a subdirectory, after a crash, or while another session is open in the same workspace; it now falls back to the most recent non-empty conversation in the current workspace or its parent/child directories.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed full-screen blank flashes in inline mode when content first pushes to scrollback, at the end of a turn, or when clearing the screen with <code>Ctrl+L</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed remote companion UIs connected to an interactive CLI session using Remote Control displaying an unauthenticated sign-in screen instead of the active session's signed-in state.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the status line reporting the terminal sandbox as disabled when the session was launched with the <code>--sandbox</code> command-line flag.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed image zoom keys (<code>Ctrl+=</code> and <code>Ctrl+-</code>) and the footer zoom hint appearing in the artifact viewer when a Mermaid diagram is rendered in ASCII mode instead of as a Kitty image.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.2.0"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.2.0">v1.2.0</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 10, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Background service registration with remote-control, expanded half-page scrolling, and content safety reporting</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Introduces remote-control service manager subcommands to run the CLI as a persistent background daemon, broadens half-page scrolling support and keybindings, and resolves issues with content filter handling, scratch directory watchers, plugin MCP initialization, schema validation, and conversation resumption.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.0" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(2)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added <code>remote-control start</code>, <code>remote-control status</code>, and <code>remote-control stop</code> subcommands to register the CLI with your operating system's service manager as a background daemon that persists across logouts and reboots, including <code>--name \<label\></code> for custom instance labels and <code>--session</code> to scope to the active login session.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved half-page scrolling across all views (altscreen mode, diff viewer, and detail panels where <code>Ctrl+D</code> no longer triggers exit prompts) and added <code>Shift+Up</code> and <code>Shift+Down</code> default keybindings (<code>navigation.half_page_up</code> and <code>navigation.half_page_down</code>).</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.2.0" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(8)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed prompts or model responses blocked by content safety filters failing with generic errors or empty turns by surfacing explicit content-filter stop reasons.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed temporary files in <code>scratch/</code> directories triggering recursive filesystem watchers and cluttering artifact reviews and checkpoints.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed MCP servers bundled inside global plugins failing to initialize at CLI startup or failing to update running states when plugins are toggled.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed schema validation errors for third-party MCP server tools whose input schemas omit <code>additionalProperties</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed redundant filesystem customization discovery walks running on every message when no slash commands were invoked.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed spurious <code>ERROR: logging before google.Init</code> log lines appearing in <code>cli.log</code> and background daemon outputs.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed macOS background service lifecycle issues, legacy script cleanup, and paid Google Cloud project license availability for Remote Control.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed older conversations failing to load with <code>unknown step type</code> errors upon resumption.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.1.28"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.1.28">v1.1.28</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 9, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Model API retry resilience, headless print optimizations, and detailed tool approval prompts</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Improves resilience to transient model API errors with extended exponential backoff, accelerates startup and sign-in by reading cached credentials, streamlines headless execution and error reporting, enhances tool approval prompts with contextual action descriptions and reasons, and fixes issues across plugin reinstalls, plugin MCP working directories, subagent lifecycle states, and command memory leaks.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.28" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(9)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved resilience to transient model API errors by retrying errors such as <code>503 Unavailable</code> with extended exponential backoff to prevent brief hiccups from aborting sessions.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved sign-in and startup speed by reading signed-in identity details from stored credentials instead of making network requests on every launch.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved headless (<code>-p</code>) runs to exit promptly upon delivering final answers, waiting for non-daemon background tasks and scheduled timers within <code>--print-timeout</code> while preserving running background daemons.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved failure reporting in headless (<code>-p</code>) runs by printing fatal errors to stderr with stable <code>error:</code> markers and adding truncation notifications.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved headless (<code>-p</code>) latency by eliminating up to 200 ms of idle delay per turn and skipping unused conversation title generation model calls.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved tool approval prompts to display specific action requests (for example, <code>Run this command?</code>, <code>Allow access to this URL?</code>, or <code>Allow calling this tool?</code>), restricting command editing to commands and including a <code>Reason:</code> explanation when triggered by hooks or cross-project files.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved model selection auditability by logging alias resolutions, <code>--effort</code> variant mappings, and deprecated model replacements in <code>cli.log</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Changed <code>--print-timeout</code> expiration behavior in headless mode to return partial output and exit successfully with a warning on stderr instead of raising a timeout failure.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Changed default URL fetching permissions to prompt for approval by default before reading external URLs unless access has been pre-granted.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.28" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(7)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed plugin reinstalls leaving behind files deleted from the source by replacing managed plugin directories cleanly, preventing self-directory installation corruption, and cleaning up disabled state entries from <code>config.json</code> upon uninstall.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed plugin-defined MCP servers resolving relative working directories incorrectly by scoping them directly to the plugin's root directory.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a startup race condition where subagents could fail to discover tools from MCP servers that were still initializing.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed subagents occasionally appearing stuck in running states in large conversations, resolving delays delivering queued idle messages.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a memory leak where finished terminal command executions retained internal state throughout long-running sessions.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed headless (<code>-p</code>) runs hanging indefinitely on interactive implementation-plan approval prompts by proceeding through plan review automatically in non-interactive mode.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed sign-in failing with project access errors after switching from a business account with a selected project to a personal account by resetting cached project and license data.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.1.27"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.1.27">v1.1.27</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 5, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Mid-conversation model execution, custom agent subagent dependencies, and MCP parameter validation fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Introduces /model \<name\> \<prompt\> for running one-off prompts with alternative models, adds conversation_title metadata to status line scripts, adds subagent dependency declarations in custom agent Markdown frontmatter, and delivers fixes for MCP argument schema validation, headless permission handling, Vim normal mode shortcuts, and cross-conversation artifact access prompts.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.27" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(7)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added <code>/model \<name\> \<prompt\></code>, which runs a single prompt on another model and then returns the session to the model it was using, so you can consult a different model mid-conversation without disturbing your saved default.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added a <code>conversation_title</code> field to the JSON payload passed to custom status line and window title scripts, so they can show the active conversation's name and pick up renames immediately.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added an <code>agents</code> list to custom agent Markdown frontmatter, letting an agent declare the subagents it depends on using the same workspace-relative, absolute, and agent-relative path rules as <code>skills</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved <code>/model</code> to show a <code>[model]</code> argument hint as you type, so the command's arguments are discoverable without opening help.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the <code>/settings</code> panel so each Verbosity option explains itself as you highlight it, describing whether tool calls, commands, and thoughts are shown in full or collapsed into a token metrics table.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the sign-in and Google Cloud setup screens to use the CLI's standard styled key hints, which follow your color scheme and keybindings and drop the navigation hint when only one option is available.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Changed <code>/model \<name\></code> to report <code>Model already set to \<name\></code> when the session is already using that model, instead of reporting a switch that did not happen.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.27" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(6)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed tool calls to MCP servers accepting arguments the server's own schema never declared, so an invented parameter is now rejected and corrected instead of being silently dropped.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed headless runs with <code>-p</code> silently skipping tool actions they were not permitted to take, which now end with a notice naming the refused actions and report them as <code>denied_actions</code> in the JSON output.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>?</code> in Vim Normal mode opening the shortcuts panel while the prompt already contained text, so character-search and replace operators such as <code>f?</code>, <code>t?</code>, and <code>df?</code> work; <code>?</code> still opens shortcuts on an empty prompt.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>/model</code> with no argument doing nothing when run before the session had finished starting up, so it now opens the model picker.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed print mode (<code>-p</code>) exiting before the session finished shutting down, which could drop the run's trailing conversation history before it reached disk.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a redundant approval prompt when the agent reads or writes another conversation's artifact files after you have already allowed access to files outside your workspace.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.1.26"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.1.26">v1.1.26</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 4, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Artifact viewer half-page scrolling, workspace picker grouping setting, and SQLite WAL flushing</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Adds half-page scrolling (Ctrl+D / Ctrl+U) to the artifact viewer, adds the pickerGrouping configuration setting for /resume, improves Mermaid flowchart ASCII rendering, updates unselected model reasoning defaults to medium, and resolves issues with worktree cleanup, SQLite WAL checkpointing on exit, and subagent approval prompts.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.26" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(5)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added half-page scrolling with <code>Ctrl+D</code> and <code>Ctrl+U</code> to the artifact viewer.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added the <code>pickerGrouping</code> setting to <code>/config</code> and <code>settings.json</code> to configure the default conversation list view in <code>/resume</code> (flat or grouped by workspace).</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved terminal ASCII diagram rendering for Mermaid flowcharts in the artifact viewer and conversation.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved <code>/logout</code> execution time by short-circuiting token removal directly to file storage when keyring storage is bypassed or unreachable.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Changed unselected model families in the interactive <code>/model</code> picker to default to medium reasoning effort instead of low.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.26" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(4)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed subagent tasks unexpectedly prompting for tool approval in always-proceed mode while viewing the subagent details panel.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed orphaned Git worktrees accumulating on disk under <code>.system_generated/worktrees</code> when killing subagents or deleting conversations.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed SQLite database WAL checkpointing on CLI exit so trailing session metadata updates are flushed to disk before shutdown.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed customization discovery logging spurious errors when accessing temporary directories or paths outside the active workspace.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.1.25"><div class="rn-version-col ambient skills, rules, and subagents by default, and delivers fixes for long MCP OAuth authorization codes, Windows skill path separators, <code>/boost</code> worker tools, and runtime nil pointer crashes.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.25" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(3)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added an opt-in workspace-grouped view to the <code>/resume</code> conversation picker, allowing users to toggle between a flat list and conversations grouped by directory with <code>Ctrl+F</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added Gemini 3.8 Flash to the model catalog when connecting with a <code>GEMINI_API_KEY</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Changed custom agents defined in Markdown to inherit ambient skills, rules, and subagents by default, matching the configuration of default agents.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.25" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(7)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed MCP OAuth authentication failing when authorization servers return authorization codes longer than 1024 characters.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed skill path matching and grouping in the <code>/skills</code> panel on Windows where host path separators (<code>\</code>) caused global and workspace skills to be misclassified.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed relative path resolution in Markdown-defined custom agents so relative subagent and plugin paths resolve correctly against the agent definition's directory.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed duplicate permission grants accumulating in configuration settings across session reloads and subagent invocations.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>/boost</code> failing at runtime by improving worker tool configurations.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a fatal nil pointer crash in the agent runtime caused by background summary updates attempting to read cached steps after trajectory closure.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Hardened Remote Control reverse-tunnel routing.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.1.24"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 astro-oabwef3t">September 2, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">MCP panel navigation improvements, multi-line comment support in mcp_config.json, and headless stream cleanup</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Improves <code>/mcp</code> panel arrow key navigation and action cycling, adds support for comments and trailing commas in <code>mcp_config.json</code>, ensures headless CLI streams close cleanly on exit, and delivers fixes for duplicate agent listings, deleted working directory startup, and <code>/btw</code> side questions with active goals.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.24" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(1)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round"
