astro-oabwef3t">Improved <code>/mcp</code> panel navigation so up and down arrow keys strictly navigate between MCP servers and plugins, left and right arrow keys cycle available actions, and <code>Esc</code> exits action mode or the panel.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.24" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(6)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed duplicate agent entries appearing in the <code>/agents</code> picker when an agent with the same name is discovered across multiple configuration sources or plugins.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed tool initialization and agent startup failing when launched from an inaccessible or deleted working directory by using absolute URI schemes for in-memory parameter schemas.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed headless CLI invocations with piped standard output or standard error hanging on exit by setting <code>FD_CLOEXEC</code> on the preserved streams so child processes do not keep the caller's pipes open.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed MCP configuration parsing failing when <code>mcp_config.json</code> contains single-line (<code>//</code>) comments, multi-line (<code>/<em> </em>/</code>) comments, or trailing commas.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed orphaned annotation files accumulating in local storage when deleting conversations.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>/btw</code> side questions in conversations with an active <code>/goal</code> being forced to continue and making unintended tool calls.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.1.23"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.1.23">v1.1.23</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 1, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Model autocompletion improvements, subagent streaming optimizations, and MCP tool resolution fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Improves <code>/model \<name\></code> autocompletion with Tab acceptance, reduces subagent streaming telemetry overhead, and delivers stability fixes for prompt hooks, MCP tools in subagents, tool call IDs in request history, and OAuth token refreshes.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.23" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(2)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved <code>/model \<name\></code> autocompletion to accept the proposed model name ghost text using <code>Tab</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Reduced subagent streaming overhead by sending subagent trajectory metadata once per subtrajectory instead of with every step.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.23" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(11)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed commands with subcommands (such as <code>models</code> or <code>agents</code>) hanging on inherited standard input pipes.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed CLI crashes caused by prompt hooks by catching hook panics and rejecting nil completion configurations in model requests.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed tool invocations and results omitting tool-call IDs when reconstructing request history for Gemini models.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed tool permission prompts for native and MCP tool calls displaying generic titles instead of human-readable action descriptions.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed unconfigured or interrupted Google Cloud authentication dropping into broken chat sessions instead of reprompting the sign-in screen.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed transient authentication errors from clock skew by proactively refreshing browser and WIF OAuth tokens 5 minutes before expiration.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed instructional cycle mode placeholders reappearing in the input prompt after typing and clearing content.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed subagents defined with <code>enable_mcp_tools=true</code> failing with unknown tool errors by ensuring the MCP dispatcher is available to the subagent executor.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed prompts submitted immediately after login being discarded by queueing them until background account verification finishes.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed cancelled or killed subagents remaining stuck in the "Running" state in the UI task drawer across ancestor conversations.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed parsing MCP JSON configuration files on Windows when saved with a UTF-8 byte order mark (BOM).</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.1.22"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.1.22">v1.1.22</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">August 27, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Model selection command arguments, reasoning effort autocompletion, transient retry handling, and UI performance fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Adds direct <code>/model \<name\></code> switching with inline ghost text autocompletion, dynamic <code>/effort</code> hint completion, artifact filesystem event coalescing, and fixes for transient HTTP 502 retries, CPU redraw utilization, and Windows path resolution.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.22" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(3)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added a <code>/model \<name\></code> argument to switch models by name, slug, or label and set defaults in one step with inline ghost text autocompletion.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the <code>/effort</code> hint to complete typed text dynamically instead of displaying a static placeholder.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved artifact handling in sessions generating many files by coalescing bursts of filesystem events into a single rescan.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.22" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(8)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed selectable reasoning effort configuration for Gemini 3.1 Pro and Gemini 3.5 Flash when authenticating using Gemini API keys.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed continuous interface redraws when the tasks panel or subagent detail panel was open with no active tasks, significantly reducing idle CPU usage.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed running subagent elapsed timers freezing on screen while the parent agent was in a waiting state.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed transient HTTP 502 Bad Gateway errors terminating runs by adding automatic retry handling with backoff.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>self</code> subagents launched from unconfigured sessions re-resolving configurations from scratch and drifting from parent autonomous execution modes.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed Windows file deletion sharing violations by retrying deletions with backoff before failing.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed headless daemon startup banner printing unsupported localhost browser URLs to service manager logs.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the built-in <code>migrate-workflows</code> skill to properly handle Windows path separators and home directories.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.1.21"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.1.21">v1.1.21</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">August 26, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Voice dictation, embedded ripgrep code search, status line cost metrics, and UTF-8 encoding fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Adds <code>/voice</code> dictation with <code>mic-serve</code> SSH forwarding, embedded ripgrep for consistent agent code search, custom status line cost tracking, granular script runner permission suggestions, and UTF-8 file editing and MCP error fixes.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.21" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(7)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added <code>/voice</code> dictation to transcribe speech directly into the prompt with <code>f5</code> and <code>/voice</code> toggle shortcuts, introduced the <code>mic-serve</code> subcommand to forward a local microphone over SSH, and improved quota messaging for concurrent stream limits.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added a <code>cost</code> field to the status line data model, exposing unrounded estimated session costs to track running token spend.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved agent code search by bundling an embedded <code>ripgrep</code> binary instead of invoking external system binaries, increasing search performance and cross-platform consistency.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the <code>always-proceed</code> permission mode to auto-approve MCP tool calls and page reads without prompting.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved allow-always permission suggestions for script runners (<code>npm run</code>, <code>yarn</code>, <code>pnpm</code>, <code>cargo run</code>) by scoping approvals to specific script names.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved conversation titles by generating them automatically on creation, providing meaningful session names in the resume picker.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved error diagnostics when an MCP server configured for Google credentials cannot locate Application Default Credentials (ADC).</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.21" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(4)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed corrupted file edits in documents containing non-ASCII text (CJK characters, accented letters, emoji) caused by inexact offset matches producing invalid UTF-8.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed sessions stalling mid-response when a tool result or file diff contained invalid UTF-8.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed file writes being reported as failures after successfully saving to disk, preventing unnecessary agent retry loops on large files and notebooks.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed precedence resolution so explicitly configured skill and plugin paths take priority over automatically discovered customizations during name collisions.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.1.20"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">August 25, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Skill icon branding, empty directory indexing in @-completion, and headless exit code fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Adds skill icon branding with Unicode alignment in catalogs and slash commands, indexes empty directories in <code>@</code> path completion, auto-approves workspace-scoped read access in review mode, and fixes headless print mode error handling.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.20" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(4)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added skill icon and visual branding support across the CLI, displaying emoji icons declared under <code>metadata.icon</code> in <code>SKILL.md</code> frontmatter across the <code>/skills</code> catalog list view, detail inspection headers, and slash command autocompletion popups with multi-byte Unicode display width calculation.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved <code>@</code> file path autocompletion by indexing empty directories alongside files in ripgrep search results, enabling directory-only and unpopulated structures to be traversed.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved permission management by automatically granting workspace-scoped read access under the default review mode, removing repetitive approval prompts for reading or listing workspace files while strictly maintaining confirmation for modifications and external access.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved Git repository inspection performance by skipping recursive submodule worktree scans while continuing to track commit pointer updates.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.20" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed print mode (<code>-p</code>/<code>--print</code>, including <code>--output-format json</code> and <code>stream-json</code>) treating benign tool execution errors and permission denials as fatal run failures with non-zero exit codes.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the CLI overwriting and discarding unparsed configuration in <code>settings.json</code> when encountering an unrecognized setting value or syntax error on startup.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>/skills</code>, <code>/plugins</code>, <code>/agents</code>, and <code>/hooks</code> commands reporting that no customizations were found when invoked without an explicit agent configuration by mounting built-in customizations.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed CJK draft jitter in the main prompt by normalizing boundary whitespace in streaming transcript updates.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the conversation spinner animation loop continuing to tick in the background after turn completion, eliminating idle CPU wakeups.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.1.19"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a astro-oabwef3t"><p>Introduces automatic free port selection for <code>--remote-control</code>, environment controls to suppress logo art for narrow terminals and screen readers, and renderer bypass aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added the astro-oabwef3t">Added <code>AGY_CLI_DISABLE_ESCAPE_SEQUENCE_OPTIMIZATIONS</code> to bypass the renderer's dirty-rectangle and diffing optimizations.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.19" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>--remote-control</code> failing when a default port was occupied by automatically binding to an available port provided by the operating system.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.1.18"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.1.18">v1.1.18</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">August 22, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Project name flags, customizable picker keybindings, typo-tolerant @-completion, and audio attachments</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Adds support for passing project names to <code>--project</code>, customizable keybindings for conversation management, typo-tolerant <code>@</code> file completion, and standard audio attachments alongside reliability and terminal layout fixes.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.18" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(6)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added support for passing a project name to <code>--project</code>, which previously accepted only a project ID and failed on anything else.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added <code>item.rename</code> and <code>item.delete</code> keybindings for the conversation picker's rename and delete actions, allowing <code>F2</code> and <code>F4</code> to be rebound in <code>keybindings.json</code> on keyboards without function keys.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved <code>@</code> file path completion with a typo-tolerant fallback, so a query with a transposed or mistyped character still finds the file without outranking exact matches.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved audio attachments by recognizing standard formats Gemini models accept, including <code>.wav</code>, <code>.mp3</code>, <code>.m4a</code>, <code>.aac</code>, <code>.flac</code>, and <code>.opus</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved keystroke responsiveness on Windows by discarding key-release events reported by the console, cutting rendering work per keystroke.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the artifact viewer footer by placing the outline shortcut next to scroll and page hints for clearer document navigation.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.18" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(6)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed print mode (<code>-p</code>) exiting successfully with an empty response when the agent state stream dropped mid-run; it now surfaces the stream error and exits non-zero.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a valueless prompt flag swallowing the next flag as its prompt (such as <code>--print --sandbox "do the task"</code>), turning both that and stray trailing arguments into explicit errors.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed expanded <code>/btw</code> cards pushing footer hints and prompt off-screen on long answers by clamping output to terminal height with scroll support.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>/resume</code> opening below the fold instead of the active workspace when running the CLI from a subdirectory.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed text losing styling after file links where the link's styling reset also cleared surrounding prose formatting.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed stray characters appearing in the prompt every few seconds by restricting periodic input-mode re-arming to terminal multiplexers.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.1.17"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.1.17">v1.1.17</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">August 20, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Consolidated agent execution harness and media attachment fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Consolidated agent execution harness for consistent tool, hook, and prompt behavior, alongside fixes for slash command visibility, Vim insert mode task navigation, and Ogg audio/video attachment MIME types.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.17" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(1)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the agent execution harness by consolidating onto a single execution path, giving more consistent tool, hook, and prompt behavior.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.17" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(3)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>/teamwork-preview</code> and some other slash commands disappearing for some users.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>Enter</code> not opening an active background task or subagent while the prompt was in Vim insert mode.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed attaching Ogg audio and video files such as <code>.ogg</code>, <code>.opus</code> and <code>.ogv</code>, which the model rejected because they were sent as the generic <code>application/ogg</code>.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.1.16"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.1.16">v1.1.16</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">August 20, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">CLI mcp management commands, debounced @ path completion, and MCP resource handling fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p><code>mcp</code> management subcommands (<code>add</code>, <code>remove</code>, <code>list</code>, <code>enable</code>, <code>disable</code>) for <code>mcp_config.json</code>, debounced <code>@</code> file path completion powered by bundled <code>ripgrep</code>, Gemini API key reasoning effort adjustment for Gemini Flash models, and comprehensive fixes for binary MCP resource offloading, Workforce Identity Federation token refresh, <code>/mcp</code> configuration preservation, Kitty keyboard protocol handling, <code>/btw</code> with custom agents, and finished task status tracking.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.16" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(3)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added <code>mcp</code> subcommands (<code>add</code>, <code>remove</code>, <code>list</code>, <code>enable</code>, <code>disable</code>) for managing MCP servers in your user-level <code>mcp_config.json</code> without hand-editing it, covering both stdio and HTTP servers through <code>--type</code>, <code>--env</code> and <code>--header</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved <code>@</code> file path completion in large workspaces by running the lookup through the bundled <code>ripgrep</code> and debouncing keystrokes, and by ranking a file whose name matches your query above a directory or generated artifact that merely contains it.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved <code>/effort</code> so it adjusts reasoning effort for Gemini 3.6 Flash and Gemini 3.7 Flash when you sign in with a Gemini API key, a route that previously reported those models as not adjustable even though the same models were adjustable on every other sign-in path.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.16" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(15)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed links printing as raw escape sequences on terminals that do not implement OSC 8 hyperlinks, such as Terminal.app, and extended the same detection to command output and alert bodies, which still emitted hyperlinks unconditionally.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a <code>=</code> character accumulating in the prompt every couple of seconds on terminals that do not implement the Kitty keyboard protocol, where the renderer's periodic re-arming of that protocol was printed as literal text instead of being interpreted.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>@</code> file path completion showing files that were in your <code>.antigravityignore</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the artifact list showing a percent-escaped filename such as <code>quarterly%20plan.md</code> for artifacts whose name contains a space, so the list row, the inline preview and the detail header all spell the name the same way.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the prompt editor's cursor becoming misaligned when text wrapped.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the <code>/mcp</code> panel dropping <code>enabledTools</code>, <code>timeoutSeconds</code>, <code>url</code> and <code>tools.eager</code> from <code>mcp_config.json</code> when you toggled a server on or off; it now preserves any field it does not recognize, so configuration written by a newer client survives an edit.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>read_resource</code> discarding non-image binary content returned by an MCP server, which now offloads every blob to disk and inlines only small text and image resources, so large PDFs, audio and other binary resources are usable instead of silently dropped.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed Workforce Identity Federation sign-in signing you out roughly every hour, because the token refresh went to the standard sign-in endpoint rather than the federated one.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed skills that ship with the CLI being reported as not built-in, so the <code>builtin</code> flag in the <code>/skills</code> listing under <code>--output-format json</code> is accurate and the <code>/skills</code> panel groups them correctly.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the reasoning-effort description under the timeline gauge in the <code>/effort</code> and <code>/model</code> pickers overflowing on narrow terminals, where it is now omitted so the gauge stays readable.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>/btw</code> failing with a planner configuration error in conversations driven by a custom or SDK-defined agent, so side questions work regardless of which agent you are running.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a failed <code>/btw</code> side question always reporting <code>model returned an empty response</code>, which was a hardcoded guess rather than the real failure; the card now shows the side question's own error when there is one.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the status line, the active-items list and the <code>/tasks</code> panel continuing to show background tasks that had already finished.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the CLI overwriting a <code>settings.json</code> it could not parse with default settings, which silently reverted every setting the next time anything was saved; a refused save now leaves the file byte-identical so you can repair it by hand, and the status line names the file.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a subagent whose definition leaves <code>model</code> at <code>inherit</code> failing to start when the parent agent has no model of its own, which now falls back to the default fast tier.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.1.15"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.1.15">v1.1.15</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">August 19, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Stream-JSON input mode, markdown agent explicit rules, and startup credential fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Stream-JSON print input mode for persistent session drivers, direct rule file configuration in markdown agent frontmatter, plugin rule support using <code>rules.json</code>, current tool call hints on the spinner, and fixes for allocator preloading on Cloud TPU VMs, keyring credential restoration, terminal scrolling and unicode display in the model picker, and artifact subdirectories.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.15" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(4)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added <code>--input-format stream-json</code> to print mode, which reads newline-delimited JSON prompts from stdin and runs one turn per message in a single conversation, so a driver can keep a session open.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added a <code>rules:</code> key to markdown agent frontmatter, so an agent can name rule files directly instead of inheriting your whole rule tree; a rule named this way always applies.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added plugin support for a top-level <code>rules.json</code>, so a plugin can declare the rule files it ships the same way it already declares its skills.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added a more specific hint on the spinner line, which now names the tool call the agent is currently running.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.15" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(10)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the CLI aborting at startup on hosts that preload their own allocator through <code>LD_PRELOAD</code>, such as Cloud TPU VM images, where it died before the interface appeared.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed personal accounts hitting a resource-exhausted error at startup when saved credentials were restored from the system keyring, and cut the repeated account checks that followed.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed your billing project, and the license tier it selects, being lost when the CLI restarted and restored saved credentials from the system keyring.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a spurious "Out of credits" message for enterprise users signed in through Application Default Credentials.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the <code>/model</code> picker cutting off models, and its own header, on terminals too short to show every model; the list now scrolls.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the <code>/model</code> picker misaligning its columns for model names that are not plain ASCII, by measuring column widths in display cells rather than bytes.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the spinner line flickering to a generic label between quick tool calls.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed streamed text corrupting non-ASCII characters into replacement characters, in both the interactive display and <code>--output-format stream-json</code> text deltas.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed artifacts written into a subdirectory of a conversation's artifact directory, such as <code>scratch/</code>, never appearing in the artifact list.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed reading a <code>.wav</code> file failing as an unsupported media type, by normalizing non-canonical MIME types such as <code>audio/wave</code> before they reach the model.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.1.14"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.1.14">v1.1.14</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">August 18, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Enterprise sign-in fast path, MCP OAuth metadata documents, /context scrolling, and markdown agent customizations</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Fast return path for enterprise sign-in, support for OAuth client ID metadata documents in MCP servers, terminal-sized scrollable <code>/context</code> and <code>/usage</code> panels, unified <code>inheritCustomizations</code> configuration for markdown agents, read-only permissions outside the workspace, and fixes for language server crashes, hyperlink rendering, artifact approval tracking, MCP error isolation, and bracketed paste.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.14" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(5)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added a faster return path for enterprise sign-in, offering returning users a single <code>Continue with ...</code> option naming the authentication method they last used.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added support for OAuth client ID metadata documents when connecting to an MCP server, allowing servers implementing this specification to connect without requiring a manually supplied client ID or dynamic client registration.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the <code>/context</code> panel, making it scrollable and dynamically sized to the terminal, with <code>/usage</code> sized the same way.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved markdown-defined agents with a single <code>inheritCustomizations</code> switch that controls whether an agent adopts skills, rules, plugins, subagents, and MCP servers, replacing inconsistent per-kind defaults.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved workspace boundary settings: accessing paths outside the workspace now grants read access only, with writes requiring authorization based on the active execution mode.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.14" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(9)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the CLI exiting silently when its language server failed at startup or during execution; failures are now logged to the terminal and exit with a non-zero status.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed sign-in URLs and hyperlinks rendering as garbled escape sequences on terminals that do not support OSC 8 hyperlinks (such as GNU <code>screen</code>), detecting unsupported terminals and rendering plain text instead.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>Enter</code> keystrokes being swallowed while the path-suggestion popup was loading, which previously sent the prompt and popped results open above it.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the artifact list marking previously unreviewed, commented, or rejected artifacts as approved when reopening the list.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed malformed MCP server configurations causing all other servers to fail; invalid entries are now logged and skipped while remaining servers continue loading.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed built-in skills, rules, and plugins being stripped when an agent configured <code>inherit_user: false</code>, ensuring built-in capabilities remain available while personal customizations are excluded.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed conversation branching and rollbacks failing with <code>too many SQL variables</code> on long trajectories.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed conversation titles unexpectedly jumping to older messages after trajectory truncation by protecting the title checkpoint.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed multi-line pastes submitting individual prompts per line on terminals that do not support bracketed paste.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.1.13"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.1.13">v1.1.13</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">August 14, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">GEMINI_API_KEY authentication, custom agent task management, embedded ripgrep caching, and conversation database fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Direct <code>GEMINI_API_KEY</code> authentication support, <code>/codesearch</code> fallback resilience, cached artifact list scrolling, <code>manage_task</code> access for custom declarative agents, embedded ripgrep caching with SHA-256 verification, and critical fixes for trajectory truncation, database growth, transcript race conditions, and subagent path validation.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.13" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(10)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added support for <code>GEMINI_API_KEY</code>, allowing the CLI to run directly against the Gemini API without signing in. Set <code>modelProvider: "gemini"</code> in <code>settings.json</code>, export <code>GEMINI_API_KEY</code>, and optionally configure <code>GOOGLE_GEMINI_BASE_URL</code> for custom endpoints. The banner and <code>/help</code> show <code>Gemini API key</code> as the credential, and <code>/logout</code> explains that it originates from the environment.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved <code>/codesearch</code> resilience by falling back to a local search when the <code>ripgrep</code> binary cannot be executed, such as when blocked by endpoint security software.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved artifact list scrolling performance by caching each file's preview line count instead of re-reading every listed file from disk on every keypress.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved custom agent support by making <code>manage_task</code> available to declarative agents that have not opted out of fundamental components, enabling custom agents to list and kill background tasks, with clearer step descriptions like <code>Killed task X</code> and <code>Checked task X</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved <code>search_web</code> step visualization by displaying the query while the search is in progress rather than only after completion.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved agent behavior after backgrounding tasks by removing anti-polling reminder text from <code>manage_task</code> and <code>manage_inbox</code> tool responses to prevent unintended polling loops.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the <code>/resume</code> picker by clearly relabeling its second tab.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved deleting conversations in the <code>/resume</code> picker by remapping the shortcut from <code>ctrl+delete</code> to <code>f4</code> for reliable cross-platform terminal compatibility on macOS.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the load time when opening large conversations by fetching step headers in batches without reading each step's full payload.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved embedded <code>ripgrep</code> reliability and security by extracting binaries into the user cache directory instead of <code>/tmp</code>, adding SHA-256 integrity verification, and using atomic renaming to prevent concurrent execution races.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.13" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(11)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed distorted startup banner rendering in Cloud Shell by automatically enabling a conservative rendering mode for web terminals.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed direct Gemini API mode getting stuck on the sign-in screen instead of launching into the main UI, and resolved failures with custom <code>GOOGLE_GEMINI_BASE_URL</code> endpoints by omitting unsupported session fields.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed conversations disappearing from the <code>/resume</code> picker after switching tabs, and preserved the <code>tab</code> hint in the shortcut bar even when a tab is empty.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed trajectory truncation over-pruning conversation history by charging the size budget solely against reclaimable steps rather than protected checkpoints or already-cleared residues.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed unbounded growth of the SQLite conversation database during background task and subagent wakeups caused by duplicate grants and settings being appended to persisted rows.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed transcript corruption caused by background messages appending while context compaction was rewriting the log.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where a hung sandbox initialization prevented subsequent messages from being processed.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed path traversal vulnerability in <code>define_subagent</code> by strictly validating model-supplied agent names before creating directories.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed artifact list rendering bugs involving wide CJK characters and emoji, boundary handling when entries disappear, and viewport scrolling overflow.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>Esc</code> key in the artifact viewer triggering duplicate review submission confirmations for previously completed reviews.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed missing tool call line rendering in collapsed/expanded tool groups for <code>invoke_subagent</code>, <code>send_message</code>, <code>manage_subagents</code>, <code>define_subagent</code>, and <code>generate_image</code>.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.1.12"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.1.12">v1.1.12</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">August 11, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Artifact heading outline, print-mode commands, artifact alerts/carousels, improved terminal hyperlinks, and extensive CLI fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Artifact outline viewer navigation, print-mode read-only slash commands, machine-readable output for <code>models</code> and <code>agents</code>, alert callout and carousel rendering in markdown artifacts, terminal hyperlink improvements, and fixes for headless <code>--mode</code>, startup diagnostics, subagent messaging, and quota reporting.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.12" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(13)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added a heading outline to the artifact viewer, opened with <code>t</code>, so a long markdown document's structure is visible at a glance and you can jump straight to a section instead of scrolling.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added non-interactive answers for more read-only slash commands in print mode, so <code>-p "/permissions"</code>, <code>/hooks</code>, <code>/help</code>, <code>/changelog</code> and <code>/config</code> each emit one tab-separated record per line — or a structured payload under <code>--output-format json</code> and <code>stream-json</code> — without starting an agent turn, spending quota or leaving a conversation behind, with <code>/help</code> listing exactly the commands print mode answers and <code>/changelog</code> printing the release notes.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added machine-readable output to the <code>models</code> and <code>agents</code> subcommands through an <code>--output-format</code> flag accepting <code>json</code> and <code>stream-json</code>, and moved their error messages and progress spinner off stdout so captured output contains only the list.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added a <code>disable-slash-command: true</code> flag for a skill's <code>SKILL.md</code> frontmatter, which hides that skill from the <code>/</code> menu and from <code>/name</code> resolution while leaving it discoverable and invocable by the model, so a large skill library no longer floods the command menu.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added rendering for alert callouts (<code>[!NOTE]</code>, <code>[!TIP]</code>, <code>[!IMPORTANT]</code>, <code>[!WARNING]</code>, <code>[!CAUTION]</code>) and for carousel blocks in markdown artifacts, which previously showed as raw markup.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved terminal hyperlink support, enabling clickable links over SSH and in hyperlink-capable <code>tmux</code>, keeping word-wrapped URLs clickable past their first line, linking URLs that the markdown renderer had left as plain text including ones inside code blocks, no longer highlighting unrelated links together on hover, and fixing horizontal scrolling and text selection on lines that contain a link.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved tool call headers so every native and MCP tool call carries a short summary of what it is doing instead of a bare name or a raw argument blob, with the same summary naming background tasks and subagents in the activity list and the matching action phrase describing the request in permission prompts.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved headroom for large toolsets by raising the per-session limit on tool declarations, so heavy MCP, plugin and skill setups stop being rejected for having too many tools.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved sign-in with Application Default Credentials by resolving the quota project automatically, so service-account logins work without extra configuration.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved repository detection so a Git repository nested inside a larger multi-repository checkout resolves to the intended root, with submodules and worktrees covered.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved headless <code>-p</code> runs so the agent settles a choice itself where it would otherwise ask, instead of stalling on a question nobody is there to answer.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the <code>schedule</code> tool by dropping the redundant <code>Timer Cancelled</code> notification when a one-shot timer with an early-termination condition is cancelled, and by naming both the condition and the exact sender whose message satisfied it in the step result.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved file paths in tool step lines by collapsing your home directory to <code>~</code> instead of truncating the front of the path, so the interesting end of a long path stays on screen.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.12" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(13)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>--mode</code> being ignored in headless <code>-p</code> runs, where a valid value such as <code>accept-edits</code> or <code>plan</code> was never applied and an unrecognized value produced no warning at all.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed startup diagnostics being swallowed into the log file instead of reaching the terminal, including crash notices, the <code>--conversation</code> not-found warning, the <code>--print</code> and <code>--prompt-interactive</code> conflict, conversation load errors, and the <code>--mode</code> and <code>--agent</code> warnings.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a character disappearing from your submitted prompt where it wrapped at the terminal's right edge, caused by the echoed prompt being indented after it had already been wrapped to the full width.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed file citations in a response losing their line numbers, so a cited range renders as <code>path:10-25</code> again instead of just the file name.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a write-in answer in a multi-select question discarding the boxes you had already ticked, and fixed a stale write-in being submitted after you went back to a predefined option.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed large background tasks crowding out the prompt by limiting each active item below the input box to a single line.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed corruption of <code>config.json</code> by writing user config atomically, so a crash or a concurrent writer can no longer leave a truncated file that silently breaks settings persistence.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the CLI giving up on a slow OS keyring after one second and falling back to empty storage, which forced a re-login; it now waits five seconds, as every other keyring operation already did.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed conversation preview titles derived from the first user message by cutting them at the first line and capping them at 500 characters, so a large pasted or scripted prompt no longer produces a multi-megabyte stored title.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a crash on Windows when resolving the conversation transcript path, by honoring the path's drive letter in the trajectory log artifact converter.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a subagent going silent on its parent when its own message failed to send, and fixed the parent being handed an empty notice when a subagent went idle without a final response.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the <code>read_url_content</code> tool leaking a connection on every call, which could eventually exhaust the machine's available ports.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the status line reporting model quota that was always one fetch out of date, so it now matches the quota you actually have left.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.1.11"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.1.11">v1.1.11</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">August 7, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Vim modal editing mode, Vim-aware submission, print-mode slash-command enhancements, and various CLI and permission fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Vim modal editing mode with Normal, Insert, Visual, and Visual Line modes, Vim-aware prompt submission, Vim editing in comment/diff views, print-mode slash-command and non-interactive enhancements, plugin configuration improvements, and fixes for command auto-approval, MCP servers, and autocomplete history.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.11" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(9)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added a Vim editing mode, off by default and switched on from <code>/settings</code> under <code>Editor Mode</code>, bringing modal editing to the prompt with Normal, Insert, Visual and Visual Line modes, a mode badge in the status line and a Vim tab in <code>/help</code>, and covering the <code>h</code>/<code>j</code>/<code>k</code>/<code>l</code>, <code>w</code>/<code>b</code>/<code>e</code> and <code>W</code>/<code>B</code>/<code>E</code> motions, the <code>0</code>/<code>^</code>/<code>$</code>/<code>gg</code>/<code>G</code> boundaries, the <code>f</code>/<code>F</code>/<code>t</code>/<code>T</code> character searches with their <code>;</code> and <code>,</code> repeats, the <code>i</code>/<code>a</code>/<code>I</code>/<code>A</code> insert entries, the <code>d</code>/<code>c</code>/<code>y</code> operators alongside <code>x</code>, <code>D</code> and <code>C</code> filling the unnamed register for <code>p</code>, and the <code>iw</code>/<code>aw</code>/<code>iW</code>/<code>aW</code>/<code>ip</code>/<code>ap</code> text objects.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added Vim-aware submission so a prompt can be sent without leaving modal editing, through an <code>Editor Mode > Insert First</code> toggle that opens the prompt in Insert mode where a bare <code>Enter</code> submits and a modified key such as <code>shift+enter</code> or <code>ctrl+j</code> inserts a newline, <code>ctrl+s</code> and <code>ctrl+enter</code> keys that submit from Normal mode, an <code>Enter</code> binding available as <code>vim.insert.submit</code>, and the full set of <code>vim.*</code> scopes in <code>keybindings.json</code> for remapping any of it.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added Vim editing to the comment editors in the diff and artifact-detail views, whose footer hints follow the active mode rather than showing one fixed set of bindings, and made a collapsed block behave as a single unit under Vim motions.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added non-interactive answers for the read-only slash commands in print mode, so <code>-p "/usage"</code>, <code>/quota</code>, <code>/credits</code>, <code>/model</code>, <code>/effort</code> and <code>/skills</code> emit one tab-separated record per line — or a structured payload under <code>--output-format json</code> and <code>stream-json</code> — without starting an agent turn, spending quota, or leaving a conversation behind.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added an explicit refusal for the remaining interactive-only slash commands in print mode, which previously fell through as literal prompt text and let the model answer as though the command had run, so <code>-p "/clear"</code> reported the context cleared while nothing was cleared; each now fails with the flag or subcommand that replaces it.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved plugin enable and disable so <code>config.json</code> is the only place enablement lives, seeded once from each plugin's manifest, which stops a plugin that later ships <code>"disabled": true</code> from switching itself off under someone who was already running it and stops a shipped-default change from moving every user on the next release.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the artifact detail view by wrapping long lines in code files instead of clipping them at the right edge.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved model retries by honoring the server-supplied retry delay instead of the client's own backoff, so a retry after a rate limit or an overload waits exactly as long as the server asks.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved model-loading errors so a permission failure such as a missing license or a missing IAM role is shown as itself, with a troubleshooting link, instead of a generic failure during the run.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.11" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(9)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an allowlist entry that tokenizes to zero command words — <code>command(time)</code>, a comment-only entry, or an empty compound such as <code>()</code> — matching every command and silently auto-approving anything the agent ran; such an entry now matches nothing.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed commands being auto-approved while the session was in request-review or strict permission mode.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed admin controls being skipped for MCP servers at startup, where a fetch made before authentication cached "admin controls not applicable" and allowed every server for the next five minutes, and fixed the built-in Chrome DevTools MCP server being blocked outright by admin controls.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed MCP progress callbacks being dropped and the task log file not being initialized, so long-running MCP tool calls report progress again.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a slash command receiving the collapsed <code>[Pasted text #N]</code> placeholder instead of the pasted content when its argument was pasted into the prompt.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the prompt saving the typed prefix rather than the completed command name to history when an autocompleted slash command was submitted, so up-arrow recall replays what actually ran, and made an alias require an exact match before auto-executing so a partial alias fills the prompt instead of running a command you did not finish typing.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an asynchronous settings refresh re-enabling the feedback survey partway through a print-mode run that had switched it off.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed spurious "Out of credits" errors, where an empty credits response was read as a balance of zero.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the "Use AI Credits" setting being offered to accounts signed in through a Google Cloud project or application default credentials, where it does not apply.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.1.10"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.1.10">v1.1.10</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">August 3, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Gemini Enterprise & WIF authentication, read-only .git sandbox rules, hook ordering improvements, and subagent and CLI fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Gemini Enterprise Business sign-in, Workforce Identity Federation (WIF) and Application Default Credentials (ADC) auth support, read-only <code>.git</code> sandbox access, hook ordering improvements, and fixes for subagent tree termination, <code>--model</code>/<code>--effort</code> flag resolution, and multi-select spacebar toggling.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.10" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(7)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added Business sign-in for Gemini Enterprise accounts, so you can authenticate with a Google Cloud project under Google Cloud terms, use a license seat allocated or auto-assigned from your organization's GE-Standard or GE-Plus subscription, run inference in a chosen region, and have your organization's administrator controls applied to various features.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added Workforce Identity Federation sign-in for enterprise users, available as the <code>Use advanced SSO config</code> option on the Google Cloud sign-in screen, so organizations that federate identity through an external provider can authenticate with their own identity provider.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added sign-in with Application Default Credentials so you can use Agent Platform.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added a non-blocking advisory banner when the same conversation is already open in another CLI instance on the same machine, pointing at <code>/fork</code> so two sessions no longer interleave writes into one trajectory.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the terminal sandbox by granting read-only rather than writable access to a Git repository's <code>.git</code> directory, so the agent can inspect repository metadata without being able to rewrite it from inside the sandbox.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved hook ordering so hooks defined in <code>hooks.json</code> run before the built-in termination checks, which lets <code>PostInvocation</code> hooks observe the final invocation of a turn and lets <code>Stop</code> hooks run at all instead of sitting unreachable behind the built-ins.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the <code>schedule</code> tool to accept <code>DurationSeconds</code> and <code>MaxIterations</code> when a model emits them as bare JSON numbers rather than strings, accepting integral values and rejecting non-integral ones with a clear error instead of failing the call.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.10" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(11)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>--model</code> and <code>--effort</code> being ignored in interactive sessions and in headless <code>-p</code> runs, where the flags were applied after model configuration had already been initialized so the run silently fell back to the persisted or default model.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a bare <code>--effort</code> resolving against the default model instead of the model you actually have selected, which could silently move you to a different model.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed stopping a subagent tree stopping only the conversation it was invoked from, while every descendant subagent and the background tasks they owned kept running and the CLI still reported them as killed.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a forced-continuation deadlock where a coordinator waiting on active subagents or background tasks would loop injecting empty continue steps until it hit the invocation limit, wasting tokens and blocking progress.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the spacebar not toggling an option in multi-select prompts, including the <code>ask_question</code> dialog and the onboarding import checkbox, which left <code>x</code> as the only working toggle; the hint bar now advertises it.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the Left and Right arrow keys being captured to navigate the input box suggestion dropdown, so you can move the cursor and edit text again while suggestions are showing.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the model picker's "No models available" state rendering without its header and footer, so it now shows the standard chrome and an <code>esc</code> hint to go back.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed tools that an MCP server marks to always run in the background executing as blocking calls that stalled the turn.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an MCP process leak when a server connection dropped unexpectedly.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the artifact viewer corrupting plain documents by horizontally clipping every document rather than only the diagram artifacts that need it.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the sandbox not recording blocked network requests when the command itself exited successfully, which hid the fact that a request had been denied.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.1.9"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.1.9">v1.1.9</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">July 31, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Slash-command expansion in print mode, non-blocking MCP startup, session-wide permission memory, system temp directory write grants, and hook and UI stability fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Added slash-command and skill expansion in print mode, non-blocking background MCP loading during interactive startup, session-scoped permission pattern recording, system temporary directory write grants, and stability fixes for stop/PostToolUse hooks, artifact viewer, and MCP authentication.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.9" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(4)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added slash-command and skill expansion to print mode, so a headless run such as <code>-p "/my-skill review this diff"</code> now resolves and applies the skill instead of sending it as literal text, with <code>--disable-slash-commands</code> to opt out.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved interactive startup so a slow or hanging MCP server no longer stalls the first agent turn, loading MCP servers in the background for the interactive session while headless and one-shot runs keep blocking so their single scripted turn still sees the full toolset.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved permission grants so a pattern approved at a prompt is recorded for the rest of the conversation, letting later commands that match it run without prompting again.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the default system temporary-directory grant to cover writes as well as reads, so agents no longer trigger a permission prompt when creating or updating files there.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.9" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(7)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed stop hooks that always block hanging the agent forever; after a configurable number of consecutive continuations, the hook can no longer block and the turn ends normally.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>PostToolUse</code> hooks firing on non-tool steps such as user input and model responses, which also caused them to ignore their configured matchers.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed slash commands not being recognized when separated from their arguments by a newline or tab, so a prompt starting with a command followed by a newline is now parsed as a command instead of being sent
