verbatim.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed deleting into a collapsed paste placeholder removing one character at a time, which left a visible fragment in the prompt while the full pasted content was still submitted; the block is now deleted atomically.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the artifact viewer losing syntax highlighting when returning from the editor view, and returning to the wrong panel when exiting the artifact detail view.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the headless <code>stream-json</code> <code>init</code> event advertising tools that are not available in your build.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed MCP servers forcing a full re-authentication after a dropped connection.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.1.8"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.1.8">v1.1.8</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">July 28, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Print mode structured output formats, custom JSON schema enforcement, copyOnSelect TUI setting, and compound-command permission improvements</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Added structured output formats (<code>json</code>, <code>stream-json</code>) for print mode, support for custom JSON schema validation, enriched tool and subagent payloads, <code>copyOnSelect</code> configuration setting, and improved compound-command permission rules.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.8" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(7)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Print mode (<code>-p</code> / <code>--print</code>) now supports structured, machine-readable output using the <code>--output-format</code> flag (<code>text</code> (default), <code>json</code>, or <code>stream-json</code>), so headless runs in CI, eval harnesses, and scripts can consume the CLI's output programmatically; these flags are now discoverable in <code>--help</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added the <code>stream-json</code> output format: a strongly-typed NDJSON event stream that emits typed <code>init</code>, <code>step_update</code>, and terminal <code>result</code> events with a stable, closed-vocabulary <code>step_type</code> discriminator, so consumers receive progress incrementally instead of waiting for the whole run to finish.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added the <code>--json-schema</code> flag to enforce a custom JSON schema on the structured output, accepting either an inline schema string or a path to a schema file; for <code>stream-json</code> the schema applies to the final <code>result</code> event.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Enriched the structured stream with a <code>tool_info</code> object for each tool call (canonical tool name, parameters, and output) and a <code>subagent_info</code> payload for delegated subagents (including <code>conversation_id</code> and <code>log_uri</code>) so consumers can correlate child trajectories.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">The JSON usage object emitted by <code>json</code> and <code>stream-json</code> now reports token accounting including <code>cache_read_tokens</code>, so non-interactive consumers can attribute prompt-cache hits.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added a <code>copyOnSelect</code> setting (default on, toggleable in <code>/settings</code>) that controls whether releasing a mouse text-selection auto-copies it to the system clipboard in the TUI's altscreen rendering mode; disable it to stop the automatic copy on release — useful when the auto-copy is unwanted or corrupts certain payloads.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved compound-command permissions so an exact chained command (such as <code>git fetch && git rebase</code>) can be saved as an allow-always rule and no longer re-prompts on the next identical run.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.1.7"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.1.7">v1.1.7</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">July 24, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Permission prompts for compound commands, and fixes for disabled plugins, MCP OAuth, and clipboard corruption</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Improved permission prompts for compound shell commands, and fixed disabled plugins running hooks, MCP OAuth issuer validation, CJK clipboard copying on Windows, and <code>/btw</code> error on startup.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.7" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(1)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved permission prompts for compound shell commands so the full command is shown when any part of it needs approval.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.7" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(5)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed disabled plugins still running their hooks and contributing other customizations, which could keep a broken hook active and break file-editing tools even after the plugin was turned off.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed MCP OAuth against providers that do not strictly follow the spec (such as Salesforce and Atlassian) by relaxing issuer validation and including the <code>refresh_token</code> grant.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>/btw</code> failing with a "parent conversation not found" error when used as the very first action in a fresh session.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed clipboard corruption of CJK and other non-ASCII text when copying on Windows.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed print mode (<code>-p</code>) sending a prompt before the account-eligibility check finished.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.1.6"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.1.6">v1.1.6</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">July 24, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Markdown format custom agents, /copy index support, progressive /codesearch streaming, default temp directory access, and stability fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Added support for defining custom agents in Markdown format (<code>agent.md</code>), optional index argument for <code>/copy</code>, progressive streaming in <code>/codesearch</code>, default read access to system temporary directory, and resolved various TUI jitter, artifact viewer, and sandbox fixes.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.6" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(7)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added support for defining custom agents using Markdown files (<code>agent.md</code>) with YAML frontmatter and H1-delimited system prompts. Markdown agents support <code>mainAgent</code>, <code>subagent</code>, <code>hidden</code>, <code>inheritMcp</code>, and <code>commandExecutionPolicy</code> frontmatter fields for fine-grained control over agent behavior. Dynamically defined subagents (using <code>define_subagent</code>) now also write Markdown format so they resolve correctly on external builds.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added an optional index argument to <code>/copy</code> so <code>/copy \<n\></code> copies the n-th most recent response to the clipboard, while <code>/copy</code> and <code>/copy 1</code> still copy the latest.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved <code>/codesearch</code> to render results progressively as they stream in, showing a live count while loading and letting you cancel an in-flight search with <code>Esc</code> instead of blocking until the whole search finishes.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved default file access by granting read access to the system temporary directory out of the box, resolved correctly per platform, so agents no longer trigger permission prompts when reading temporary files.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved support for markdown-based custom agents so custom agent management and selection behave more consistently.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved customization discovery by sorting rules and discovered paths deterministically, preventing unstable prompt ordering and needless prompt-cache misses.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved overall reliability and stability across the CLI with additional hardening and fixes for intermittent failures in background tasks, print mode, and interactive flows.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.6" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(11)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed switching from a custom agent back to the default agent using <code>/agents</code>, which previously failed silently and left the conversation stuck on the custom agent's persona.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a crash when a command was blocked by sandbox permissions before its output was captured, and cleaned up the permission approval and denial messages.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the artifact viewer emitting garbage escape bytes when cycling to image mode on terminals that are detected but cannot actually render Kitty graphics, such as iTerm2.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the first keystroke (such as <code>Esc</code>) being dropped when opening the first artifact view on some non-Kitty terminals.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed conversation jitter and a stranded input box during streaming so transient markdown reflow no longer shifts the pending line and input box upward.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed print mode (<code>-p</code>) surfacing the real conversation-creation failure instead of a misleading "no active conversation" error.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the message list dropping its header when rewinding or resetting conversation steps.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a background auto-updater double-spawn race where two processes could each spawn an updater within a single update window.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed sandbox error reporting so blocked actions are recorded even when the network proxy is disabled.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the screen going blank after the authentication page.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the <code>ctrl+b</code> shortcut being hardcoded to background shell commands even when none were running, so a remapped <code>ctrl+b</code> is now respected whenever there are no running shell commands in the conversation.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.1.5"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.1.5">v1.1.5</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">July 21, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Reasoning effort command, model slug pinning, subagent model configuration, and TUI stability improvements</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Added <code>/effort</code> command and <code>--effort</code> flag for reasoning effort selection, stable model slugs, subagent model config, redesigned <code>/model</code> picker, scrollable <code>/settings</code> panel, and various MCP and background task fixes.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.5" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(8)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added a <code>/effort</code> command to view and change the current model's reasoning effort, with a left/right timeline-gauge picker and a direct <code>/effort \<level\></code> form so you can trade latency for depth on the fly.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added an <code>--effort</code> flag to select a model's reasoning-effort variant when launching the CLI.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added stable, user-facing model slugs that appear in the <code>/model</code> picker and are accepted by <code>--model</code>, so you can pin a specific model reliably across sessions.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added a <code>model</code> option to custom agent frontmatter so an agent runs at a chosen model tier (such as <code>flash</code> or <code>pro</code>) when invoked as a subagent, defaulting to <code>inherit</code> (the parent's model).</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Redesigned the <code>/model</code> picker to group models by their base model and choose reasoning effort from a timeline gauge navigable with Left and Right, and added an effort badge to the status line for models that expose multiple effort variants.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the <code>/settings</code> (<code>/config</code>) panel by making it a bounded, scrollable list so it renders correctly in short terminals instead of overflowing, and stopped it from flickering when opening and closing dropdowns.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved background-task reliability by moving long-running work onto a shared lifecycle with deterministic startup and shutdown and panic-safe launching, so a failure in one background task no longer disrupts the session and pending analytics are flushed on exit instead of dropped.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved responsiveness of bursty background refreshes by coalescing rapid repeated triggers into a single run, cutting redundant work.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.5" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(5)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a crash when triggering Authenticate on a remote MCP server in the <code>/mcp</code> panel.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed MCP tool results containing embedded resources being silently dropped, so text and inline media returned by MCP servers now surface in the conversation.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed permission checks splitting a single command into a pipeline when an argument contained quoted shell metacharacters (such as <code>--grep="a|b"</code>), which caused spurious permission prompts.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the file-view and file-search tools failing with invalid-UTF-8 errors when a multi-byte character was split at a truncation boundary.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a data race when collecting customization rules by guarding the shared structures.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.1.4"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.1.4">v1.1.4</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">July 18, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Slash command chaining, diff scrolling fixes, and headless policy enforcement</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Added slash command stacking (chaining multiple commands in a single prompt), diff viewer scroll fixes, headless mode settings.json policy enforcement, and subagent declaration fixes.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.4" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(2)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added support for stacking multiple leading slash commands in a single prompt, so a chain like <code>/plan /grill-me \<prompt\></code> parses, activates, and renders every command in the order you typed them.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved scrolling in the <code>/diff</code> viewer so paging through a diff no longer jitters or pushes the status line off the screen when lines wrap or comments expand.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.4" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(5)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed custom agents that declare <code>subagent: false</code> still appearing in the available-subagents list and being invocable as subagents.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed headless (<code>-p</code> / <code>--print</code>) runs so they now honor persisted <code>settings.json</code> policies, including <code>permissions</code>, file access, sandbox mode, auto-execution, and artifact review.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>/btw</code> side-questions leaking into the conversation list as duplicate entries that carried the parent conversation's title.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the prompt to honor a custom Enter binding to <code>prompt.insert_newline</code>, so a remapped Enter inserts a newline instead of submitting.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed eligibility error messages so the CLI shows the real reason again instead of defaulting to a generic "unknown reason".</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.1.3"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Codesearch command, copy-on-select, and performance optimizations</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>New <code>/codesearch</code> command for regex workspace searches, copy-on-select, async skill discovery, and stability fixes.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.3" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(7)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added a <code>/codesearch</code> command (aliases <code>/cs</code> and <code>/search</code>) to interactively search code across your workspace, interpreting queries as regex by default with <code>-F</code>/<code>--literal</code> for exact matching and <code>f:</code>/<code>file:</code> globs to include or exclude paths.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added copy-on-select in no-flickering mode so dragging highlights text and releasing the mouse copies the ANSI-stripped selection to the clipboard, and hides the virtual scrollbar so it no longer interferes with copying multi-line output.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added an indicator at each context-compaction boundary so you can see where earlier compaction happened.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved interactive startup latency by loading skills asynchronously so the CLI no longer blocks on a synchronous, filesystem-heavy skill-discovery pass during bring-up.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved eligibility error handling by showing errors with a verification URL inline in the input loop instead of stacking them above the screen.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved customization loading latency for skills, rules, agents, and hooks by consolidating directory walks and caching filesystem lookups to cut redundant I/O during discovery.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Removed the padding spaces around inline code for tighter rendering.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.3" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(9)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed code-block corruption where <code>$..$</code> math expansion desynced from the Markdown parser and mangled fenced shell snippets such as <code>git fetch "$GIT_REMOTE"</code> by detecting fenced code blocks line-by-line.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed headless (<code>-p</code>) runs hanging or silently auto-approving tools that require a permission confirmation, so the CLI now soft-denies such tools and prints a stderr notice naming the allow-rule needed to permit them.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed outside-of-workspace file writes being incorrectly auto-approved in always-proceed mode.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed high CPU and unbounded render cost on large conversations in no-flickering mode by making index rebuilds idempotent so the conversation index converges instead of growing on every rebuild.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed lingering artifact comments after dismissing the artifact detail view and corrected no-flickering-mode row math so the status line renders correctly within the viewport.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed repeated sign-in prompts on Linux caused by the OS keyring: the CLI now bypasses the keyring when no D-Bus session bus is present (headless hosts and containers), skips it for an hour after a timeout, and uses longer keyring timeouts so a slow-but-successful credential read is no longer cut short and forced into a fresh login.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed MCP servers hanging the agent indefinitely when a server never responds by bounding connection, tool-listing, and per-tool-call attempts with timeouts.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed conversations breaking after certain tool calls, which previously corrupted the conversation history and blocked all further responses.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed customization rules being loaded twice when a rules directory is reachable through a symlink.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.1.2"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.1.2">v1.1.2</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">July 13, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Full diff preview, OAuth print mode improvements, and lag fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Added full-screen diffs for file creations, OAuth authorization code pasting in print mode, and 5000+ step latency optimizations.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.2" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(3)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added an <code>f</code> (full diff) shortcut to the create-file tool review screen so new-file confirmations can open a full-screen diff view, matching the existing file-edit experience.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added support for pasting the OAuth authorization code in print mode (-p) using the controlling terminal (/dev/tty on POSIX and CONIN$ on Windows) when stdin is consumed by a piped prompt, and made truly headless runs fail fast with an actionable message instead of blocking.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved responsiveness on large conversations (5000+ steps) in no flickering mode by switching hot-path line-count methods to pointer receivers, cutting the per-frame prefix-sum cost and eliminating sustained 99% CPU and keystroke lag.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.2" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(6)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed print mode silently downgrading to the default model when --model cannot be resolved by hard-failing with a non-zero exit and listing the available models, while interactive sessions keep the fallback-with-warning behavior.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed permission checks not respecting the allowlist for nested command substitutions, so a command like echo "$(dirname $(git rev-parse --show-toplevel))" now runs without prompting when echo and git are allowlisted, instead of double-counting the nested command and prompting for review.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the CLI keybindings file staying out of sync with /keybindings when new default bindings are introduced by persisting the injected defaults while preserving user overrides.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed garbled builtin tool headers such as CodeSearch(4 files found...) by mapping generic tool steps back to clean summaries like Read(/path) and CodeSearch(query).</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed mcp manager failing to resolve tool schema paths in standalone mode and leaking MCP server subprocesses after shutdown, which previously caused panics and cleanup failures for custom agents loading MCP tools.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a data race and copy-on-write violation when updating subagent states by cloning their stats before in-place mutation, preventing corrupted step counts and status for parallel subagents.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.1.1"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.1.1">v1.1.1</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">July 10, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Custom agents flag, in-file keyword search, and Jujutsu diff fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Added <code>--agent</code> flag, in-file keyword search for artifacts, recursive subagent updates, and Jujutsu workspace diff fixes.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.1" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(6)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added the <code>--agent</code> flag and <code>agent/agents</code> subcommand, allowing users to select a custom agent at launch and list available agents.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added in-file keyword search (<code>/</code>) and jump navigation (<code>n/N</code>) to the artifact detail viewer, allowing users to find and cycle through matches without disrupting terminal escape sequences or image grids.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added support for displaying nested subagents (grandchild and deeper) and handling tool confirmation requests across all subagent depths by recursively relaying nested subtrajectory updates to the root conversation.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Changed the default mode to respect write_file permissions allowlisted in <code>settings.json</code> under <code>permission.allow</code>, so pre-approved file writes no longer prompt for review.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Changed the default name for the newly initialized project to <code>CLI Project</code> for clearer workspace identification.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the session exit output by placing the resume command on its own line, making it easier to copy and paste in terminals and tools like tmux.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.1" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(6)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed print mode (<code>--print</code> / <code>-p</code>) silently exiting with a success code and empty output when a request failed server-side, now writing the error to stderr and returning a non-zero exit code.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>agy -p</code> hanging when run inside a shell script or subprocess by no longer reading stdin when a prompt is provided using a flag.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a data race on the <code>/btw</code> cancellation function.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed interactive <code>/diff</code> viewer defects in Jujutsu (jj) workspaces by correctly prioritizing <code>.jj</code> over <code>.git</code> in colocated repos, fixing commit hash regex boundaries, and correctly highlighting active <code>@</code> graph nodes.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed workspace-local hooks defined in <code>\<workspace\>/.agents/hooks.json</code> not loading after trusting a folder by reloading hooks whenever workspaces change.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed misaligned markdown tables containing file links in chat output.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.1.0"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.1.0">v1.1.0</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">July 8, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Request-review mode, execution mode cycling, and line-level diff previews</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Public release of mode cycling, default request-review mode with interactive line-level diff previews, and settings panel integration.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.0" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(8)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Agent execution mode cycling is now publicly available: <code>default</code> > <code>accept-edits</code> > <code>plan</code>).</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added <code>request-review</code> (default) mode as the default execution behavior: automatically pauses before file write operations to display an interactive, line-level diff preview (<code>f</code> shortcut) where users can review, accept, or reject individual code modifications before they are saved to disk.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added an <code>Agent Mode</code> option to the <code>/settings</code> panel so users can set and persist a default execution mode (<code>default</code>, <code>accept-edits</code>, <code>plan</code>) without manually editing <code>settings.json</code> or passing <code>--mode</code> on startup, with real-time synchronization so changes take effect immediately.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added a dedicated <code>"Create file"</code> confirmation preview for new file creations (<code>write_to_file</code> without overwrite): renders new content as an addition-only diff preview.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added <code>/plan</code> mode to replace legacy <code>/planning</code>, and removed <code>/fast</code> slash commands: consolidated and simplified execution mode switching around <code>shift+tab</code> mode cycling and the <code>/plan</code> mode prefix</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved file-edit diff preview rendering: computed accurate line-level diffs with context lines (<code>3</code> lines) and hunk separators, capped inline preview height with truncation hints, and added a comment confirmation prompt when exiting the diff view with unsent comments.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved UI footer keybinding hints across all panels (such as <code>/tasks</code>, <code>/agents</code>, <code>/permissions</code>, and <code>/mcp</code>) by replacing hardcoded hint strings with centralized layout helpers that dynamically respect customized global and local keybinding configurations (<code>keybindings.json</code>).</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the multiline conversation rename view in the <code>/resume</code> picker by dynamically adjusting input box width and padding, and right-aligning metadata columns (<code>workspace</code>, <code>steps</code>, <code>time</code>) on the top line to prevent horizontal scrolling or layout shifts during active renaming.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.1.0" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(6)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the tool confirmation dialog to accurately check normalized file URIs against active workspace directories, resolving an issue where valid in-workspace file creations and reads were incorrectly flagged with an <code>"Reason: outside workspace"</code> warning.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed workspace initialization failures when launching the CLI inside dot-prefixed directories (such as  .parent_dir/project ) by scoping path exclusion filters strictly to relative paths inside the workspace rather than rejecting dot-prefixed ancestor directories.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the <code>/agents</code> view header displaying <code>agent.json</code> instead of <code>agent.md</code> when creating new subagents.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the <code>/agents</code> panel's <code>"Create New Agents"</code> section displaying the wrong global configuration directory (<code>~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/</code> instead of <code>~/.gemini/config/</code>), ensuring users create global subagents in the location actively scanned during startup discovery.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed statusline shortcut hints (<code>? for shortcuts</code>) and redundant escape hints (<code>Esc to cancel</code>) erroneously appearing inside full-screen overlay panels (such as <code>/changelog</code>, <code>/artifact</code>, and <code>/settings</code>) by correctly tracking overlay panel states.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed inconsistent timestamp formatting in the <code>/tasks</code> panel and task detail views by converting agent-initiated background task timestamps (<code>time.Time</code>) from UTC to the local timezone.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.0.16"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.0.16">v1.0.16</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">July 2, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Streaming task logs, model generation retries, and markdown subagents</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Auto-scrolling background task logs, automatic client-side generation retries, and markdown-based dynamic subagents.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.0.16" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(2)</span></span><svg aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the <code>/tasks</code> detail panel to automatically scroll to the bottom as new background task logs stream in, and default to the latest output when opened while preserving scroll position if scrolled up manually.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved model generation resilience by adding automatic client-side retries when encountering transient errors.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.0.16" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(4)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed dynamically defined subagents by transitioning definitions from JSON to Markdown format, fixing an issue where dynamically created subagents failed to invoke.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a crash occurring when executing background tasks or terminal commands that produce empty outputs (such as <code>sleep</code>).</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed shutdown resource leaks by integrating the shared SQLite summary store for background synchronization and resolving goroutine and database connection leaks on CLI exit.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a permission manager hook error by safely handling empty decision strings returned by pre-tool hooks instead of failing with an "unknown pre-tool class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">July 1, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Subagent & task status bar, editor warnings, and Windows fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>New interactive status bar for subagents and background tasks, clipboard image pasting improvements, and Windows fixes.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.0.15" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(5)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Introduced a new interactive status indicator below the input box that displays active subagents and background tasks in real-time, making it easy to monitor and navigate parallel workflows at a glance.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added <code>ctrl+g</code> on the artifact view to open $EDITOR. Also added a warning confirmation prompt before opening the editor in the artifact detail view if there are unsent comments, and ensured these comments are preserved upon reload if the artifact content was not modified.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added <code>alt+v</code> as an alternative paste shortcut on Windows to resolve issues where ctrl+v is intercepted by the terminal emulator, enabling reliable image pasting.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the <code>/permissions</code> panel to dynamically reload configurations from disk and prevent accidental overwrites.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Increased the MCP connection timeout to 60 seconds to improve reliability for slow-starting custom MCP servers.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.0.15" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a bug on Windows where print mode and other non-TUI command outputs were silently discarded when run in non-TTY environments (such as pipes or subprocesses).</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed Windows editor fallback to use "edit" or "notepad" when the editor setting is "auto" and no editor is configured, instead of attempting to consistently.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.0.14"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">June 30, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Indefinite goals, subagent auto-approvals, and plugin class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.0.14" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary "always proceeds" mode for subagents to auto approve artifacts, preventing them from hanging when the parent is blocked.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.0.14" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an MCP configuration path mismatch in the CLI and permission manager to ensure reliable custom MCP server loading.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a TUI layout race astro-oabwef3t">June 27, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Optimistic slash commands, strict permission matching, and multiline undo</h3><div astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.0.13" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved command permission security by making "Always Approve" rule matching strict (non-regex) by default, while allowing users to explicitly opt-in to regex matching by prepending rules with <code>regex:</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved command permission usability by relaxing redirection checks, allowing safe commands with output redirection (for example, <code>tool > file</code>) to match without requiring strict full-command approval.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.0.13" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(5)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a bug where the CLI would temporarily render skill commands without their slash prefix during optimistic updates by deferring prefix stripping to the serialization boundary, ensuring the UI always displays exactly what the user typed.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a redundant CLI exit message by removing the "Resume in the same project" hint line, leaving only the standard resume command to simplify exit output.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Resolved bugs during UI transitions (such as opening subagent details or logging out) by introducing a unified synchronization mechanism that prevents key lockups and ensures overlay panels like the /help view are properly reset.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a bug in the CLI prompt editor where undo and redo history stacks could become desynchronized during rapid mutations by decoupling the history state into a unified, pointer-backed structure.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a bug where browser-related prompt sections were missing from the agent's prompt registry, ensuring browser-based tasks execute reliably.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.0.12"><div class="rn-version-col terminal hyperlinks, and shift+n diff cycling</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Added <code>--project</code> launch flags, dynamic OSC8 terminal hyperlinks, reverse diff cycling, and permission priorities.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.0.12" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(5)</span></span><svg aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added support for <code>--project</code> and <code>--new-project</code> launch flags to allow users to explicitly set or create projects, and updated the project resolution logic to default regardless of the active workspace.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added a confirmation prompt when pressing <code>Esc</code> in comment mode with unsaved modifications to prevent users from accidentally discarding their work in review views.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added dynamic OSC8 terminal hyperlink support to render clickable links in supporting terminals, with automatic fallback stripping for backward compatibility.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Introduced reverse diff cycling navigation mapped to <code>shift+n</code> in unified diff review mode to allow users to easily cycle backwards through diff blocks.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved permission config merging priorities by ensuring project-specific configurations (located in <code>~/.gemini/config/projects/</code>) take precedence over global settings in <code>~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/settings.json</code>.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.0.12" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(4)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a regression where <code>ctrl+o</code> scrollback clearing failed by restoring the use of cached fields rather than shared pointer comparisons for trajectory toggle detection.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a rendering bug where Makefile syntax (like <code>$(call ...)</code>) inside code blocks was mistakenly parsed and mangled by LaTeX math expansion, by introducing a state machine that restricts expansion to prose segments.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an enterprise network connectivity issue by restoring AES-NI compile-time optimizations, which prevents Deep Packet Inspection (DPI) firewalls from incorrectly flagging and resetting TLS connections.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed incorrect key strings by removing the unsupported backtab default binding and correcting invalid <code>pgdn</code> references to <code>pgdown</code> to align with Bubble Tea v2 canonical names.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.0.11"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 astro-oabwef3t">June 24, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Ctrl+C interrupt flow, persistent resume cache, and AltScreen tool review</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Added <code>ctrl+c</code> interrupt and exit handling, parallel <code>/resume</code> metadata caching, and expanded AltScreen tool confirmations.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.0.11" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added <code>ctrl+c</code> as an exit and interrupt key: The first press cancels active agent operations (like streaming responses), and a double-press triggers the exit flow. Also added a dynamic exit hint in the status line.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved <code>/resume</code> loading performance by implementing a persistent metadata cache and parallel loader, eliminating severe latency with large conversation histories and preventing background loading log spam.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added an expanded AltScreen view for tool confirmations (accessible using <code>ctrl+g</code>), allowing users to view and edit the full command and associated permissions in a dedicated full-screen view, replacing the inline edit (<code>e</code>) key.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added the <code>AGY_CLI_CMD_OUTPUT_PERCENTAGE</code> environment variable, allowing users to customize the maximum height of command outputs in the TUI as a percentage of the terminal height.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added strict key name validation to the keybindings system to reject invalid key names (like typos) and suggest canonical alternatives, preventing "dead keys" from being registered.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added a validation warning when <code>ctrl+c</code> is mapped to a non-default action, clarifying that the system always intercepts <code>ctrl+c</code> to interrupt active operations or exit, and providing instructions on how to resolve the warning.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved command output rendering by making the output height dynamic, improving the readability of commands like <code>/keybindings</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved text rendering with ANSI-aware word wrapping at word boundaries and prevented URLs containing hyphens from being incorrectly split across lines.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the <code>/resume</code> experience: added support for pasting clipboard text into the search filter and rename fields, upgraded the rename input to a multiline editor to prevent long titles from being hidden, and fixed a bug where the navigation cursor could disappear.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved keybinding validation warning messages to use user-facing names (for example, <code>cli.escape</code>) instead of internal representation names.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved startup behavior by only creating the <code>keybindings.json</code> configuration file when the user explicitly runs the <code>/keybindings</code> customization command, rather than automatically generating it on every startup.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved keybinding error presentation by replacing the persistent error footer with transient error alerts, freeing up valuable terminal space.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.0.11" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(4)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" prompt contains text, only triggering the exit flow when the prompt is empty.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the <code>ctrl+c</code> exit safety valve to ensure it always works as an interrupt or exit key, regardless of how it is mapped in the user's custom keybindings configuration.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed VCS commit tree rendering to reserve the <code>@</code> marker exclusively for the actual current commit in the VCS history rather than the synthetic "Working Copy" entry, helping users easily identify the working copy parent.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed authentication error handling to gracefully handle unsigned-in states by returning an empty configuration and suppressing noisy error fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Hardened command execution permission checks by enforcing strict exact-match verification for PowerShell scripts, complex shell redirections ( <code>></code> , <code>2>&1</code> ), and unparseable strings to prevent sandbox escapes.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Hardened sandbox execution by adding <code>.git</code> to the core list of dangerous paths, preventing unauthorized or destructive repository modifications.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a bug where allowlisted terminal commands with quoted arguments (for example, <code>python -c "print(1)"</code>) would silently fail to match at runtime due to flawed whitespace tokenization.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a bug in headless print mode resumption (<code>--conversation</code>/<code>-c</code> <code>-p ...</code>) where the CLI would dump the entire historical conversation transcript instead of only printing the newly generated response.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a CPU compatibility issue on ARM64 devices without AES hardware support.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.0.8"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.0.8">v1.0.8</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">June 12, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Slash command history, Models & Quota page, and TUI performance</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Replay slash command history with Up arrow, redesigned Models & Quota page, and statusline quota usage indicators.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.0.8" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(9)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added support for capturing slash command history, allowing users to use the up arrow to replay previously entered slash commands.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Redesigned the "Models & Quota" page (enabled by default, replacing the legacy usage page) to gracefully handle disabled quota buckets by displaying a dimmed "Disabled" status and omitting the progress bar.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added display of quota usage and execution mode in the status line.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved <code>/btw</code> to be more token efficient and support streaming responses for a smoother user experience and fixed premature truncation.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added a per-line guard against extremely long single-line pastes in the TUI prompt editor to prevent performance lag, replacing them with an expandable placeholder.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Redesigned the <code>/resume</code> conversation picker to align the workspace column and added adaptive column dropping (workspace, time, steps) to support narrow terminals.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Redesigned the <code>/tasks</code> list and detail views for better alignment and readability, placing start times on the left, right-aligning status, and capping the panel height.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved configuration saving by propagating write failures as transient error flashes on the statusline.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved settings inheritance by ensuring the CLI inherits the <code>use_ai_credits</code> setting from global user settings on startup.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.0.8" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(8)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a bug where the <code>/hooks</code> command wrote configurations to <code>~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/hooks.json</code> instead of the shared <code>~/.gemini/config/hooks.json</code>, ensuring hooks remain synchronized between the TUI and the backend.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a CPU compatibility issue (SIGILL on non-AES-NI CPUs), preventing immediate crashes on startup on older CPUs (like Intel Ivy Bridge) or VM environments that lack AES-NI support.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed dynamic reloading of custom skills and system slash commands, ensuring they are instantly discovered in autocomplete upon conversation switch or <code>/add-dir</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a TUI hang in the artifact view during long sessions by optimizing the rendering complexity of large step histories.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an autocomplete bug where a command that is an exact prefix of another (for example, <code>/conv</code> vs <code>/conv-switch</code>) would aggressively auto-complete and hide the suggestions menu.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a race condition where sending a message immediately after denying a permission request would fail due to incomplete backend cleanup.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed potential OOM risks when reading large clipboard files by verifying file size before reading.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed Windows and Wayland-only Linux distributions clipboard image and file reading.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.0.7"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.0.7">v1.0.7</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">June 9, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Configurable MCP timeouts, artifact gutter line mapping, and Wayland clipboard</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Configurable MCP server connection timeouts, accurate line-numbered artifact gutters, and native Wayland clipboard support.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.0.7" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(6)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added a configurable timeout for launching MCP servers, allowing users to specify a custom timeout or set it to <code>-1</code> to disable the timeout completely.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Revamped the artifact viewer gutter numbering and line mapping to accurately align terminal viewport lines with actual 1-based source file line numbers, including support for wrapped lines and collapsed Mermaid diagrams.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Increased the maximum tool calls limit to 512 for Gemini models, allowing agents to perform significantly more complex, multi-step tasks in a single turn.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added support for installing plugins directly from GitHub subpaths (with branch resolution).</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added native Wayland clipboard support (wl-paste) on Linux, falling back to <code>xclip</code> for X11 environments, and prioritized copied files (from file managers) over raw image data.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Preserved unknown fields in <code>settings.json</code> during read, write, and merge operations, preventing settings from being silently wiped out when switching between different CLI versions or builds.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.0.7" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(8)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a bug where the CLI could get stuck in a pending state (showing a transient spinner) after sending a message due to stale status updates.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a bug where the wrong workspace directory was displayed in the header and <code>/help</code> menu when multiple workspaces were active.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a desync bug in the agent state management where stale callbacks from previous runs could be used upon cache hits in new agent state.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed Windows-specific sandbox network proxy issues, resolving a hang during connection hijacking and correcting tunnel response protocols.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a bug where the archival status timestamp was not correctly saved when archiving conversations.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a potential stack overflow crash by introducing a non-recursive warning output mechanism for pre-conversation errors.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed variable resolution in plugins, ensuring gemini cli variables like <code>${extensionPath}</code> correctly resolves to the final installation directory.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed layout boundary overflow, scrolling visibility, and out-of-bounds scrolling bugs in the artifact detail view when inline comments are present.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.0.6"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div 1.0.6">v1.0.6</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">June 6, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Path auto-completion, optimistic prompt rendering, and fuzzy matching</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Path autocompletion for <code>/open</code> and <code>/add-dir</code>, optimistic chat prompt rendering, and fuzzy slash command matching.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.0.6" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(5)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added shell-style path auto-completion for <code>/open</code> and <code>/add-dir</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added optimistic rendering for user chat prompt submissions, injecting messages immediately into the viewport to eliminate perceived input lag.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added fuzzy and partial substring matching across slash commands. For example, <code>/el</code> suggests <code>/help</code> and <code>/model</code> where previously no completions were suggested.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Skipped subagent conversations from <code>/resume</code>, keeping the standalone conversation picker focused purely on direct user initiated conversations.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added a <code>stack_with_default</code> flag to the <code>statusLine</code> configuration to render both the default Antigravity status line and custom status line output vertically stacked.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.0.6" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(3)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a bug when suggestion was not triggered when <code>@</code> is typed after <code>(</code>. Enabled unconditional typeahead suggestions whenever <code>@</code> is typed without preceding whitespace, streamlining mention workflows.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a bug where entering a prompt immediately after pressing <code>Esc</code> (to interrupt an active agent stream) caused the newly typed input to be swallowed or rejected.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>--sandbox</code> flag propagation in headless print mode (<code>-p</code> / <code>--print</code>), ensuring sandbox isolation is correctly enforced during non-interactive execution.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.0.5"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added <code>--model</code> to set model when launching CLI. Also a new <code>models</code> subcommand to list available models.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added <code>/permissions</code> command which allows to add/edit/remove permissions rules for each of the three configs above directly inside the CLI.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Allowed opening the Artifact Review panel (shortcut <code>ctrl+r</code>) while answering pending questions or tool permission confirmations, preserving your current progress when toggling back.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved statusline layout by merging active tip and artifact status on a single line and truncating with ellipsis on narrow terminals to prevent collisions.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved customization support by allowing directories in the customization manager to be passed as workspace directories, enabling correct trajectory metadata population and <code>/add-dir</code> support.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added support for <code>url</code> in <code>mcp_config.json</code> to configure MCP servers directly using a URL.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved <code>/resume</code> performance: optimized lazy loading of conversation details, filtered out empty conversations, and added support for scanning SQLite database files (<code>.db</code> and <code>.db-wal</code>).</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved autocomplete: Tab completion for slash commands now resolves to the matched alias instead of the primary command name (for example, <code>/se</code> autocompletes to <code>/settings</code> instead of <code>/config</code>).</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Integrated the permissioning system with the rest of Antigravity. CLI permissions now merges project level permissions, permissions from user settings shared with Antigravity, and permissions from the CLI <code>settings.json</code>.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.0.5" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a bug that metadata was written in the current directory as opposed to <code>~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/cache</code> when running using
