astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.0.4">v1.0.4</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">June 1, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">SQLite conversation store, LaTeX math rendering, and centralized project cache</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>SQLite conversation database format, terminal LaTeX math rendering, and centralized project discovery cache.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.0.4" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(8)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added SQLite (.db) conversation support and will be CLI’s conversation format. Fixed a bug when importing SQLite conversation from Antigravity 2.0 to CLI.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added LaTeX math rendering, enabling the CLI to display beautiful mathematical formulas directly in the terminal viewport. Set <code>AGY_CLI_DISABLE_LATEX</code> environment variable to turn off LaTeX rendering globally if desired.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Decoupled project discovery from local <code>.antigravitycli</code> workspace directories. The CLI now stores workspace-to-project mappings in a centralized <code>~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/cache/projects.json</code> file, eliminating repository clutter and speeding up project discovery to a single-map lookup.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Collapses all newlines and consecutive whitespaces in conversation previews and titles before rendering list items, preventing visual UI layout breaks in the picker rows.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Styled the separator space between the line number column and diff content to match the text blocks, ensuring background highlights stretch seamlessly across the viewport width in tool outputs and <code>/diff</code> details.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Aligned the interactive <code>/changelog</code> and <code>agy changelog</code> cache paths to both use <code>antigravity-cli</code>, and made the caching process synchronous to resolve a race condition where immediate process exit terminated the cache write.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Moved VCS detection out of the synchronous CLI startup path to prevent slow initialization.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Parallelized the MCP server initialization sequence, preventing slow or hanging custom MCP servers from blocking independent, fast-starting servers (like local plugins) from loading on startup or configuration reloads.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.0.4" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(3)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Resolved sporadic and permanent UI hangs caused by a stateful callback streamer race condition during network drops or extremely fast agent steps.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Resolved inconsistent behavior where selecting skill-derived slash commands from autocompletion suggestions cleared the input without executing. Autocompleted skill commands are now correctly submitted to the backend.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Resolved an issue where exclusion rules and allowlists configured in rules.json were silently ignored, causing the discovery engine to load every .md rule file unconditionally at boot.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.0.3"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.0.3">v1.0.3</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">January 1, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">G1 AI credits support, /credits panel, and input prompt fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>G1 AI credits support when standard quotas run out, in-CLI <code>/credits</code> panel, and prompt input fixes.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.0.3" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(4)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added support for G1 credits in the Antigravity CLI. Users can now utilize G1 credits when their standard quota runs out. This includes a new <code>UseG1Credits</code> setting to enable automatic credit usage and a real-time display of remaining credits in the status bar.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added a new <code>/credits</code> panel that provides an in-CLI interface with a direct link to purchase additional G1 credits.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Redesign CLI logo on Apple Terminal.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved color scheme preview in settings and onboarding: added warnings and thought process examples to the preview, and corrected link styling to only underline the URL.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.0.3" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(6)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an infinite loop in the prompt input. Navigating left (<code>wordLeft</code>) when encountering spaces at the very beginning of the input no longer causes an infinite hang.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed custom MCP server disabling using the TUI. Resolved a directory path mismatch where pressing the <code>[Disable]</code> button wrote to the legacy <code>mcp_config.json</code> path instead of the migrated <code>config/mcp_config.json</code> path, ensuring custom MCP servers can now be successfully disabled and unloaded.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>$EDITOR</code> environment variable parsing: Resolved issues where arguments containing <code>=</code> (for example, <code>--alternate-editor=vi</code>) were incorrectly split, causing editor launch failures.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>/diff</code> detail view truncation: implemented dynamic line wrapping based on terminal viewport width and added automatic tab-to-space expansion to prevent layout overflow.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed project discovery robustness: updated the CLI to skip invalid or broken symlinks in <code>.antigravitycli/</code> rather than failing immediately, allowing discovery of valid projects.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>AskQuestion</code> state management: memorizes selected options, write-in values, and UI states when navigating back and forth (<code>KeyLeft</code>) between questions in multi-question dialogs.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.0.2"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.0.2">v1.0.2</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">January 1, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Account hiding flag, subagent timeouts, and TUI fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Added <code>AGY_CLI_HIDE_ACCOUNT_INFO</code> flag, subagent-specific interaction timeouts, and TUI fixes.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.0.2" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(2)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added <code>AGY_CLI_HIDE_ACCOUNT_INFO</code> environment variable to hide email and plan tier from the header.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved <code>/help</code> shortcuts tab by sorting shortcuts by keybinding key, adding missing keybindings (like <code>ctrl+r</code>, <code>ctrl+o</code>, <code>alt+j</code>, <code>ctrl+k</code>), and generalizing scrolling (PageUp/PageDown/GoToTop/GoToBottom) for both Commands and Shortcuts tabs.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.0.2" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(7)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed timeout overrides: restricted the default 60-second interaction timeout specifically to subagents, preventing the main agent from being unconditionally capped.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a nil-pointer panic in Sandbox Mode: resolved a typed nil interface comparison when fetching URL content.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed fallback skill discovery in Standalone mode: ensures custom/fallback skills are successfully loaded even if the standard configuration directory is missing, and added automatic path deduplication to prevent duplicates.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed command rendering in message history: prefixed slash commands with a caret (<code>></code>) in response block headers to clearly distinguish user-typed commands from agent outputs.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed plugin installation path mismatch: updated the <code>plugin</code> subcommand to install downloaded plugins directly to the shared configuration directory (<code>~/.gemini/config/</code>) rather than the private application data folder, making them instantly discoverable.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed Git short-hash support in diff selection: updated the commit hash recognition pattern in the  /diff  commit selection tree to match Git's standard 7-character short hashes (and up to 40-character full hashes).</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed statusline subcommand handling and recursive loops: added case-insensitive subcommand parsing (help, delete, reset, enable/on, disable/off) to the /statusline command, providing direct control to toggle or revert custom statuslines and blocking recursive shell hangs during help queries.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.0.1"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-cli" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 1.0.1">v1.0.1</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">January 1, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Sandbox auto-approvals, plugin discovery, and clipboard fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Added <code>proceed-in-sandbox</code> auto-approvals, automated plugin skill discovery, and clipboard image reading.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.0.1" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(8)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added <code>proceed-in-sandbox</code> tool permission mode. Auto-approves terminal commands that run inside the secure sandbox, requesting manual approval only when a command attempts to bypass the sandbox.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Integrates consumer/free-tier onboarding directly into the CLI.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added plugin discovery for skills and agents. Automatically scans installed plugin directories to make custom skills and specialized agents available for execution in the CLI.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Moves the <strong>terminal</strong> color scheme to the top of the selection list, making it the default choice during onboarding and in <code>/settings</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved <code>/usage</code> and <code>/quota</code> commands. Forces a real-time reload of model configuration and remaining quotas, allowing you to see updated real-time consumption statistics immediately.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved step rendering layout. Calculates available terminal width dynamically and uses middle-truncation (<code>/foo/.../bar</code>) for file path tools to prevent layout shifting on narrow screens.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved session deletion keybinding in <code>/resume</code>. Changed the shortcut from <code>ctrl+d</code> to <code>ctrl+delete</code> to resolve conflicts with the global exit keybinding (<code>ctrl+d</code> <code>ctrl+d</code>) and preserve Emacs-style forward-delete in search input fields.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Restored automatic table wrapping, preventing long cells inside markdown tables from being truncated.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="cli" data-version="1.0.1" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(5)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed OAuth token persistence and authentication hangs.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed Windows log redirection and resizing issues. Resolved a critical bug where logs were not redirected correctly on Windows, which previously caused the terminal to swallow window resize events and shut down slowly.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed pasted text line counting. Corrected line counting for user inputs to ensure extremely long inputs are correctly folded into a <code>[Pasted text #X +Y lines]</code> placeholder to keep the viewport clean.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed onboarding stability. Resolved a race condition where a concurrent terminal resize event during onboarding could revert the UI to a blank onboarding screen.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Resolved an issue where deleted files (represented by <code>+++ /dev/null</code>) had their deletion lines incorrectly merged into the previous file's diff.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-cli-1.0.0"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/docs/ide/extensions/vscode" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View Visual Studio Code extension docs">v1.7.0</a></h3><span class="rn-latest-tag astro-oabwef3t">Latest</span></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">October 5, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Native Windows desktop notifications, undo/redo and side-by-side actions for diff reviews, Cloud Workstations authentication, and inline diff stability fixes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Introduces native Windows desktop notifications when unfocused, undo/redo and side-by-side tab actions for diff reviews, interactive authentication in Cloud Workstations and Cloud Shell, and stability fixes for inline diff reviews and language server installation.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="vscode" data-version="1.7.0" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(6)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added native Windows toast notifications with direct conversation navigation when the VS Code window is unfocused, with automatic fallback to in-IDE notifications.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added undo and redo support (<code>Ctrl+Z</code> / <code>Cmd+Z</code> and <code>Ctrl+Y</code> / <code>Cmd+Shift+Z</code>) for individual and file-level <strong>Accept</strong> and <strong>Reject</strong> actions during inline diff reviews, keeping the changes overview bar synchronized.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added <strong>Accept All</strong> and <strong>Reject All</strong> action buttons to the side-by-side diff editor tab title bar and automatic diff tab opening when starting a review or selecting files from the changes overview with inline diffs disabled (<code>antigravity.enableInlineDiff: false</code>).</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Updated read-only (<code>Resolved</code>) diffs to display the saved <strong>Accept</strong> or <strong>Reject</strong> outcome in the tab title and mark rejected changes inline with a <strong>Rejected</strong> indicator.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added an interactive authentication prompt card, pre-flight credential validation, native login terminal workflow, and automatic background token refresh when running in Google Cloud Workstations or Google Cloud Shell.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved language server download and installation error messages to surface specific network failure causes (such as DNS lookup, connection reset, or TLS certificate-trust errors) and actionable certificate remediation guidance.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="vscode" data-version="1.7.0" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(9)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed release manifest and language server download failures on macOS and corporate networks by falling back to Node's bundled root certificates (and <code>NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS</code>) when VS Code's certificate list cannot verify the release host.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where VS Code Auto Save could overwrite or prematurely resolve active inline diff reviews by keeping deleted lines out of the saved buffer, prompting to disable Auto Save or switch to side-by-side diffs, and deferring diff renderer changes until active reviews finish.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed redundant in-IDE completion toast notifications appearing when the Antigravity chat panel is already open and displaying the active conversation.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed inline diff review handling so individual hunk <strong>Accept</strong> and <strong>Reject</strong> decisions are preserved when saving (<code>Ctrl+S</code> / <code>Cmd+S</code>), clicking <strong>Accept all</strong> or <strong>Reject all</strong>, closing tabs with or without saving, or starting the next chat turn.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed inline diff review lifecycle issues to preserve exact end-of-file bytes and newlines, keep pending reviews editable across window reloads and file renames, and prevent stale creation diffs from overwriting user-modified files.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed clicking <strong>Review</strong> or a file in the chat and review panes so past turns without an active review open as read-only diffs without reopening or auto-accepting other files' pending reviews, and normalized file URIs on Windows.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed UI lag in the inline diff changes toolbar and unresponsive <strong>Accept all</strong> actions when opening workspaces inside large or home-directory Git repositories.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed clicking chat file links with <code>#L</code> line or line-range fragments (such as <code>#L42</code> or <code>#L10-L20</code>) opening duplicate editor tabs instead of focusing the existing workspace file and selecting the target lines.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Updated in-IDE feedback reports to include the installed Antigravity extension version and omit local binary filesystem paths.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-vscode-1.6.0"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/docs/ide/extensions/vscode" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View Visual Studio Code extension docs">v1.6.0</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 29, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Terminal context integration (@terminal), native notifications, auto-accept inline diffs on chat send, and faster startup</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Introduces <code>@terminal</code> context integration to reference active terminals and command output in chat, adds in-editor and native Linux desktop notifications when the agent finishes or needs input, enables auto-accepting pending background inline diffs on chat send, and delivers faster extension startup, resilient binary downloads, and comprehensive inline diff lifecycle fixes.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="vscode" data-version="1.6.0" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(15)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added <code>@terminal</code> context integration in chat to capture and reference open VS Code terminals, recent shell commands, exit codes, and up to 8 KiB of output per terminal directly in your prompts.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added <strong>Antigravity: Add Terminal Selection or Output to Chat</strong> (<code>antigravity.insertTerminalSnippet</code>, <code>Cmd+L</code> on macOS) to the terminal right-click context menu, with automatic clipboard fallback.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added native VS Code information notifications with <strong>Open Chat</strong> and <strong>Dismiss</strong> actions when an agent task completes or needs input while the Antigravity chat panel is unfocused, automatically suppressing duplicate notifications when chat already has focus.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added native Linux desktop notifications (<code>notify-send</code>) with the VS Code desktop entry and Antigravity icon when the VS Code window is unfocused.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added the <code>antigravity.autoAcceptOnChat</code> setting (enabled by default) to automatically accept pending inline diffs in background files when sending a new chat message, while keeping the active file's inline diff open for review.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added an in-panel error recovery screen when the background server fails to start or crashes mid-session, with <strong>Try again</strong> to reconnect and automatic recovery polling.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added one-click <strong>Report issue</strong> diagnostic submission from the error screen that packages host and installation logs (<code>diagnostics.json.gz</code>) and submits feedback even when the backend CLI is offline.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved extension startup latency by caching binary version checks using file metadata instead of computing a full-file SHA-256 hash on every launch.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved initial backend binary downloads by replacing the fixed 120-second deadline with a 30-second rolling inactivity timer and automatic HTTP <code>Range</code> resume across retries.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved backend download progress reporting with monotonic 10% step updates capped cleanly at 100%.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved activation cleanup to automatically remove abandoned temporary staging files and unpack directories older than 24 hours.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved backend installation retries to re-fetch the latest release manifest before retrying so mid-download release updates do not fail checksum verification.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Increased the default backend startup timeout (<code>antigravity.serverStartupTimeoutMs</code>) to 30 seconds and improved startup diagnostics to distinguish early process exits and signal kills from timeouts.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved the loading screen progress bar with hardware-accelerated animations and synchronized transitions into the chat view.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Updated the Antigravity activity bar icon to a crisp monochrome vector asset and refreshed the extension marketplace icon so it renders without clipping.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="vscode" data-version="1.6.0" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(12)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed manual save (<code>Cmd+S</code> / <code>Ctrl+S</code>) and tab close (<strong>Don't Save</strong>) behavior in files with active inline diffs to prevent saving raw diff markers or leaving unintended edits on disk.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed inline diff application so unsaved manual edits made before or on top of agent changes are preserved.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed closing a tab with active inline diffs so selecting <strong>Save</strong> accepts the changes, <strong>Don't Save</strong> rejects them, and <strong>Cancel</strong> keeps the tab and diff review open.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed inline diff review zones disappearing across consecutive multi-turn agent edits.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the editor tab dirty indicator (<code>●</code>) remaining visible and triggering save-conflict dialogs after accepting or rejecting inline diffs.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed inline diff disposal and <code>files.autoSave: onFocusChange</code> handling when switching between editor tab groups.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed reviewing earlier conversation turns so the diff view accurately shows cumulative file changes.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed diff view behavior when opening resolved versus unresolved files from the <strong>Files Changed</strong> sidebar.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed repeated "Choose a repository" prompts appearing after every inline diff edit in multi-repository workspaces.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed reopening an older conversation unexpectedly re-applying its file edits to the workspace.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed artifact review webviews clearing their own <strong>Proceed</strong> button when mounting.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed backend server readiness checks failing behind localhost proxy settings, validated the working directory before spawning when a workspace folder is deleted, and added automatic rollback if binary promotion fails on Windows.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-vscode-1.5.0"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div with diagnostic logs, resilient auto-updates with offline fallback, and Windows file-lock recovery</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Introduces in-editor feedback and diagnostic log reporting, resilient backend auto-updates with fast-path offline startup, Windows binary upgrade file-lock recovery, and faster secondary panel loading.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="vscode" data-version="1.4.0" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(4)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added the <strong>Antigravity: Provide Feedback</strong> command (<code>antigravity.feedback</code>) and an <strong>Antigravity - Settings</strong> status bar item to open extension settings and submit feedback or issue reports directly from VS Code.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added automatic buffering of extension installation and server operational logs so diagnostic logs can be attached when submitting feedback.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved backend auto-updater reliability with exponential-backoff retries (<code>Retry-After</code> header support), offline fallback to an existing valid binary when network downloads fail, and a 3-second fast-path startup check so unresponsive update servers do not block extension startup.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Updated the minimum required Antigravity backend version to <code>1.1.11</code>.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="vscode" data-version="1.4.0" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed Windows <code>EPERM</code> and <code>EBUSY</code> file-locking errors during backend binary upgrades by staging running executables aside before replacement, retrying locked file operations with exponential backoff, and cleaning up stale <code>.old</code> binary backups.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed blank webview screens and reduced latency when opening secondary panels (Settings, Artifacts, and Terminal) by skipping redundant loading-screen rebuilds once the backend server connection is cached.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-vscode-1.3.0"><div class="rn-version-col class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Updated the initialization loading screen with the Antigravity emblem, title typography, and an animated progress bar.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Improved astro-oabwef3t"><p>Introduces a status bar shortcut for Settings, new configuration options for auto-opening edited files and pinning the server port, and fixes for browser-based VS Code environments, astro-oabwef3t">Added an <strong>Antigravity - Settings</strong> status bar item and the <strong>Antigravity: Open Antigravity Settings</strong> (<code>antigravity.openSettings</code>) command for quick access to extension settings.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added the <code>antigravity.autoOpenFiles</code> setting (defaulting to <code>false</code>) to control whether files are class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added automatic retry with exponential backoff and an interactive retry card when downloading and verifying the background server binary.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added automatic editor scrolling to focus the next remaining diff hunk after accepting or rejecting a hunk.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where starting a new conversation or switching conversations discarded pending agent edits instead of saving them to disk.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where bulk <strong>Accept All</strong> and <strong>Reject All</strong> diff actions were not persisted across window reloads, causing resolved diffs to reappear.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where agent edits unconditionally opened modified files in the editor, preventing <code>"Content of file is newer"</code> save conflicts.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where extension state queries with an empty key filter returned an empty object instead of all stored settings.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed console error noise when synchronizing file diff states before the webview is ready.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Addressed other minor user interface issues.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-vscode-1.0.0"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/docs/ide/extensions/vscode" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View Visual Studio Code extension docs">v1.0.0</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">August 18, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Initial 1.0.0 release of Antigravity for VS Code with agentic chat, in-editor inline diffs, interactive plan review, and Remote-SSH support</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Introduces the 1.0.0 release of Antigravity for VS Code, bringing an agentic AI coding assistant with sidebar chat, in-editor inline diff review, interactive implementation plan artifacts, and Remote-SSH workspace support.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="vscode" data-version="1.0.0" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(8)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added automatic installation, integrity verification, and channel-aware updates for the local Antigravity backend server.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added the Antigravity sidebar chat view with keyboard shortcuts to add editor selections to chat (<code>Cmd+L</code> / <code>Ctrl+L</code>), toggle chat focus (<code>Cmd+L</code> / <code>Ctrl+L</code>), start a new conversation (<code>Cmd+Shift+L</code> / <code>Ctrl+Shift+L</code>), accept (<code>Alt+Enter</code>) or reject (<code>Alt+Shift+Enter</code>) agent steps, and interrupt the agent (<code>Escape</code>).</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added in-editor inline diff decorations and CodeLens actions (<code>Accept</code> / <code>Reject</code>) for reviewing agent code changes, along with <strong>Accept All Changes</strong>, <strong>Reject All Changes</strong>, and <strong>Antigravity: Toggle Inline Diff</strong> (<code>antigravity.enableInlineDiff</code>) to switch between inline and side-by-side diff views.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added the <strong>Antigravity Artifact Viewer</strong> custom editor for previewing, commenting on, and approving agent implementation plans and Markdown artifacts, along with support for exporting conversations.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added automatic port-forwarding resolution and tunnel connection retry support for VS Code Remote-SSH and remote development workspaces.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Scoped conversations to the active workspace folder and preserved pending conversation state when opening or switching workspace folders.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Synchronized VS Code theme colors and editor font families with the Antigravity chat view, and added support for right-click context menus, native Cut/Copy/Paste shortcuts, and mouse back/forward navigation.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added the <strong>Antigravity: Reset Conversation State</strong> and <strong>Antigravity: Show Third Party Notices</strong> commands, dedicated <strong>Antigravity</strong> and <strong>Antigravity LS</strong> output channels, and streamlined extension settings.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="vscode" data-version="1.0.0" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(5)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed OAuth sign-in browser launch, post-login callback focus, and authentication state revalidation across Antigravity views.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed <code>Cmd+L</code> / <code>Ctrl+L</code> so it reliably focuses the chat input when opening the Antigravity sidebar and toggles the sidebar closed when the chat input is already focused.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed agent diff handling so switching conversations or editing files externally preserves user changes in a read-only diff view instead of overwriting the file, and prevented background agent edits from stealing editor focus.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed the <strong>Proceed</strong> button in planning mode, directory navigation in the VS Code Explorer, and <code>BigInt</code> message serialization in webview communication.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Addressed other minor user interface issues.</li></ul></details></div></div></article></div></div><div class="rn-ext-panel astro-oabwef3t" id="ext-panel-visual-studio" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="ext-tab-visual-studio" logic with exponential backoff when downloading and verifying the Antigravity CLI binary to handle transient network interruptions.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Synchronized background colors and theme palettes across the Chat, Settings, and Artifact views to match the active Visual Studio theme without an initial light/dark flash on load.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="visual-studio" data-version="1.0.260826.0" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(5)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where the <strong>Accept All</strong> / <strong>Reject All</strong> agent edits bar failed to appear or update after unloading and reloading the Antigravity tool window.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where docked auto-hide tool windows overlapped by or adjacent to the Antigravity tool window failed to collapse on click or suffered from rendering bleed.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed a potential crash when closing the Antigravity or Settings tool windows during shutdown.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an authentication issue during initial startup that could prevent theme and onboarding state from synchronizing with the local server, and resolved a sign-in redirect loop during onboarding project or license selection.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Addressed other minor user interface issues.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-visual-studio-1.0.260818.0"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/docs/ide/extensions/visual-studio" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View Visual Studio extension docs">v1.0.260818.0</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">August 18, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Initial release of Antigravity for Visual Studio with agentic chat, side-by-side diff previews, interactive planning artifacts, and chorded keyboard shortcuts</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Introduces the initial release of the Google Antigravity extension for Visual Studio, featuring an integrated AI agent chat tool window, native side-by-side diff previews with batch edit controls, interactive planning artifact tabs, automatic CLI management, and full Visual Studio theme and shortcut integration.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="visual-studio" data-version="1.0.260818.0" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(8)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added native in-memory agent edit previews using Visual Studio's side-by-side diff viewer, along with an <strong>Accept All</strong> / <strong>Reject All</strong> action bar and automatic document saving without external file-reload prompts.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added support for opening interactive planning mode artifacts (such as implementation plans and walkthroughs) directly in Visual Studio document tabs.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added a standalone, dockable Antigravity Settings tool window with single-instance focus management and automatic state restoration across Visual Studio restarts.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added <code>Ctrl+\</code> chorded keyboard shortcuts for toggling the Antigravity window with active editor selection context (<code>Ctrl+\, Ctrl+Alt+L</code>), starting a new conversation (<code>Ctrl+\, Ctrl+Shift+L</code>), approving or rejecting agent steps (<code>Ctrl+\, Alt+Enter</code> / <code>Ctrl+\, Shift+Alt+Enter</code>), and interrupting the agent (<code>Ctrl+\, Escape</code>).</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added automatic background management of the Antigravity CLI (<code>~/.gemini/bin</code>), including automatic downloads, checksum verification, multi-instance lock detection, ephemeral port allocation, and offline fallback.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added automatic Visual Studio Light and Dark theme detection and live theme synchronization across the loading screen, Chat, Settings, and Artifact views.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added a dedicated <strong>Google Antigravity Language Server</strong> Output window pane for live backend log streaming alongside the <strong>Google Antigravity</strong> extension Output pane.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Added bundled End User License Agreement (EULA), Marketplace README overview, and a <strong>Third-Party Notices</strong> command in the Visual Studio <strong>Help</strong> menu.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="visual-studio" data-version="1.0.260818.0" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(4)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed workspace synchronization when opening or switching Visual Studio solutions and <strong>Open Folder</strong> workspaces, and added support for switching workspaces directly from the chat view.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed standard editing shortcuts (<code>Ctrl+V</code>, <code>Ctrl+C</code>, <code>Ctrl+X</code>, <code>Ctrl+A</code>, <code>Ctrl+Z</code>, <code>Ctrl+Y</code>) and <strong>Edit</strong> menu commands when focus is inside the Antigravity tool window.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Fixed an issue where signing in or out in the Settings window could trigger a sign-in redirect loop or lose the active workspace context.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t">Addressed other minor user interface issues.</li></ul></details></div></div></article></div></div></div></div><div class="rn-panel astro-oabwef3t" id="panel-sdk" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="tab-sdk" data-panel-id="sdk" style="display: none;"><h2 class="rn-panel-heading astro-oabwef3t">Antigravity SDK</h2><div class="rn-timeline astro-oabwef3t"><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-sdk-0.1.21"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-sdk" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 0.1.21">v0.1.21</a></h3><span class="rn-latest-tag astro-oabwef3t">Latest</span></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">October 5, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Experimental API isolation with @beta namespaces, declarative subagent skills configuration, and bulk hook registration</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>This release introduces experimental API isolation using the <code>@beta</code> decorator and <code>.beta</code> namespaces, granular skills configuration for subagents, and bulk hook registration during agent initialization. It also expands typing support for tool context annotations, refines <code>UsageMetadata</code> arithmetic operations, and resolves an issue where tool calls with empty string IDs were unintentionally deduplicated.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.21" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(9)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Experimental API isolation with beta namespaces</strong>: Introduces the <code>@beta</code> decorator and <code>google.antigravity.beta</code> module, enabling access to preview and experimental features on class and instance namespaces without polluting the stable API surface.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Declarative subagent skills configuration</strong>: Adds <code>skills_config</code> support to <code>SubagentConfig</code>, allowing developers to explicitly configure subagent skill inheritance, override skills, or isolate subagents from parent skills.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Bulk hook registration</strong>: Enables registering sequences of hooks at once during <code>Agent</code> session initialization and within <code>HookRunner</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Beta API decorator and namespace</strong>: Added <code>@beta</code> and <code>BetaNamespace</code> support to expose preview classes, methods, and properties under dedicated <code>.beta</code> accessors and <code>google.antigravity.beta</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Subagent skills management</strong>: Added <code>SubagentSkillsConfig</code>, <code>SubagentInheritSkillsConfig</code>, <code>SubagentNoneSkillsConfig</code>, and <code>SubagentOverrideSkillsConfig</code> to <code>SubagentConfig</code> for declarative control over subagent skills.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Session hook collections</strong>: Updated <code>HookRunner</code> and <code>Agent</code> session initialization to accept collections of hooks in addition to individual registrations.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Qualified typing in tool annotations</strong>: Expanded <code>ToolRunner</code> string annotation inspection to resolve qualified typing wrappers (<code>typing.Optional</code>, <code>typing.Union</code>, and <code>typing.Annotated</code>) when injecting <code>ToolContext</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>UsageMetadata arithmetic</strong>: Harmonized <code>UsageMetadata.__sub__</code> typing and zero-value identity behavior to align with <code>UsageMetadata.__add__</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Tool inspection</strong>: Added <code>__repr__</code> to <code>ToolWithSchema</code> for clearer terminal output and debugging inspection.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.21" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(1)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Tool call deduplication</strong>: Fixed an issue in <code>Conversation.receive_chunks()</code> where tool calls with empty string IDs (<code>id=''</code>) were treated as duplicate IDs and dropped; they are now treated as absent IDs and yielded correctly.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-sdk-0.1.18"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-sdk" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 0.1.18">v0.1.18</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 21, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Local model support with LiteRT and LocalOpenAI, standardized evaluation presets, and custom subagent models</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>This release announces official Antigravity SDK local model support with first-class <code>LiteRTAgentConfig</code> and <code>LocalOpenAIAgentConfig</code> configurations, introduces a standardized evaluation preset (<code>AgentConfig.eval()</code>) for core coding evaluations, enables custom subagent model overrides, turns on the <code>schedule</code> tool by default across standard tool groups, and pairs background task management with command execution.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.18" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(13)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Antigravity SDK and local models support</strong>: Official local model support is now ready to use with first-class <code>LiteRTAgentConfig</code> and <code>LocalOpenAIAgentConfig</code> configurations. Developers can execute on-device models with automated lightweight presets or integrate with local OpenAI-compatible endpoints.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Standardized Evaluation Preset (<code>AgentConfig.eval()</code>)</strong>: Adds a standardized, benchmark-ready preset that configures autonomous permissions, benchmark retry behavior, daemon execution in commands, and strips image generation and subagents to focus on core coding evaluations.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Custom subagent models</strong>: Allows developers to assign specific model targets to subagents independently of the root agent configuration.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Built-in schedule tool</strong>: Enables the <code>schedule</code> tool by default across standard tool groups, permitting agents and subagents to set timers and cron-based background jobs alongside task management.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Automatic lightweight presets for LiteRT</strong>: Instantiating <code>LiteRTAgentConfig</code> now automatically applies optimized lightweight presets—including reduced prompt overhead and synchronous context compaction—without requiring an explicit call to <code>.lightweight()</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Task management pairing</strong>: Automatically enables the <code>manage_task</code> tool whenever <code>run_command</code> or <code>schedule</code> is active, allowing background task lifecycle management.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>LiteRT and local model examples</strong>: Added getting-started guides and end-to-end examples demonstrating on-device execution with <code>LiteRTAgentConfig</code> (Gemma 4 26B) and OpenAI-compatible local endpoints using <code>LocalOpenAIAgentConfig</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Sandbox availability warning</strong>: Added a session startup warning when <code>enable_sandbox=True</code> is requested on an environment or OS backend where sandbox isolation cannot be enforced.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Extended JSON Schema normalization</strong>: Added schema normalization support for OpenAPI and JSON Schema Draft 7 / 2020-12 keywords (such as <code>multipleOf</code>, <code>prefixItems</code>, and <code>dependentSchemas</code>) and prevented accidental mutation of uppercase sample values.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Optimized ToolRunner coercion</strong>: Improved type resolution for closure-scoped tools and forward-referenced <code>ToolContext</code> parameters, and introduced TypeAdapter caching to accelerate tool call execution.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Top-Level <code>ServiceTier</code> Export</strong>: Re-exported <code>ServiceTier</code> at the root package namespace for easier import parity.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Excluded <code>ASK_QUESTION</code> from Default Tools</strong>: Excluded <code>BuiltinTools.ASK_QUESTION</code> from <code>BuiltinTools.default()</code>. Default configurations run autonomously; agents in headless workflows will no longer attempt interactive user prompts. To re-enable interactive questions, explicitly pass <code>BuiltinTools.ASK_QUESTION</code> in <code>enabled_tools</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Single compaction threshold dial</strong>: Simplified <code>CompactionConfig</code> to a single <code>token_threshold</code> property, deprecating legacy context token limits and interval dials. To configure context compaction, specify <code>CompactionConfig(token_threshold=...)</code>.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.18" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(1)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Service tier ingestion</strong>: Fixed an unhandled <code>ValueError</code> when connecting using gateways reporting unlisted backend service tiers (such as Vertex AI <code>PROVISIONED_THROUGHPUT</code>) by safely ignoring unknown tier values while preserving token counts.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.18" data-item-title="Patches"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Patches<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(1)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Removed Deprecated <code>modified_arguments_json</code></strong>: Removed the legacy JSON string fallback in tool hook interception in favor of <code>modified_args</code>.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-sdk-0.1.17"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-sdk" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 0.1.17">v0.1.17</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">September 14, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Conversation compaction controls, forward-looking budget scopes, and tool output token truncation</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>This release introduces first-class conversation compaction controls using <code>CompactionConfig</code>, delta and forward-looking budget scopes for session resumption, tool output token truncation limits, and expanded arithmetic operations on <code>UsageMetadata</code>. It also delivers OS-level terminal sandboxing examples and automated tool wrapper reflection preservation.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.17" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(7)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Compaction configuration</strong>: Adds <code>CompactionConfig</code> on <code>AgentConfig</code> to govern sliding-window conversation history compaction through an explicit token ceiling dial (<code>token_threshold</code>), deprecating <code>CapabilitiesConfig.compaction_threshold</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Forward-looking budget scope</strong>: Adds <code>BudgetScope.FORWARD_LOOKING</code> to <code>BudgetConfig</code> to enforce model call and token budgets across newly resumed execution turns without counting previous historical usage.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Tool output token truncation</strong>: Exposes <code>tool_output_truncation_config</code> across agent configurations to cap token output volume initially for <code>run_command</code> executions in localharness.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>UsageMetadata arithmetic</strong>: Supports standard Python arithmetic protocols on <code>UsageMetadata</code>, enabling scalar multiplications, scaling, and accumulating token counts using built-in <code>sum()</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Tool wrapper metadata preservation</strong>: Preserves function signature, module name, annotations, and original callable references using <code>__wrapped__</code> when registering tools with <code>ToolWithSchema</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Interactive REPL policy flattening</strong>: Automatically flattens nested policy lists during interactive REPL upgrades so nested command authorization rules correctly upgrade to prompt the user.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Terminal command sandboxing guide</strong>: Added getting-started guide and references demonstrating OS-level command sandboxing using <code>RunCommandConfig(enable_sandbox=True)</code> paired with execution policies.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.17" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(3)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Tool call deserialization</strong>: Fixed dropped tool arguments during tool call handling when incoming payloads provide structured dictionary arguments rather than serialized JSON strings.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Local step trajectory tracking</strong>: Fixed missing provenance metadata by forwarding <code>trajectory_id</code> from incoming tool calls to local connection execution steps.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Dynamic content proto resolution</strong>: Fixed an <code>AttributeError</code> when importing <code>struct_converter</code> in external environments missing internal protobuf definitions by resolving descriptor types dynamically at runtime.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-sdk-0.1.16"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-sdk" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 0.1.16">v0.1.16</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">August 31, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Default Gemini 3.8 Flash model upgrade, lightweight agent configuration, and Vertex AI Express mode</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>The 0.1.16 release updates the default model for new agents to <code>gemini-3.8-flash</code> for enhanced reasoning capabilities, introduces a <code>.lightweight()</code> method on agent configs for small and local models, adds Vertex AI Express mode with API keys, and expands tool runner support for callable classes and dataclass functors.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.16" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(8)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Default model update to Gemini 3.8 Flash</strong>: The default model for new agents and configurations has been updated to <code>gemini-3.8-flash</code>, delivering higher reasoning quality and stronger task performance. Developers can override this default by specifying <code>model</code> in their configuration:</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Optimized lightweight agent configuration</strong>: New <code>.lightweight()</code> method on agent configurations (including <code>LocalAgentConfig</code> and <code>LiteRTAgentConfig</code>) applies preset optimizations for smaller, local-running models by configuring minimal tool sets, minimal prompting, and disabling background subagents:</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Vertex AI Express Mode (API key support)</strong>: Developers can now connect to Vertex AI using Express mode by providing an API key directly on <code>LocalAgentConfig(vertex=True, api_key="...")</code>, simplifying authentication without needing full GCP project/location ADC setup:</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Support for callable class and dataclass tools</strong>: The SDK's <code>ToolRunner</code> now inspects and executes tools defined as callable class instances (functors) and dataclass methods, enhancing flexibility for custom tool implementations:</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>OS sandbox opt-in for commands</strong>: Added an <code>enable_sandbox</code> field to <code>RunCommandConfig</code> to allow developers to execute terminal commands within an OS-level sandbox environment:</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Tool invocation argument flexibility</strong>: <code>ToolWithSchema</code> and public callable proxies now accept both positional (<code><em>args</code>) and keyword arguments (<code></em>*kwargs</code>), matching standard Python function calling conventions.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Support for <code>genai.Content</code> Media</strong>: Introduced a structconverter to support media blocks from <code>genai.Content</code> objects within the SDK, enabling richer multimodal interactions.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>MCP dependency compatibility widening</strong>: The SDK now supports both <code>mcp>=1.0</code> and <code>mcp<3.0</code> dependencies.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.16" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(3)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Stop hook integration</strong>: Agents now support the <code>StopHook</code> lifecycle hook, triggered when an agent's execution is externally stopped, enabling custom cleanup and resource logging actions:</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Interactive example agent behavior</strong>: Fixed interactive SDK examples (<code>interactive_cli.py</code>, <code>human_in_the_loop.py</code>) to consistently configure <code>AgentBehavior.INTERACTIVE</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Tool runner stdio MCP environment</strong>: Resolved an issue in exported SDK examples where <code>stdio</code> MCP servers failed by ensuring child processes use <code>sys.executable</code> within the active virtual environment.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.16" data-item-title="Patches"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Patches<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(1)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Step token usage deprecation</strong>: Deprecated <code>Step.usage_metadata</code> in favor of consolidated turn-level (<code>ChatResponse.usage_metadata</code>) and session-level (<code>agent.conversation.total_usage</code>) reporting.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-sdk-0.1.15"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-sdk" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 0.1.15">v0.1.15</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">August 25, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Subagent-scoped custom tools, context compaction lifecycle hooks, and universal JSON schema normalization</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>The 0.1.15 release introduces subagent-exclusive tool scoping to reduce context overhead, adds an <code>on_compaction</code> lifecycle hook for observing context checkpointing, expands platform compatibility with Alpine Linux musl wheels, resolves workspace path normalization and custom Vertex endpoint routing, and adds universal JSON Schema normalization for custom Python tools when targeting local OpenAI-compatible LLM endpoints.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.15" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(9)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Subagent-scoped custom tools</strong>: Custom tools can now be registered directly on subagents without requiring registration on the root agent, strictly isolating them to the subagent's context and reducing root context tokens.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Context compaction lifecycle hook</strong>: Improved support for the <code>on_compaction</code> lifecycle hook to accurately capture compaction events and summaries when long-running conversations trigger checkpointing.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Custom base URL and secure Vertex proxy support</strong>: <code>VertexEndpoint</code> now supports routing requests to custom <code>base_url</code> reverse proxies or enterprise gateways without leaking ambient Google Cloud Application Default Credentials (ADC) OAuth tokens or conflicting with project/location configurations.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Universal JSON Schema normalization for custom tools</strong>: Custom Python tools now produce standard OpenAPI / JSON Schema compliant parameter definitions with lowercase types and camelCase combiners, eliminating schema errors when connecting to local OpenAI-compatible engines such as Ollama, LM Studio, or vLLM.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong><code>from_bytes</code> Helper Export</strong>: Exported the <code>from_bytes</code> helper in top-level <code>google.antigravity</code> alongside <code>from_file</code> for creating binary and multimodal content payloads.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>PEP 656 musllinux wheel support</strong>: Added <code>musllinux_1_1</code> wheel platform tags for x86_64 and aarch64 architectures, enabling direct installation using pip on Alpine Linux containers.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Isolated harness environment configuration</strong>: Added per-connection environment dictionary resolution for <code>ANTIGRAVITY_HARNESS_PATH</code>, avoiding mutation of global <code>os.environ</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Tool call metadata preservation</strong>: Preserved <code>id</code>, <code>step_id</code>, and <code>server_name</code> metadata across all <code>ToolResult</code> executions, including batch calls, errors, and unknown tools.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Relative workspace path normalization</strong>: <code>LocalAgentConfig(workspaces=...)</code> now resolves relative directory paths and tilde (<code>~</code>) expansions against the current working directory (<code>os.getcwd()</code>).</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.15" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(6)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Local OpenAI tool schema validation</strong>: Fixed HTTP 400 "Invalid discriminator value" errors on local OpenAI endpoints by canonicalizing tool parameter schemas to standard JSON Schema.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Vertex custom gateway token leaks</strong>: Fixed host GCP OAuth bearer token leakage and environment variable collisions when using custom <code>base_url</code> endpoints with <code>VertexEndpoint</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Subagent custom tool routing</strong>: Fixed tool dispatch failures when subagents defined tools not registered on the root agent.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong><code>UsageMetadata</code> Service Tier Loss</strong>: Fixed <code>UsageMetadata.__sub__</code> dropping the <code>service_tier</code> field when calculating per-turn usage deltas.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>WebSocket deprecation warnings</strong>: Resolved <code>DeprecationWarning</code> exceptions when reading WebSocket close codes across varying websockets library versions.</li><li class="rn-item
