astro-oabwef3t"><strong>OpenTelemetry optional dependency guard</strong>: Fixed test collection failures on minimal environments lacking <code>opentelemetry.sdk</code>.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.15" data-item-title="Patches"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Patches<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(1)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Deprecated <code>TriggerDelivery</code> Removal</strong>: Removed the unused <code>TriggerDelivery</code> enum from <code>types.py</code>.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-sdk-0.1.14"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-sdk" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 0.1.14">v0.1.14</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">August 21, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Workspace path resolution against current working directory, compaction event deduplication, and Vertex auth overrides</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Resolves relative workspace paths against the current working directory, prevents duplicate compaction hook dispatches and compaction index inflation, and adds support for Vertex AI gateway authentication overrides.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.14" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(1)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Vertex endpoint authentication overrides</strong>: Enhanced <code>VertexEndpoint</code> to support custom authentication headers and token overrides when routing inference requests through API gateways or proxy servers.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.14" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(2)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Workspace path resolution</strong>: Fixed relative workspace paths in <code>LocalAgentConfig(workspaces=[...])</code> incorrectly resolving against <code>app_data_dir</code> instead of the process working directory.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Compaction hook deduplication</strong>: Fixed <code>on_compaction</code> hook dispatching multiple times per compaction event and resolved over-counting in <code>compaction_indices</code> by adding terminal state deduplication.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-sdk-0.1.13"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-sdk" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 0.1.13">v0.1.13</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">August 18, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Pre-tool argument modification, synchronous lifecycle hooks, RunCommandConfig timeouts, and step correlation IDs</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>The 0.1.13 release introduces pre-tool argument modification capabilities in lifecycle hooks, adds native support for synchronous hook functions, and establishes structured command execution configuration with configurable execution timeouts. It also improves tool execution observability with step correlation IDs and enhances connection resilience during client disconnects.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.13" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(5)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Pre-tool hook argument modification</strong>: Pre-tool lifecycle hooks can now sanitize, transform, or override tool input arguments before tool execution begins.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Synchronous hook function support</strong>: Lifecycle hook decorators now accept standard synchronous functions alongside asynchronous coroutines without raising runtime await errors.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Structured command execution configuration</strong>: Command execution settings are now consolidated under <code>RunCommandConfig</code>, introducing configurable timeouts that default to 10 minutes (600 seconds) alongside daemon execution controls.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Tool lifecycle step correlation</strong>: <code>ToolResult</code> and <code>ToolExecutionError</code> event payloads now include <code>step_id</code>, enabling end-to-end tracking and correlation of tool invocations across trajectory steps.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>VS Code debugging configuration</strong>: Updated <code>setup_vscode_debugging.sh</code> to target the canonical <code>getting_started/hello_world</code> starter example and explicitly configure Gemini Developer API defaults.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.13" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(2)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Synchronous hook decorator execution</strong>: Fixed a runtime <code>TypeError</code> when decorating synchronous functions with <code>@pre_turn</code>, <code>@post_tool_call</code>, and other lifecycle hooks by verifying awaitability before awaiting hook responses.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>LiteRT early client disconnects</strong>: Suppressed unhandled <code>ConnectionResetError</code>, <code>ConnectionAbortedError</code>, and <code>BrokenPipeError</code> exceptions when clients disconnect early from local LiteRT server connections.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-sdk-0.1.12"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-sdk" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 0.1.12">v0.1.12</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">August 13, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Fix proto deserialization regression for ActionGenerateImage output paths</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>The 0.1.12 release fixes a client-side proto deserialization regression in image generation result handling.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.12" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(1)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>ActionGenerateImage proto skew</strong>: Resolved client-side deserialization errors where the bundled binary emitted <code>ActionGenerateImage.output_path</code> while the shipped Python proto lacked the corresponding field, ensuring robust protobuf parsing across image generation steps.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-sdk-0.1.11"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-sdk" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 0.1.11">v0.1.11</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">August 11, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Default Gemini 3.7 Flash upgrade, session budget limits, Vertex AI Express Mode, and autonomous behavior modes</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>The 0.1.11 release updates the default model to <code>gemini-3.7-flash</code>, introduces session-level budget enforcement and turn termination stop reasons, Vertex AI Express Mode authentication, and an autonomous agent behavior setting. It also expands tool hook metadata, resolves string annotation coercion for postponed evaluation, and improves MCP server and subagent stability.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.11" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(6)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Default model upgrade to Gemini 3.7 Flash</strong>: Upgraded the default inference model to <code>gemini-3.7-flash</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Session budget enforcement and stop reasons</strong>: Added <code>BudgetConfig</code> to define session-level usage limits (total tokens, turns, and cost) and <code>StopReason</code> enum (<code>BUDGET_EXCEEDED</code>, <code>TURN_LIMIT</code>, <code>USER_CANCELLED</code>, etc.) to inspect turn termination causes.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Vertex AI Express Mode support</strong>: Added native support for Express Mode authentication using <code>VertexEndpoint(api_key=...)</code> and <code>LocalAgentConfig(vertex=true, api_key=...)</code>, simplifying headless and non-GCP deployments.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Autonomous agent behavior mode</strong>: Control the agent's behavior with <code>AgentBehavior</code>. By default the SDK now has an <code>AgentBehavior.AUTONOMOUS</code> mode (previously <code>AgentBehavior.INTERACTIVE</code>) to streamline scripting, background, and headless interaction modes. Override by setting <code>CapabilitiesConfig(agent_behavior=AgentBehavior.INTERACTIVE)</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Multi-interface hook registration</strong>: Enabled single-instance registration across multiple hook interfaces (<code>PreToolHook</code>, <code>PostToolHook</code>, <code>PreTurnHook</code>), allowing for cross-functional instrumentation without duplicate invocations.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>PreToolArgs metadata</strong>: Exposed <code>trajectory_id</code> and <code>step_index</code> on tool hook payloads for chat thread context tracing.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.11" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(4)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>ToolRunner string annotation coercion</strong>: When using <code>from __future__ import annotations</code>, tool argument coercion failed on stringified types; resolved by resolving type annotations using <code>typing.get_type_hints</code> before type adaptation.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>MCP server example port binding</strong>: Ephemeral port race conditions in test and example server startup were resolved by binding directly to port 0.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Subagent deadlock prevention</strong>: Handled subagent fatal errors in the localagent executor to prevent deadlocks when subagents terminate abnormally.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Empty input validation</strong>: Added input validation to prevent SDK unresponsiveness on empty or whitespace-only prompts.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-sdk-0.1.10"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-sdk" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 0.1.10">v0.1.10</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">August 4, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Prioritized inference tiers, real-time token usage streaming, and context-aware hook decorators</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>The 0.1.10 release introduces support for Gemini Prioritized Inference service tiers, real-time token usage event streaming, stateful context-aware hook decorators, and explicit tool call correlation IDs across hook callbacks. It also standardizes default system instruction merging behavior, fixes WebSocket compaction events, and expands local Gemma model documentation.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.10" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(10)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Gemini prioritized inference service tier</strong>: Configure agents to utilize Gemini Prioritized Inference service tiers for high-priority model execution with automated graceful fallback.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Tool call ID correlation in lifecycle hooks</strong>: Inspect <code>call_id</code> attributes on tool executions, errors, and hooks to correlate multi-step tool invocations across lifecycle callbacks.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Context-aware hook decorators</strong>: Decorate hook handlers (<code>@hooks.pre_turn</code>, <code>@hooks.post_tool_call</code>, etc.) that optionally accept <code>HookContext</code> as a parameter to maintain state and share data across lifecycle callbacks.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>ActionCompaction event emission and hook</strong>: Track context window compaction notifications over WebSockets and intercept them using <code>@hooks.on_compaction</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Standardized system instructions strategy</strong>: Plain string instructions default to appending to built-in instructions. To override and completely replace built-in instructions, pass <code>CustomSystemInstructions</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Live token usage reporting</strong>: Introduced real-time <code>UsageUpdate</code> event streaming so token usage accumulates live during agent execution rather than delaying updates until state transitions.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Interactive CLI spinner</strong>: Updated CLI interactive loop spinner to list all active tool names when running concurrent tool calls (for example, <code>Running tools 'tool_a', 'tool_b'</code>).</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Module re-exports</strong>: Re-exported <code>ReadUrlContentResult</code> and <code>SearchWebResult</code> in <code>connections.local</code> for uniform tool result access.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Local Gemma model documentation</strong>: Added guides and tutorials for running agents locally with Gemma models using LiteRT and OpenAI-compatible endpoints.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>LiteRT token output limit</strong>: Increased <code>max_output_tokens</code> default in LiteRT local server configuration from 8,192 to 16,384 tokens to prevent truncation during complex reasoning and generation tasks.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.10" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(2)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>ActionCompaction event emission</strong>: Fixed issue where compaction notifications were suppressed in external SDK releases, causing <code>@hooks.on_compaction</code> handlers and <code>conversation.compaction_indices</code> tracking to fail; compaction events now emit properly over WebSockets.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>MCP test server startup</strong>: Fixed a race condition where the HTTP port was exposed before uvicorn server startup completed, which previously caused intermittent <code>ConnectionRefusedError</code> failures during test initialization.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-sdk-0.1.9"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-sdk" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 0.1.9">v0.1.9</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">July 27, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Model-call retry/backoff configuration, LiteRT warm-up scaling, and tool execution error handling</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Release 0.1.9 of the Google Antigravity Python SDK adds model-call retry/backoff configuration, improves tool registration for custom functions, adds missing <code>BuiltinTools</code> exports, fixes user audio payload processing, and adds connection <code>DebugConfig</code> support along with <code>ToolExecutionError</code> handling.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.9" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(5)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Model-call retry and backoff configuration</strong>: Exposes configurable retry and backoff parameters in <code>LocalAgentConfig</code> for model calls.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>LiteRT warm-up timeout scaling</strong>: Dynamically scales LiteRT engine warm-up timeouts based on context size and synchronizes warm-up cleanup.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Improved tool registration</strong>: Enhances automatic tool registration and docstring parsing for custom Python functions.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>BuiltinTools typing exports</strong>: Exports <code>BuiltinTools</code> at top-level package and typing boundaries for subagent tool configuration.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Connection DebugConfig</strong>: Adds base <code>DebugConfig</code> options for enhanced connection debugging and logging.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.9" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(2)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Audio payload processing</strong>: Fixes processing for user audio payloads in interactive agent sessions.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>ToolExecutionError handling</strong>: Introduces <code>ToolExecutionError</code> to SDK error types for explicit tool failure handling.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-sdk-0.1.8"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-sdk" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 0.1.8">v0.1.8</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">July 21, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Gemini 3.6 Flash default model, custom subagent instructions, and Pydantic argument coercion</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Release 0.1.8 of the Google Antigravity Python SDK updates the default text model to Gemini 3.6 Flash, adds support for custom subagent instructions, and implements automatic Pydantic argument coercion for tool calls. It also brings defensive prompt sanitization, configurable tool retries, and comprehensive stability improvements for local agent execution.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.8" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(7)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Default model upgrade to Gemini 3.6 Flash</strong>: Upgrades the default generative text model in the Python SDK to <code>gemini-3.6-flash</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Pydantic TypeAdapter tool argument coercion</strong>: Automatically coerces stringified numbers, booleans, and nested models returned by LLMs into strict Python types declared in tool signatures.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Custom subagent instructions</strong>: Adds capability to specify custom system instructions and allowlisted tool configurations for spawned subagents in multi-agent workflows.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Prompt sanitization</strong>: Strips null bytes (<code>\x00</code>) and non-printable control characters (<code>DEL</code>, <code>BEL</code>, <code>C1</code>) from incoming user prompts at the wire boundary to prevent HTTP 400 errors and terminal corruption.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Configurable tool retries</strong>: Add <code>RetryConfig</code> definitions in local configuration to allow fine-tuned model and output retry policies.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Operating system telemetry</strong>: Populate client OS and version information in telemetry headers to improve diagnostic tracking.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Native usage accumulation</strong>: Enable native addition (<code>+</code> and <code>+=</code>) operations on <code>UsageMetadata</code> instances.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.8" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(7)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Large tool output handling</strong>: Dynamic output truncation and removal of WebSocket frame limits to resolve connection termination on large tool responses.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Subagent idle synchronization</strong>: Fix race conditions where multiple idle states could cause <code>receive_steps()</code> to hang indefinitely.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Custom tool policy enforcement</strong>: Ensure custom tools properly trigger pre-tool policy checks and cleanly report denials.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Media MIME type inference</strong>: Raise <code>ValueError</code> instead of Pydantic validation failures when media MIME types cannot be inferred from file extensions.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>LiteRT log noise</strong>: Suppress verbose C++ LiteRT engine diagnostic output by default.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Policy copy idempotency</strong>: Prevent duplicate workspace policy prepending during deep copies of <code>BaseLocalAgentConfig</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Type safety</strong>: Enforce typed <code>types.Step</code> arguments in <code>OnCompactionHook</code>.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-sdk-0.1.7"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-sdk" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 0.1.7">v0.1.7</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">July 14, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Multi-threaded state handling, subprocess isolation, extra high thinking, and local model MCP</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>Release 0.1.7 of the Google Antigravity Python SDK expands end-user control over agent execution environments, strengthens concurrency safety for stateful tools, and introduces deeper reasoning capabilities. Key highlights include atomic multi-threaded state handling for tools and hooks, customizable subprocess environment variable isolation, support for an "extra_high" thinking severity level, and full Model Context Protocol (MCP) and subagent support across local model backends. This release also resolves interactive console prompt clobbering and improves socket discovery under containerized setups.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.7" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(9)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Multi-threaded hook and tool state handling</strong>: Developers can now safely read and mutate shared context variables across multi-threaded tools (<code>asyncio.to_thread</code> or <code>ThreadPoolExecutor</code>) using atomic updates and thread locking:</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Custom subprocess environment variables</strong>: Developers can now pass custom environment variables directly to isolated agent instances using <code>LocalAgentConfig</code>, avoiding pollution of the global parent environment:</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Extra high thinking severity support</strong>: Developers can now configure an <code>"extra_high"</code> thinking severity level for complex reasoning tasks without needing to specify or override the model name:</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>MCP and subagent support for local models (LiteRT and OpenAI)</strong>: Developers using local Gemma (<code>LiteRTAgentConfig</code>) or local OpenAI-compatible endpoints (<code>LocalOpenAIAgentConfig</code>, such as Ollama or LM Studio) can now directly configure subagents and register Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers, enabling full local multi-agent and MCP tool workflows.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Environment hydration</strong>: Hydrates GCP/Vertex parameters (project, location, routing) dynamically from standard GOOGLE_CLOUD environment variables when not explicitly passed during LocalAgentConfig initialization.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Default image generation model optimization</strong>: Updated the default image generation model (<code>DEFAULT_IMAGE_GENERATION_MODEL</code>) to <code>"gemini-3.1-flash-lite-image"</code>. Previous default image models often took too long to run on average during standard agent execution loops; this lightweight model ensures dependable, high-speed image generation by default while remaining fully replaceable using explicit model configuration if higher fidelity is required.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Local WebSocket connections</strong>: Retries socket connections by resolving to both "localhost" and "127.0.0.1", ensuring dependable harness discovery under containerized setups.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Tool runner public asyncness</strong>: Preserves original asynchronous and synchronous execution interfaces of tools when accessed using ToolRunner.get_public_callable.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Config inheritance and gaps</strong>: Cleaned up redundant base overrides in LocalAgentConfig and corrected Pydantic validation failures arising from None initialization variables.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.7" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(5)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Interactive console spinner clobbering</strong>: Fixed a bug in <code>run_interactive_loop</code> where background spinner animation frames (<code>⠼ Reasoning...</code>) continuously clobbered user confirmation prompts (<code>async_input</code> and <code>ASK_USER</code> policy checks) every 80ms. The active spinner is now explicitly cleared (<code>\r\033[K</code>) and paused when an interactive input prompt opens, keeping confirmation lines readable and resuming the spinner smoothly after input is submitted.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Duplicate tool call events</strong>: Fixed client rendering issues by filtering out custom tool events from StepUpdate payloads to prevent duplicate event dispatches.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>SDK idle transitions</strong>: Corrected premature agent shutdown situations by properly checking and clearing idling flags once a TrajectoryStateUpdate signals transition to running.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>LiteRT connection engine</strong>: Rectified local engine initialization bugs, including a missing protobuf import, a token constructor argument mismatch, and OpenAITool inheritance.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Shutdown connection handshake</strong>: Added extra execution buffer to local connections during agent shutdown, ensuring safe persistent state storage actions.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-sdk-0.1.6"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-sdk" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 0.1.6">v0.1.6</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">July 9, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">LiteRT model configurations, dynamic GCP environment hydration, and performance updates</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>This release expands local execution capabilities by broadening support for multimodal and web-connected tool workflows. Developers can now run Gemma models locally using LiteRT or integrate with local OpenAI-compatible APIs, natively return multimodal media from custom tools, and leverage a more robust, streamlined lifecycle hooks framework that is fully aligned with Model Context Protocol (MCP) workflows.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.6" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(4)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Local model connectivity</strong>: Introduced <code>LiteRTAgentConfig</code> and <code>LiteRTConnectionStrategy</code> for LiteRT-LM (supporting local Gemma execution), <code>LocalOpenAIAgentConfig</code> and <code>LocalOpenAIConnectionStrategy</code> for OpenAI-compatible APIs (supporting Ollama and LM Studio), and a background loopback HTTP translation server.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Multimodal tool outputs</strong>: Enabled custom tools to return media assets (<code>Image</code>, <code>Document</code>, <code>Audio</code>, <code>Video</code>) directly using a single tool response without needing separate follow-up turns (<code>supplemental_media</code>).</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Built-in web fetch tool</strong>: Integrated the <code>read_url_content</code> tool end-to-end for fetching structured web content natively with the <code>ReadUrlContentResult</code> Pydantic model.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>MCP string prefix modernization</strong>: Decoupled tool calls and safety policy engines from legacy <code>"mcp_"</code> string synthesis, resolving name mismatch issues by utilizing explicit <code>server_name</code> attributes in tool evaluation.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.6" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(4)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Python 3.14 compatibility</strong>: Resolved namespace class conflicts and typing normalization issues in <code>agent.py</code> and <code>public_api_test.py</code> under Python 3.14 deferred annotations evaluation.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Vertex validation errors</strong>: Cleared a misleading reference to API keys in <code>VertexEndpoint</code> validation error messages, limiting fields to project and location.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>OTel trace warnings</strong>: Resolved detached <code>contextvars</code> warnings and set-status race conditions by removing <code>use_span</code> context managers from Turn/Session hooks and checking span recording readiness.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Exception wrapping mapping</strong>: Fixed <code>agent_middleware</code> integration check failures by making the example check for error message substrings instead of strict exception types.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-sdk-0.1.5"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-sdk" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 0.1.5">v0.1.5</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">June 25, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Streaming tool calls, custom subagent delegates, and thread safety enhancements</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>This release introduces native OpenTelemetry tracing support, declarative subagent configurations, improved type safety, and critical robustness and compatibility updates.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.5" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(5)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>OpenTelemetry tracing support</strong>: Integrates OpenTelemetry tracing into the SDK to translate session, turn, step, and tool lifecycle events into standard GenAI-compliant semantic spans for advanced monitoring and performance debugging, with custom task-safe active span propagation for tool execution.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Declarative subagent configurations</strong>: Added <code>SubagentConfig</code> and <code>SubagentCapabilities</code> in <code>types.py</code> to support constructing static subagents with declarative instructions and tools directly.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Type safety in <code>AgentConfig</code></strong>: Type-annotated policies, hooks, and triggers parameters on <code>AgentConfig</code> and its subclasses to improve type safety and overall developer experience.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Lifecycle hook routing</strong>: Shifted core orchestration of <code>OnSessionStartHook</code>, turn-level hooks (<code>PRE_TURN</code> and <code>POST_TURN</code>), and <code>OnSessionEndHook</code> to the connection layer, implementing the Python-side <code>HookRouter</code> for event routing.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Public API cleanup</strong>: Hid internal validation methods on media and error classes by prefixing them with an underscore (<code>_validate_mime_type</code> and <code>_from_pydantic</code> on validation errors).</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.5" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(2)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Historical step absorption</strong>: Ensured historical step absorption is properly drained during initialization to prevent persistence non-linearity issues in <code>Conversation</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Python 3.14 compatibility</strong>: Resolved potential name shadowing in the <code>Conversation</code> class by renaming the top-level connection module import.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-sdk-0.1.4"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-sdk" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 0.1.4">v0.1.4</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">June 18, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Async tool execution buffers, memory state persistence, and error handling improvements</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>This release introduces major architectural refactorings, public API standardizations, and key new capabilities centered on centralizing model configurations to natively support multi-model backends, exposing a new built-in Google Web Search capability, enabling environment variable passing for Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers, and simplifying the agent initialization flow by removing dynamic runtime registrations.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.4" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(10)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Built-in web search tool</strong>: Exposes the <code>SEARCH_WEB</code> tool directly within the SDK, enabling agents to leverage Google Search for grounded real-time information retrieval, complete with new developer examples (<code>web_tools.py</code>).</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>MCP server environment variables</strong>: Added support for configuring and passing custom environment variables to launched stdio servers using the new <code>env</code> field in <code>McpStdioServer</code>.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Base URL and HTTP headers support</strong>: Out-of-the-box support for setting custom base URLs and HTTP headers.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Image generation aspect ratio</strong>: Updated the SDK model config and wrapper to support specifying <code>aspect_ratio</code> within the image creation tool configuration.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Centralized multi-model configuration</strong>: Replaces legacy singular <code>gemini_config</code> options with a unified, repeated <code>models</code> collection on <code>AgentConfig</code> and <code>LocalAgentConfig</code> to support multi-model routing, fallback strategies, and automated selection helpers.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Agent session lifecycle and API standardization</strong>: Improves runtime safety by removing dynamic post-initialization hook and trigger registration in favor of session creation-time declarations.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Top-level package exports</strong>: Exposed core SDK constructs (including <code>Content</code>, <code>Image</code>, <code>Document</code>, <code>Audio</code>, <code>Video</code>, <code>from_file</code>, <code>BuiltinTools</code>, and <code>SystemInstructions</code>) directly under the <code>google.antigravity</code> root module for easier access.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Hook base class exports</strong>: Consolidated the base hooks implementation by exporting <code>DecideHook</code>, <code>InspectHook</code>, and <code>TransformHook</code> from the hooks package root.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Top-level policy package</strong>: Created a new top-level policy package to clean up hook and workspace path validation dependencies.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Relocated trigger types</strong>: Moved the <code>FileChange</code> model and <code>FileChangeKind</code> enum from <code>types.py</code> to the specialized triggers package.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-sdk-0.1.3"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-sdk" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 0.1.3">v0.1.3</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">June 11, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Initial subagent orchestration and standard Google AI model targets</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>This release introduces per-server MCP timeout configurations and improves local connection error handling.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.3" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(2)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Per-server MCP timeout</strong>: Added configuration support to set custom timeouts (in seconds) for individual MCP servers (<code>BaseMcpServerConfig.timeout_seconds</code>).</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Terminal error propagation</strong>: The local connection now propagates terminal trajectory errors from the <code>localharness</code> binary as structured <code>AntigravityExecutionError</code> exceptions in the Python SDK during step collection.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-sdk-0.1.2"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-sdk" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 0.1.2">v0.1.2</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">June 4, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Google Antigravity SDK release 0.1.2 updates</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>This release adds Windows platform support, introduces programmatic turn-level stream cancellation, simplifies safety policy configurations for the Model Context Protocol (MCP), and removes the deprecated MCP SSE transport.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.2" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(3)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Windows platform support</strong>: Native compatibility added for Windows x86_64 and ARM64 environments. Path and file URI resolution now correctly handles drive letters and directory separators under Windows.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Programmatic turn-level cancellation</strong>: Added programmatic stream cancellation using <code>ChatResponse.cancel()</code>. This programmatically aborts active generation turns directly from the client and raises <code>AntigravityCancelledError</code> (subclass of <code>asyncio.CancelledError</code>) to cleanly signal cancellation in the async flow (<code>examples/getting_started/cancellation.py</code>).</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Direct MCP safety policy configuration</strong>: Overloaded <code>policy.allow</code>, <code>policy.deny</code>, and <code>policy.ask_user</code> to accept server configurations (<code>BaseMcpServerConfig</code>) directly instead of typing namespaced string paths. Policy evaluation follows a 9-level precedence model (Specific > Prefix Wildcard > Global Wildcard) with longest-match prefix validation to protect against collisions.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.2" data-item-title="Patches"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Patches<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(1)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Deprecation of SSE transport</strong>: Removed the legacy <code>McpSseServer</code> configuration and connection handlers in favor of standard Stdio and Streamable HTTP connection strategies.</li></ul></details></div></div></article><article class="rn-row astro-oabwef3t" id="rel-sdk-0.1.1"><div class="rn-version-col astro-oabwef3t"><div class="rn-version-row astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-version-heading astro-oabwef3t"><a href="/download#antigravity-sdk" class="rn-version-tag astro-oabwef3t" title="View release 0.1.1">v0.1.1</a></h3></div><time class="rn-date-text astro-oabwef3t">May 29, 2026</time></div><div class="rn-card astro-oabwef3t"><h3 class="rn-headline astro-oabwef3t">Google Antigravity SDK release 0.1.1 updates</h3><div class="rn-summary astro-oabwef3t"><p>This release focuses on significant enhancements to the Model Context Protocol (MCP) integration, adds native Vertex AI authentication support, improves robustness with better error handling, and includes critical fixes for token usage tracking.</p></div><div class="rn-categories astro-oabwef3t"><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.1" data-item-title="Improvements"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Improvements<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(4)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>MCP tool filtering and simplified policies</strong>: Added support for <code>enabled_tools</code> (allowlist) and <code>disabled_tools</code> (denylist) in server configurations, and overloaded safety policy helpers (<code>policy.allow</code>, <code>policy.deny</code>, <code>policy.ask_user</code>) to accept the MCP server configuration object directly.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Vertex AI authentication</strong>: Integrated native support for Vertex AI authentication in the Python SDK.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>MCP tool prefixing and validation</strong>: The SDK now automatically namespaces and prefixes MCP tools (<code>mcp_{server_name}_{tool_name}</code>) to prevent name collisions when connecting multiple MCP servers. The <code>name</code> field is now mandatory in MCP server configurations and validated as a proper Python identifier.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Improved error handling</strong>: The SDK now raises explicit, descriptive exceptions for terminal errors rather than failing silently.</li></ul></details><details class="rn-accordion astro-oabwef3t" data-release-detail data-product="sdk" data-version="0.1.1" data-item-title="Fixes"><summary class="rn-accordion-summary astro-oabwef3t"><span class="rn-cat-title astro-oabwef3t">Fixes<span class="rn-count astro-oabwef3t">(2)</span></span><svg class="rn-chevron astro-oabwef3t" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="15" height="15" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" class="astro-oabwef3t"></polyline></svg></summary><ul class="rn-list astro-oabwef3t"><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Structured output token tracking</strong>: Fixed a bug where token <code>usage_metadata</code> was not correctly returned when structured output (<code>response.structured_output()</code>) was requested.</li><li class="rn-item astro-oabwef3t"><strong>Type checking warnings</strong>: Fixed <code>pytype</code> warnings (including <code>wrong-keyword-args</code> in <code>LocalAgentConfig</code>) across several modules.</li></ul></details></div></div></article></div></div><div class="rn-panel astro-oabwef3t" id="panel-ide"
